*Jiahai Huang,Anjie Qiao,Zhen Wang,Defu Lian,Yutong Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出图导向表示引导（GRG）框架，通过将预训练分子编码器的表示知识注入扩散Transformer，显著提升单步逆合成任务的精度（USPTO-50k上Top-1达58.6%）和多样性（15.5），同时加速35%训练收敛，并引入基于表示相似度的重排序机制进一步优化结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于随机过程的分子图生成器仅通过产品-反应物对进行训练，以间接隐式方式获取化学相关表征，缺乏显式的表示引导。受计算机视觉领域将预训练编码器语义蒸馏到DiTs的启发，探索在逆合成任务中引入表示引导能否带来类似增益，以及图特定设计选择如何实现这一目标。

Method: 系统性地在统一设计空间内进行实证研究，涵盖教师分子表示、端点和粒度选择、去噪器中的注入深度、对应策略和引导方案。据此提出GRG框架，将预训练分子编码器的表示知识注入扩散Transformer生成器，并设计基于表示相似度的重排序机制。

Result: 在USPTO-50k数据集上，Top-1/3/5/10准确率分别达到58.6/77.2/83.4/87.1，多样性提升至15.5，均大幅超越基线。在分布外设置下所有top-k指标一致提升。训练epoch减少35%，墙钟时间减少30%。

Conclusion: 表示引导能够促进生成器获取内在化学语义，显著提升逆合成性能、收敛速度和生成多样性，且无需训练额外验证器的重排序机制能进一步改善排名列表顶部结果。

Abstract: Stochastic process-based molecular graph generators have become the state of the art for template-free single-step retrosynthesis. However, these models are typically trained only on product-reactant pairs, thereby acquiring chemistry-relevant representations in an indirect and implicit manner. Meanwhile, recent advances in computer vision demonstrate that offering representation guidance to a generator can effectively distill semantics from pretrained encoders into DiTs, substantially improving both convergence and generation quality. Whether similar gains extend to the retrosynthesis task, and what graph-specific design choices can make them work, remains an open question. To address these questions, we conduct a systematic empirical study over a unified design space spanning teacher molecular representations, endpoint and granularity choices, injection depths in the denoiser, correspondence strategies and guidance scheme. Guided by these considerations, we develop Graph-oriented Representation Guidance (GRG), which achieves 58.6 / 77.2 / 83.4 / 87.1 top-1 / 3 / 5 / 10 accuracy on USPTO-50k, while increasing diversity to 15.5, both substantially outperforming the adopted base generator. Notably, GRG consistently improves all top-k metrics in out-of-distribution settings, suggesting that representation guidance facilitates the acquisition of intrinsic chemical semantics. Meanwhile, the introduced representation guidance reduces the number of epochs by 35% and the wall-clock time by 30% to reach comparable performance. In addition, we introduce a simple yet effective representation-similarity-based reranking mechanism, which further improves the top of the ranked list without training an additional verifier.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 提出一种多任务对抗模型，将公平性和隐私性作为整体目标嵌入模型训练；通过学习隐藏敏感属性同时保留任务相关信息的潜在表示；通过优化的代价函数动态平衡公平性、隐私性和准确性三者之间的关系。

Conclusion: 本文证明了将公平性和隐私性作为核心目标而非事后考虑的有效性，提出的多任务对抗模型实现了三者之间的动态平衡，为构建更符合伦理标准和隐私法规的集中式数据驱动应用提供了一种可行的解决方案。

Abstract: The integration of fairness and privacy in centralized data-driven applications is critical, especially as these systems increasingly influence sectors with significant societal impact. Current methods rarely address privacy, fairness, and accuracy together, which can potentially compromise ethical standards and privacy regulations. However, balancing these three objectives is quite challenging since each of objective often imposes conflicting requirements on the design and training of models, making it difficult to optimize one without compromising the others. This paper introduces a novel multitask adversarial model that treats fairness and privacy as integral objectives rather than afterthoughts, and learns a latent representation that hides sensitive attributes while preserving essential task-related information. Our approach dynamically balances fairness with accuracy and privacy through an optimized cost function with minimal performance loss even under strict conditions. Extensive testing on diverse datasets shows the ability of our model to achieve high standards of fairness and privacy without significant sacrifice to accuracy. Benchmarking against state-of-the-art privacy and fairness standards shows that our method enhances the robustness of privacy, fairness, and accuracy optimization, proving its adaptability across various datasets.

</details>


### [41] [The Normalized Maximum Likelihood for Regular Non-Smooth Models: Measure-Theoretic Foundations and Geometric Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24477)
*Trenton Lau,Gary P. T. Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对非光滑估计器（如Lasso、稀疏SVM）的正则化最大似然（NML）编码长度的严格计算框架。通过结合几何测度论与保守雅可比，证明了非光滑模型随机复杂度的良定义性，并设计了PDL-PPMH采样器（一种几何MCMC算法）来精确计算该量。实验表明，该方法在无需数据分割的情况下，能达到与交叉验证统计上无差异的预测最优值，为现代机器学习中非光滑模型的NML理论分析铺平了道路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于余面积公式的NML计算方法仅适用于光滑模型，而现代机器学习中广泛使用的非光滑估计器（如Lasso、稀疏SVM）导致该框架失效。因此需要建立针对非光滑模型的NML严格计算框架。

Method: 1) 利用几何测度论和保守雅可比，将余面积公式推广到正则路径可微Lipschitz（PDL）估计器；2) 提出Propose-and-Project Metropolis-Hastings（PDL-PPMH）几何MCMC采样器，包含随机切空间提议和非光滑投影求解器；3) 通过理论证明各组件收敛性。

Result: 1) 在高维Lasso后验（P=2000）上验证了方法的鲁棒性；2) 量化了精确性与混合时间之间的计算权衡；3) 实证表明精确NML准则在无需数据分割的情况下，能达到与交叉验证统计上无差异的预测最优值。

Conclusion: 本文为非光滑正则化模型的NML编码长度提供了严格的理论基础与可行的计算方法，为替代传统交叉验证提供了数据高效的准则，并为该领域的进一步理论分析奠定了基础。

Abstract: The Normalized Maximum Likelihood (NML) codelength, or stochastic complexity, represents a principled criterion for universal coding. While recent coarea-based formulations provided a calculation method for smooth models, this framework collapses for the non-smooth estimators ubiquitous in modern machine learning (e.g., Lasso, Sparse SVMs). In this work, we provide a rigorous framework for computing the NML for regular path-differentiable Lipschitz (PDL) estimators. By applying classical geometric measure theory and bridging the coarea formula with conservative Jacobians, we prove that the stochastic complexity for non-smooth models is well-posed and theoretically consistent with the outputs of modern Automatic Differentiation. To compute this quantity exactly, we introduce the Propose-and-Project Metropolis-Hastings (PDL-PPMH) sampler, a geometric MCMC algorithm capable of traversing the non-differentiable level sets of the maximum likelihood estimator. We theoretically justify its components, including a stochastic tangent space proposal and a provably convergent non-smooth projection solver. We demonstrate the method's robustness by sampling from a high-dimensional Lasso posterior ($P=2000$), while simultaneously quantifying the computational scaling that governs the trade-off between exactness and mixing time. Crucially, we empirically demonstrate that our exact NML criterion provides a highly data-efficient alternative to cross-validation, achieving statistically indistinguishable predictive optima without requiring data splitting. Altogether, our work paves the way for the theoretical analysis of the NML codelength for regular non-smooth models.

</details>


### [42] [Zeroth-Order Nonconvex Nonsmooth Optimization with Heavy-Tailed Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24513)
*Zhuanghua Liu,Luo Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对具有Lipschitz连续目标函数和重尾噪声的非凸非光滑随机优化问题，提出了一种基于截断两点梯度估计的随机零阶算法，实现了与一阶算法相当的收敛保证，并在数值实验中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多机器学习应用中存在重尾噪声，而传统随机零阶优化算法在处理此类噪声时性能退化严重。现有方法在非凸非光滑场景下缺乏理论保证或复杂度不优，亟需设计高效算法。

Method: 采用在线到非凸转换框架，通过对两点梯度估计器进行截断（clipping）来控制重尾噪声的影响，构建随机零阶优化算法。

Result: 算法在$(\delta, \varepsilon)$-Goldstein平稳点下的零阶Oracle复杂度为${\mathcal O}(d^{\frac{p}{2(p-1)}}\delta^{-1}\varepsilon^{-\frac{2p-1}{p-1}})$，其中维度依赖性与随机凸非光滑问题的已知最优结果匹配，精度参数的依赖性与最优随机一阶算法一致。

Conclusion: 所提算法在重尾噪声下达到了最优的维度依赖性和精度参数依赖性，填补了非凸非光滑随机零阶优化领域的理论空白，数值实验验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: This paper considers the nonconvex nonsmooth problem in which the objective function is Lipschitz continuous. We focus on the stochastic setting where the algorithm can access stochastic function value evaluations with heavy-tailed noise, which is prevalent in many popular machine learning applications. We propose a stochastic zeroth-order algorithm that refines the framework of online-to-nonconvex conversion by clipping the two-point gradient estimator. The theoretical analysis shows that our algorithm can find a $(δ, ε)$-Goldstein stationary point with zeroth-order oracle complexity of ${\mathcal O}(d^{\frac{p}{2(p-1)}}δ^{-1}ε^{-\frac{2p-1}{p-1}})$, where $d$ is the problem dimension and $p\in(1,2]$ is the order of bounded moments. Note that our dependence on dimension $d$ matches the best-known results of stochastic zeroth-order optimization for finding the sub-optimal solution of a stochastic convex nonsmooth problem. In addition, our dependence on accuracy parameters $δ$ and $ε$ is consistent with that of the best-known stochastic first-order algorithms for stochastic nonconvex nonsmooth problems. Finally, we conduct numerical experiments to demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气候变化和人类活动导致内陆水资源日益稀缺、脆弱且难以可持续管理，迫切需要可靠且自动化的地表水体检测、监测与评估方法。

Method: 1) 对比三种不同的机器学习架构进行水体识别；2) 通过定量指标和真实案例评估性能；3) 采用NDWI阈值法进行直接对比；4) 提出适用于水质指数的有意义配色方案。

Conclusion: 数据驱动的机器学习方法在水体检测中优于传统指数法；提出的配色方案可改善水质指数解译，支持更好的环境决策。

Abstract: With climate change and increasing human pressure on natural landscapes, inland water resources are becoming progressively scarcer, more vulnerable, and more difficult to manage sustainably. Reliable and automated methods for detecting, monitoring, and assessing surface water bodies are therefore of growing scientific and practical importance. In this paper, we investigate and compare three distinct machine learning architectures for water body identification and monitoring. Their performance is evaluated through quantitative metrics and real-world examples. Furthermore, a direct comparison with classical NDWI thresholding is conducted on a representative test image to highlight differences between data-driven and index-based approaches. This analysis allows us to identify the best-performing model in terms of accuracy, robustness, and practical applicability. Beyond detection, a major challenge for meaningful water quality assessment lies in the consistent and interpretable visualization of spectral water indices. Standard color mapping techniques are often inadequate or potentially misleading for environmental applications. To address this gap, we propose a suite of meaningful color schemes adapted for water quality indices, facilitating clearer interpretation, comparison, and decision-making for human users.

</details>


### [44] [ECHO: Terminal Agents Learn World Models for Free](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24517)
*Vaishnavi Shrivastava,Piero Kauffmann,Ahmed Awadallah,Dimitris Papailiopoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ECHO是一种混合目标函数，它将策略梯度损失与辅助的环境观测预测损失结合，利用终端反馈作为密集监督信号，在无需额外 rollout 的情况下显著提升了 CLI 智能体的性能，在 TerminalBench-2.0 上使 GRPO 的 pass@1 翻倍，并展现出无需专家示范的自我改进能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 agent RL（如 GRPO）在训练时丢弃了终端返回的环境观测信息，仅使用稀疏的结局级奖励更新动作 token，失败 rollout 中蕴含的丰富环境反馈信号未被充分利用。

Method: 提出 ECHO（环境交叉熵混合目标），在标准策略梯度损失基础上增加一个辅助损失，训练策略模型预测自身动作导致的环境观测 token（stdout、错误、文件、日志等）。该辅助损失复用 GRPO 的同一前向传播，无需额外 rollout。

Result: 在 TerminalBench-2.0 上，ECHO 使 GRPO pass@1 翻倍：Qwen3-8B 从 2.70% 提升至 5.17%，Qwen3-14B 从 5.17% 提升至 10.79%。ECHO 显著降低了环境 token 的交叉熵，而单独 GRPO 几乎无变化。从基础模型出发，ECHO 无需专家示范即可匹配 expert-SFT-then-GRPO 的性能。

Conclusion: 环境观测不仅是未来动作的上下文，更是每个 rollout 中已经存在的密集、在策略监督信号。在某些设置下，仅靠环境预测损失即可实现无需验证器的自我改进，使策略在未见过的 OOD 任务上也能通过环境交互学习提升。

Abstract: CLI agents are the closest thing language models have to an embodied setting: the model emits commands, the terminal executes them, and the returned stream -- stdout, errors, files, logs, and traces -- records the consequences. We argue that this stream is a supervision signal, but standard agent RL discards it: GRPO-style training updates action tokens with sparse outcome-level rewards while ignoring environment responses already in the rollout. Failed rollouts provide little policy-gradient signal despite containing rich evidence about how the environment responds. We introduce ECHO (Environment Cross-entropy Hybrid Objective), a hybrid objective that combines the standard policy-gradient loss on action tokens with an auxiliary loss that trains the policy to predict environment observation tokens resulting from its own actions. ECHO reuses the same forward pass as GRPO, requires no additional rollouts, and turns terminal feedback into dense supervision for all rollouts. ECHO doubles GRPO pass@1 on TerminalBench-2.0: Qwen3-8B improves from 2.70% to 5.17%, and Qwen3-14B from 5.17% to 10.79%. ECHO also produces policies that better predict terminal dynamics, even on trajectories they did not generate: across held-out rollouts, it sharply reduces environment-token cross-entropy while GRPO alone barely changes it. From base Qwen3-8B, ECHO matches expert-SFT-then-GRPO performance on held-out terminal tasks without expert demonstrations, and recovers roughly half of the expert-SFT initialization benefit on TerminalBench-2.0. In some settings, the environment prediction loss alone enables verifier-free self-improvement, allowing policies to improve on unseen OOD tasks by learning only from environment interactions. Together, these results suggest that environment observations are not merely context for future actions, but a dense, on-policy supervision signal already present in every rollout.

</details>


### [45] [What Are We Actually Decoding? Source Attribution for Non-Invasive Brain-to-Language Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24524)
*Xinyu Zhang,Sichao Liu,Runhao Lu,Alexandra Woolgar,Lihui Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个MEG到音频检索的审计框架，将神经语言解码的性能分解为三个来源：结构捷径、窗口级刺激锁定的证据和跨窗口上下文聚合。通过固定时长窗口和刺激身份分割控制结构泄漏，并引入组上下文偏差（GCB）作为推理时的可审计干预，使R@1性能提升可归因于特定来源。结果表明脑到语言的性能应进行源归因而非简单报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经语言解码中结果可能被非刺激诱发的神经证据（解码器先验、基于嵌入的度量和非神经结构干扰如信号时长）膨胀，因此需要将报告的性能增益追溯到特定来源，解决归因问题。

Method: 将刺激锁定的MEG到音频检索重构为审计框架，分离三类来源；采用固定时长窗口和刺激身份分割来控制结构泄漏；提出组上下文偏差（GCB）作为推理时的可审计干预。

Result: 信号盲高斯噪声在变长解码下达到66.3% R@1，但在固定窗口和刺激身份分割控制下降至接近随机；固定窗口检索恢复可测量的MEG-音频可区分性；95.7%的Top-1错误选择了错误句子；GCB使R@1在Gwilliams上从44%提升至52%，在MOUS上从22%提升至29%。

Conclusion: 脑到语言的性能应进行源归因而不仅仅是报告，GCB可作为受控的源归因干预工具。

Abstract: In non-invasive neural language decoding, results can be inflated by sources that are not stimulus-evoked neural evidence: decoder priors, embedding-based metrics, and non-neural structural nuisances such as signal duration. The methodological challenge is therefore attribution: a reported gain is more informative when it can be traced to a specific source. We recast stimulus-locked MEG-to-audio retrieval as an auditing framework that separates apparent performance into three sources - structural shortcuts, window-level stimulus-locked evidence, and cross-window contextual aggregation - and provides a diagnostic for each. Signal-blind Gaussian noise reaches 66.3% Rank@1 (R@1) under variable-length decoding but collapses to near chance once fixed-duration windows and stimulus-identity splits are enforced, isolating structural leakage. Under these controls, fixed-window retrieval recovers measurable MEG-audio discriminability, while an oracle sentence-bucket diagnostic shows that 95.7% of Top-1 errors select the wrong sentence, localising the residual bottleneck to sentence-level competition. We audit this contextual source with Group Context Bias (GCB), an inference-time additive logit bias that pools sentence-consistent evidence across windows while leaving the base retrieval scores and candidate pool fixed. Used as a score-space intervention, GCB makes the contextual source measurable: R@1 shifts from 44% to 52% on Gwilliams and from 22% to 29% on MOUS under the same fixed setting. GCB is auditable under this design: its effect collapses under random-grouping perturbations and vanishes when local evidence is attenuated in MEG or is near chance in EEG, supporting its use as a controlled source-attribution intervention. These results suggest that brain-to-language performance should be source-attributed, not merely reported.

</details>


### [46] [Rethinking Federated Unlearning via the Lens of Memorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24545)
*Jiaheng Wei,Yanjun Zhang,He Zhang,Leo Yu Zhang,Chao Chen,Kok-Leong Ong,Jun Zhang,Yang Xiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于记忆分离的联邦遗忘方法FedMemPrune，通过分组记忆评估区分独特记忆与重叠知识，并采用剪枝策略重置冗余参数，在保护隐私的同时保持模型效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦遗忘方法忽略遗忘数据与保留数据间的重叠信息，导致遗忘不彻底及客户端间不公平；需要一种能区分独特记忆与共享知识的遗忘机制。

Method: 提出分组记忆评估（Grouped Memorization Evaluation）作为样本级度量，分离记忆化知识与重叠知识；在此基础上提出联邦记忆剪枝（FedMemPrune），通过重置负责记忆的冗余参数实现遗忘。

Result: 大量实验表明FedMemPrune在匹配基于重训练的遗忘基线同时，比现有联邦遗忘算法更有效地消除记忆化，遗忘性能强且不牺牲保留知识的效用。

Conclusion: 通过记忆化视角重新审视联邦遗忘，证明了分离独特记忆与重叠知识是实现有效且公平的联邦遗忘的关键路径。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) increasingly needs machine unlearning to comply with privacy regulations. However, existing federated unlearning approaches may overlook the overlapping information between the unlearning and remaining data, leading to ineffective unlearning and unfairness between clients. In this work, we revisit federated unlearning through the lens of memorization. We argue that unlearning should mainly remove the unique memorized information attributable to the data to be forgotten, while preserving overlapping patterns that are also supported by the remaining data. Specifically, we propose Grouped Memorization Evaluation, an example-level metric that separates memorized knowledge from overlapping knowledge. Building on this metric, we introduce Federated Memorization Pruning (FedMemPrune), a pruning-based unlearning approach that resets redundant parameters responsible for memorization. Extensive experiments show that FedMemPrune closely matches retraining-based unlearning baselines while more effectively eliminating memorization than existing federated unlearning algorithms, yielding strong unlearning performance without sacrificing the utility of retained knowledge.

</details>


### [47] [RL with Learnable Textual Feedback: A Bilevel Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24547)
*Utsav Singh,Sidhaarth Sredharan,Souradip Chakraborty,Amrit Singh Bedi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出双层次自然语言演员-评论家方法（Bi-NAC），将可学习的文本反馈形式化为Stackelberg双层次优化问题，联合训练评论家生成能改善策略的反馈和演员利用这些反馈，在MATH-500、MBPP和GPQA上相比GRPO等基线方法显著提升样本和参数效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLHF方法中，文本反馈（critic生成的自然语言反馈）通常被视为固定或辅助信号，忽略了反馈不应仅正确、还应能实际改善策略这一关键属性。可学习反馈与策略的学习能力之间存在耦合关系，需要双层次优化框架来形式化。

Method: 将可学习文本反馈下强化学习问题形式化为Stackelberg双层次规划，提出Bi-NAC框架：外层优化评论家生成能最大化策略收益的反馈，内层优化演员利用反馈改进推理，两者联合训练。

Result: 在MATH-500上，2B模型Bi-NAC（46.6%）超越3B GRPO（41.4%）；在GPQA上，6B模型Bi-NAC（49.3%）超越7B GRPO（43.6%），展示出跨参数规模的样本和参数效率优势。

Conclusion: 可学习文本反馈的双层次优化能有效提升LLM推理强化学习的效率，Bi-NAC通过联合训练评论家和演员实现了优于固定反馈和纯奖励基线的性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards can improve LLM reasoning, but learning remains sample-inefficient when terminal rewards are sparse. This has motivated a growing line of work on RL with textual feedback, where a critic model generates natural language feedback to guide a reasoning model (the actor), augmenting scalar rewards with richer learning signals. However, existing methods typically treat feedback as fixed or auxiliary, which misses a key property: feedback should not merely be correct, but should improve the policy (actor model) when provided in context. This motivates a paradigm of learnable textual feedback for RL. Yet the learnability and usefulness of feedback depend on the policy's ability to learn from it, making RL with learnable feedback an inherently bilevel problem. We formalize this coupling as a Stackelberg bilevel program and derive Bilevel Natural Language Actor-Critic (Bi-NAC), which jointly trains a critic to generate reward-improving feedback and an actor to exploit it. Across MATH-500, MBPP, and GPQA, Bi-NAC improves sample and parameter efficiency over RL and fixed-critic baselines: our 2B model outperforms the 3B GRPO baseline, achieving 46.6% versus 41.4% on MATH-500, while our 6B model surpasses the 7B GRPO baseline, achieving 49.3% versus 43.6% on GPQA.

</details>


### [48] [Deep ZakaiJ: Structured Filtering for Jump-Diffusion Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24548)
*Yan Leng,Thibaut Mastrolia,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Deep ZakaiJ模型，将Zakai非线性滤波方程嵌入神经编码器-解码器架构，用于部分观测的跳跃扩散系统中的潜状态推断，通过Strang分裂实现可微分的滤波近似，在合成、金融和海洋数据集上提升了分布预测质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典跳跃扩散模型假设刚性参数形式，而近期神经跳跃模型在完全观测轨迹上操作，无法推断控制动态的隐藏状态，因此需要一种能处理部分观测且能推断潜状态的跳跃扩散系统模型。

Method: 将Zakai非线性滤波方程嵌入神经编码器-解码器架构；编码器通过Strang分裂为三个可解释子步骤（先验传播、扩散创新、跳跃创新）递归更新潜状态信念，得到一阶精度的可微滤波近似；解码器结构化为跳跃扩散模型，显式条件于滤波信念，保持连续动态与不连续冲击的分离。

Result: 在合成、金融和海洋数据集上，Deep ZakaiJ在分布预测上表现更优，点精度保持竞争力，实现了校准的预测区间，并在合成和定性案例研究中恢复了可解释的潜结构。

Conclusion: Deep ZakaiJ有效结合了经典滤波理论与深度学习，为部分观测的跳跃扩散系统提供了兼具理论原则性和可扩展性的建模方案。

Abstract: Time series driven by unobserved latent states frequently exhibit abrupt jump discontinuities whose timing and magnitude cannot be predicted from observed history alone. Classical jump-diffusion models offer a principled mathematical framework but assume rigid parametric forms, while recent neural jump models operate on fully observed trajectories without inferring the hidden states that govern the dynamics. We propose \textit{Deep ZakaiJ}, a latent-state model for partially observed jump-diffusion systems that embeds the Zakai nonlinear filtering equation into a neural encoder--decoder architecture. The encoder recursively updates a belief over the latent state via Strang splitting into three interpretable substeps: prior propagation, diffusion innovation, and jump innovation, yielding a differentiable, first-order-accurate approximation of the exact filtering evolution. The decoder is a structured jump-diffusion model explicitly conditioned on the filtered belief, preserving the separation between continuous dynamics and discontinuous shocks. On synthetic, financial, and oceanographic datasets, \textit{Deep ZakaiJ} improves distributional forecasts while remaining competitive in point accuracy, achieving calibrated predictive intervals and recovering interpretable latent structure in synthetic and qualitative case studies.

</details>


### [49] [Position: AI for Science Should Treat Measurement-to-Dataset Pipelines as Inference Components](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24558)
*Ling Zhan,Xiaoyao Yu,Tao Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出AI4Science工作流将数据集当作固定接口，但在依赖间接观测的领域中，从测量到数据集的管道本身就是推理组件。作者识别了三种失效模式（隐藏假设空间、未经认证的可迁移性、不受约束的多重性），并通过大规模神经科学实证审计发现跨数据集稳定性标准下存活率仅约0.0004%。呼吁将管道变为可计算的观测框架，以量化管道的充分性和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI4Science工作流往往将发布的数据集视为底层系统的固定接口，忽略了在多阶段测量、重建和预处理管道中产生的间接观测表征。作者认为这些测量到数据集的管道自身就是推理组件，将它们的输出当作"给定数据"会冻结观测模型并掩盖管道选择的不确定性。

Method: 论文首先从理论上识别出三种失效模式（隐藏假设空间、未经认证的可迁移性、不受约束的多重性），然后通过大规模神经科学实证审计来检验这些主张，采用跨数据集稳定性标准评估管道的存活率。

Result: 在跨数据集稳定性标准下，管道的生存率约为0.0004%，说明大多数管道在不同数据集间的稳定性和可迁移性极差。三种失效模式在实证中均得到验证。

Conclusion: 呼吁AI4Science社区通过领域特定的可计算观测框架（Computable Observation Frameworks）将管道变为可计算的推理对象，从而量化管道的充分性和稳定性，将隐式的实现选择转化为可审计、可复现、可累积的科学证据。

Abstract: AI for Science (AI4Science) workflows often treat the released dataset as a fixed interface to the underlying system.
  However, in domains relying on \emph{indirect observation}, the learner observes a derivative representation produced by multi-stage measurement, reconstruction, and preprocessing pipelines.
  \textbf{We argue that these measurement-to-dataset pipelines are inference components: treating their outputs as ``given data'' freezes an observation model and obscures uncertainty over feasible pipeline choices.}
  We identify three failure modes arising from this ``frozen lens'': \textbf{(C1) hidden hypothesis space}, where the released dataset does not specify the pipeline configuration or its validity conditions; \textbf{(C2) uncertified transportability}, where a pipeline may be documented but its regime of validity is untested, so failures under distribution shift cannot be adjudicated; \textbf{(C3) ungoverned multiplicity}, where many defensible pipelines exist and dispersion is real but not propagated into uncertainty-aware evidence.
  We stress-test these claims with a large-scale neuroscience empirical audit, finding a survival rate of $\approx 0.0004\%$ under a cross-dataset stability criterion.
  We call on the AI4Science community to make pipelines \emph{computable} inference objects via domain-specific Computable Observation Frameworks.
  This shift enables quantifying pipeline adequacy and stability, converting implicit implementation choices into auditable, reproducible, and cumulative scientific evidence.

</details>


### [50] [PILOT: Policy-Informed Learned Optimization for Adaptive Deep Network Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24570)
*Sattam Altuuaim,Lama Ayash,Muhammad Mubashar,Naeemullah Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PILOT是一种在线优化器，通过梯度方向一致性信号动态调整更新规则（动量、归一化、符号更新之间的平衡），在FashionMNIST和CIFAR-10上显著优于传统静态优化器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统深度学习优化器在训练前就固定了更新结构的函数形式，无法适应损失景观中梯度行为（稳定、噪声、不一致）的动态变化，限制了优化性能。

Method: 提出PILOT优化器，利用梯度方向一致性作为局部训练稳定性的信号，据此动态调整动量、归一化和符号更新之间的平衡，使优化器在梯度稳定、噪声或不一致时自适应改变更新行为。

Result: 在CNN架构上，PILOT在FashionMNIST达到94.13%，在CIFAR-10达到81.94%；在ResNet-18上，分别达到95.71%和93.42%，均高于所有对比优化器。

Conclusion: 在训练过程中学习自适应调整更新结构可以提升紧凑和深层卷积模型的性能，且仅需简单的一阶优化框架即可实现。

Abstract: Despite the central role of optimization in deep learning, most optimizers rely on update structures whose functional form is fixed before training begins. This static design can limit their ability to respond to changing gradient behavior across the loss landscape, where training may shift between stable, noisy, and inconsistent regimes.
  This study proposes PILOT (Policy-Informed Learned OpTimizer), an online optimizer that adapts its update behavior during training. Rather than using a fixed balance between momentum, normalization, and sign-based updates, PILOT uses gradient-direction agreement as a signal of local training stability. Conditioning the update rule on this agreement signal allows the optimizer to adjust its behavior when gradients become stable, noisy, or inconsistent.
  Experiments on FashionMNIST and CIFAR-10 show that PILOT consistently achieves the highest accuracy among the evaluated optimizers across convolutional settings. On the CNN architecture, PILOT reaches 94.13% on FashionMNIST and 81.94% on CIFAR-10. On ResNet-18, it further improves performance, reaching 95.71% on FashionMNIST and 93.42% on CIFAR-10. These results suggest that learning how to adapt the update structure during training can improve performance across both compact and deeper convolutional models while preserving a simple first-order optimization framework.
  The implementation of PILOT is publicly available at https://github.com/SattamAltwaim/PILOT.git

</details>


### [51] [Polymorphism Is Rotation: Operational Mechanistic Interpretability from a Two-Layer Transformer to Pythia-70m](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24577)
*Jordan F. McCann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 独立训练的Transformer在残差流基上计算相同函数，但这些基之间存在均匀随机旋转（SO(d_model)上的正交变换）。作者称这种现象为"多态性"：相同的函数，但内部坐标互不可理解。通过一对模型间一次矩阵乘法即可消除：在单批激活上进行正交Procrustes拟合，就能在独立训练的模型之间迁移稀疏自编码器特征字典和转向向量，无需重新训练。标准SAE通用性指标无法发现该现象。Procrustes旋转矩阵R服从Haar分布，其与随机正交矩阵的差异在0.1%以内。转向向量根据与R不变子空间的对齐程度呈现三种迁移模式。该结论在Dyck-3和Pythia-70m模型上得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发现并解释独立训练的Transformer虽计算相同函数，但其内部表征存在随机旋转的差异，这种"多态性"阻碍了模型间的可解释性工具（如SAE特征字典、转向向量）的直接迁移。

Method: 通过正交Procrustes分析两模型同一位置激活值之间的旋转关系；使用Haar分布检验验证旋转矩阵的随机性；分析转向向量在R的不变子空间下的迁移行为；在Dyck-3和Pythia-70m上验证。

Result: Procrustes旋转R服从Haar(SO(d_model))分布；标准SAE通用性指标（解码器列余弦相似度98%）掩盖了编码器-解码器坐标框架的不对齐；一个Procrustes旋转即可恢复重组质量至EV 0.025以内；转向向量呈现三种迁移模式（干净、部分、反转），无共享I/O时全部反转。

Conclusion: 独立训练的同构Transformer之间存在不可约的随机旋转差异，但通过Procrustes拟合可消除这一障碍，实现可解释性工具的跨模型迁移。这揭示了"表征多态性"现象，为模型比较和集成提供了新视角。

Abstract: Independently trained transformers compute the same function in residual-stream bases that differ by a uniform random rotation on $\mathrm{SO}(d_{\mathrm{model}})$. We call this phenomenon polymorphism: same function, mutually unintelligible interior coordinates. One matrix multiplication per model pair removes it: an orthogonal Procrustes fit on a single batch of activations transfers sparse-autoencoder feature dictionaries and steering vectors between independently trained models, with no retraining.
  The phenomenon is invisible to the standard SAE universality metric. Decoder-column cosine similarity matches across seeds at 98%, the SAE-universality headline number, while an SAE trained on one seed reconstructs another seed's activations at negative explained variance, worse than predicting the constant mean. The decoder columns align; the encoder reads from a rotated frame. A single Procrustes rotation $R$ restores reconstruction to within 0.025 EV of the within-seed ceiling at every internal site.
  $R$ is Haar-distributed: $\|R - I\|_F$ matches the random-orthogonal prediction $\sqrt{2 d_{\mathrm{model}}}$ to 0.1% at $d_{\mathrm{model}} = 512$, and a Kolmogorov-Smirnov test of $R$'s eigenvalue spectrum against Haar $\mathrm{SO}(d_{\mathrm{model}})$ returns $p \approx 1.000$ pooled and per-pair. Diff-of-means steering vectors transfer in three regimes by alignment with $R$'s invariant subspace: clean when pinned by shared output weights, partial when overlapping the rotated subspace, inverted otherwise. With no shared I/O (Pythia), all three collapse to universally inverted. The same rotation account holds across training checkpoints within a single run.
  Validated on a 104k-parameter Dyck-3 transformer and nine independently-trained Pythia-70m seeds on The Pile, via a pre-registered four-bar operational framework. Frontier-scale (10B+) replication remains open.

</details>


### [52] [An Effective-Rank Audit of Alignment-Induced Activation Shifts: Confound Control, Constructive Calibration, and Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24583)
*Yuki Nakamura*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过有效秩 ρ_eps 量化了指令微调LLM的对齐诱导激活偏移，提出了混淆控制分解方法，揭示了秩诊断的脆弱性本质及其局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究（Arditi et al., 2024）观察到单拒绝方向现象，但缺乏量化该现象的连续指标，且对对齐诱导激活偏移的测量受chat模板等混淆因素干扰，亟需系统性的诊断工具。

Method: 提出对齐修正矩阵的有效秩 ρ_eps = rank_eps(M_Ds)/d 作为连续量化指标；设计四变量分解（M_naive, M_template, M_aligned, M_DiD）分离模板格式化、对齐阶段偏移和拒绝中介方向；在3层MLP上构建校准实验检验秩与鲁棒性的关系。

Result: (1) 混淆控制后 ρ_eps 为 {0.0029, 0.0048, 0.0044}，SVD残差大4-7倍；(2) 适度秩最大化（λ=5）带来消融鲁棒性，强正则化（λ=50）则否，ρ_eps是脆弱性指标而非鲁棒性目标；(3) 秩诊断不具安全性特异性（LRH基线高2-3倍），SVD主序与因果序不匹配，谱隙假设在经验上和结构上均失败。

Conclusion: ρ_eps 可作为对齐诱导偏移的脆弱性诊断指标，但不能直接作为提升鲁棒性的优化目标；匹配的Mirsky下界仍为开放问题。

Abstract: We audit alignment-induced shifts in residual-stream activations of three open-weight instruction-tuned LLMs (Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, Gemma-2-9B-it, Qwen-2.5-7B-Instruct) using the effective rank of the alignment modification matrix on safety-relevant inputs, rho_eps := rank_eps(M_Ds)/d, which formalizes the single-refusal-direction observation of Arditi et al. (2024) as a continuous quantity. The paper has three contributions. (1) Confound-controlled measurement: a four-variant decomposition (M_naive, M_template, M_aligned, M_DiD) separates chat-template formatting, alignment-stage shift, and the refusal-mediating direction, and recovers the Arditi refusal direction on M_DiD at |cos| in {0.77, 0.86, 0.50} (Llama/Gemma/Qwen); chat-template-controlled rho_eps is {0.0029, 0.0048, 0.0044}, and the centered SVD residual is 4-7x larger. (2) Constructive calibration on a 3-layer MLP across rho_eps in {0.008, 0.17, 0.33, 0.40} exhibits a sweet-spot vs. brittle distinction: mild rank-maximization (lambda=5) buys ablation robustness, while strong regularization at the same nominal rho_eps (lambda=50) does not. rho_eps is a diagnostic for fragility, not a target whose mechanical inflation buys robustness. (3) Limits of rank-based diagnostics: (a) not safety-specific (LRH baseline is 2-3x the safety value); (b) SVD principal ordering does not match causal ordering (Llama u_2 inert despite ranking second; cumulative ablation non-monotone at k=5); (c) the spectral-gap hypothesis required to upgrade the O(rho_eps * d) achievability bound to a matching Mirsky-route lower bound fails empirically (1/90 Llama layer-reference pairs, 0/36 MLP combinations) and structurally (kappa_lb <= 2/(eps * r)). The matching lower bound remains an open problem.

</details>


### [53] [LAPLEX: The FFT of Learnable Laplace Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24584)
*Łukasz Struski,Hanna Blazhko,Piotr Kubaty,Jacek Tabor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LAPLEX 是一类基于可学习坐标锚点的精确可训练拉普拉斯核算子，支持维度高达 10^9 的隐式全秩稠密矩阵-向量运算，实现了在无需存储稠密矩阵的情况下获得稠密层的表达能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习中的快速线性代数方法存在一种权衡：要么采用固定几何与精确计算（如傅里叶变换），要么采用自适应几何但需要稠密参数、随机特征或低秩近似。LAPLEX 旨在突破这一权衡，实现兼具几何自适应性和精确高效计算的方法。

Method: LAPLEX 层是一种由可学习坐标锚点隐式定义的全秩稠密矩阵，具有类似 FFT 的计算缩放特性。它通过结构化的小参数集来表示和应用稠密矩阵，从而支持维度高达 10^9 的可训练矩阵-向量运算。

Result: LAPLEX 可生成紧凑的投影和分类头（可解释为软性可训练路由模型），也可作为高效 Gram 算子用于维度为 3·10^6 的展平图像的高维协方差建模，同时保留可见空间结构且不施加卷积偏置。

Conclusion: LAPLEX 实现了表达性与存储成本的解耦：行为如同稠密可训练矩阵，但通过少量结构化参数表示和应用。这使得在普通稠密层无法企及的场景中实现数据自适应的全局交互成为可能。

Abstract: Fast linear algebra in deep learning usually comes with a choice: fixed geometry and exact computation, as in the Fourier transform, or adaptive geometry paid for by dense parameters, random features, or low-rank surrogates. To move beyond this trade-off, we introduce LAPLEX, a class of exact, trainable (phased) Laplace-kernel operators.
  A LAPLEX layer is a typically full-rank dense matrix, implicitly defined by learnable coordinate anchors, with FFT-like scaling. Consequently, it supports trainable matrix--vector operations at vector dimensions up to $10^9$ on modern GPUs. As a neural layer, it yields compact projections and classification heads interpretable as soft, trainable routing models. The same primitive also serves as an efficient Gram operator, enabling high-dimensional covariance models on flattened images of dimension $3 \cdot 10^6$ that preserve visible spatial structure without imposing convolutional bias. These applications reflect a single principle: dense geometry can be learned without storing a dense matrix, which enables data-adaptive global interactions in regimes where ordinary dense layers are out of reach. In this sense, LAPLEX separates expressivity from storage cost: it behaves like a dense trainable matrix, but is represented and applied through a small structured set of parameters.

</details>


### [54] [Beyond Generative Priors: Minority Sampling with JEPA-Guided Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24631)
*Sol Park,Soobin Um*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于JEPA引导的扩散采样框架，通过世界模型（JEPA）定义的真实世界先验来生成低密度（少数）样本，解决了传统方法依赖生成模型先验导致语义稀缺性失真问题，在无条件、类别条件和文生图任务中显著提升了少数样本的保真度和语义有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有少数采样方法依赖生成模型（如扩散模型）学到的先验来定义"稀缺性"，导致生成的少数样本仅相对于生成器自身的密度分布而言是稀有的，无法反映真实世界中的语义稀缺性。作者主张从世界中心（world-centric）的视角出发，以真实世界先验（而非生成器诱导密度）来定义稀有性。

Method: 提出JEPA Guidance框架：在扩散采样过程中，利用联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）——一种世界模型，编码了广泛且语义丰富的表征——作为引导信号，将扩散轨迹引向JEPA隐式密度下的低密度区域。为降低计算开销，设计了原则性的近似策略并给出了理论误差界。

Result: 在无条件生成、类别条件生成和文生图三种设置下的大量实验表明，JEPA引导始终提升了少数样本的保真度和语义有效性，在捕捉真实世界的稀缺性方面全面优于基于生成器的基线方法。

Conclusion: 以世界模型（JEPA）作为引导源来定义和采样语义稀缺样本是可行且有效的，能够更好地对齐真实世界的稀有性概念，为少数采样提供了新的范 paradigm。

Abstract: Minority sampling aims to generate low-density instances on a data manifold and is of central importance in applications such as medical diagnosis, anomaly detection, and creative AI. Existing approaches, however, define minority samples relative to generative priors learned from training data, confining rarity to model-specific notions that may poorly reflect real-world semantics. In this work, we propose a world-centric perspective on minority sampling, which defines rarity with respect to real-world priors rather than generator-induced densities. To this end, we introduce JEPA guidance, a diffusion sampling framework guided by a Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) -- a class of world models that encode broad, semantically rich representations. JEPA guidance steers diffusion trajectories toward low-density regions under the implicit density induced by the JEPA, thereby aligning generated minorities with real-world semantic rarity. To make JEPA guidance computationally practical, we develop principled approximation strategies accompanied by theoretical error bounds, significantly reducing the overhead of guidance computation. Extensive experiments across unconditional, class-conditional, and text-to-image generation demonstrate that JEPA guidance consistently improves the fidelity and semantic validity of minority samples, outperforming generator-centric baselines in capturing real-world notions of rarity. Code is available at https://github.com/soobin-um/jepa-guidance.

</details>


### [55] [WLNO: Wavelet-Laplace Neural Operator for Solving Partial Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24658)
*Muhammad Abid,Arth Sojitra,Omer San*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WLNO通过将Haar小波多尺度空间分解与拉普拉斯神经算子（LNO）的极点-残差公式融合，在五个基准PDE问题上持续优于LNO，尤其在具有强空间多尺度结构的 Burgers 方程和Navier-Stokes方程上改进最为显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LNO虽能通过可学习系统极点和残差捕获瞬态与稳态动力学，但缺乏显式机制来提取复杂PDE解中固有的空间局部化多尺度特征。

Method: 在LNO核心上添加并行单级Haar离散小波变换分支，将提升后的特征图分解为四个频段（LL、HL、LH、HH），对各子带独立应用可学习1×1卷积后通过逆DWT重构，两分支通过可学习sigmoid门控权重α_wav融合。

Result: 在扩散方程、Burgers方程、反应扩散系统、Darcy流和二维Navier-Stokes方程五个基准上，WLNO一致优于LNO，在具有尖锐激波前和相干涡结构的强空间多尺度问题上改进最大。

Conclusion: 基于小波的多尺度空间分解是拉普拉斯域算子学习的原理性且有效的补充。

Abstract: This work introduces the Wavelet-Laplace Neural Operator (WLNO), a novel neural operator that fuses Haar wavelet multi-scale spatial decomposition with the Laplace-domain pole-residue formulation of the Laplace Neural Operator (LNO). While LNO captures transient and steady-state dynamics through learnable system poles and residues, it lacks an explicit mechanism for extracting spatially localized multi-scale features inherent in complex PDE solutions. WLNO addresses this by augmenting the LNO core with a parallel single-level Haar discrete wavelet transform (DWT) branch that decomposes the lifted feature map into four frequency subbands: approximation (LL), horizontal detail (LH), vertical detail (HL), and diagonal detail (HH) and applies independent learned $1\times1$ convolutions to each subband before reconstruction via the inverse DWT. The two branches are fused through a learnable sigmoid-gated weight $α_\mathrm{wav}$, initialized to give a small initial contribution to the wavelet branch, allowing the model to adaptively balance Laplace-domain dynamics against spatial multi-scale features throughout training. WLNO is evaluated against LNO on five benchmark PDE problems using identical hyperparameters, training data, and evaluation protocols: the diffusion equation, the Burgers equation, the reaction-diffusion system, Darcy flow, and the two-dimensional Navier-Stokes equation. WLNO consistently outperforms LNO on all five problems, with the most pronounced improvement on problems with strong spatial multi-scale structure, such as the Burgers equation with sharp shock fronts and the Navier-Stokes equation with coherent vortical structures, while remaining consistent across smoother and elliptic problems. These results demonstrate that wavelet-based multi-scale spatial decomposition is a principled and effective complement to Laplace-domain operator learning.

</details>


### [56] [IterInject: Indirect Prompt Injection Against LLM Agents via Feedback-Guided Iterative Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24659)
*Zixuan Chen,Jiaxiang Chen,Li Luo,Ke Xu,Xiaoxiang Huang,Tanfeng Sun,Xinghao Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出反馈驱动的迭代框架\oursys，通过诊断器-优化器-生成器闭环自动优化间接提示注入攻击，在多个基准和Claude Code上显著优于现有方法，并通过机制分析发现注意力阈值机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有间接提示注入攻击依赖静态载荷，无法自适应智能体的防御机制；即使自适应方法也缺乏结构化反馈指导优化。

Method: 提出\oursys框架，包含三个闭环模块：基于规则的诊断器生成结构化结果标签与行为描述；基于LLM的优化器结合完整优化历史精炼载荷；合成步骤从失败模式生成新伪装种子实现策略空间自演化。

Result: 在AgentDojo和InjectAgent上显著优于静态基线和现有自适应方法；在Claude Code上对9个目标中的5个实现完全成功；机制分析发现注意力阈值机制并通过因果干预验证。

Conclusion: 反馈驱动的迭代优化能有效突破智能体防御，注意力阈值机制为防御设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: LLM-based agents are increasingly deployed for complex tasks requiring planning, tool use, and interaction with external services. Their reliance on untrusted external content exposes them to indirect prompt injection (IPI), in which adversarial instructions embedded in retrieved data hijack agent behavior. Existing attacks rely on static payloads that cannot adapt to agent-specific defenses; even recent adaptive methods lack structured feedback to guide optimization. We introduce \oursys, a feedback-guided iterative framework that closes the loop between injection, diagnosis, and refinement: a rule-based diagnoser produces structured outcome labels with behavioral descriptions, and an LLM-based optimizer refines payloads conditioned on the full optimization history. A synthesis step generates new disguise seeds from failure patterns, enabling the strategy space to self-evolve. On AgentDojo and InjectAgent, \oursys substantially outperforms static baselines and existing adaptive methods across four victim models. Extension experiments on Claude Code, a production-grade coding agent with layered defenses, show that optimized payloads achieve full success on 5 of 9 targets; even those that resist full exploitation exhibit measurable improvement from iterative refinement. We further present a mechanistic analysis of IPI, identifying an attention-mediated threshold mechanism in mid-to-late layers; three causal interventions validate this finding and point to concrete defense directions.

</details>


### [57] [Trajectory-Based Difficulty Scoring for Reliable Learning on Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24680)
*Tomer Lavi,Bracha Shapira,Nadav Rappoport*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于轨迹的难度分数（TDS），利用梯度提升树中每棵树的累积预测轨迹来计算实例级难度估计，并在多个基准测试中优于现有方法。TDS可应用于主动学习、选择性预测和共形预测等数据挖掘任务，并通过SHAP聚类揭示失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度提升树在表格数据上表现优异，但往往对长尾的难例预测效果不佳，缺乏有效的实例级难度评估方法。

Method: 从每棵树的累积预测轨迹中提取可解释的描述符（如方差、振荡峰值、符号翻转和尾部稳定性），训练轻量级回归模型预测留出损失，并通过经验CDF将结果校准为[0,1]区间的难度分数。

Result: TDS与误差的秩相关性很强，在分类任务上优于已有的实例难度和不确定性基线，在回归任务上具有竞争力。在主动学习、选择性预测和共形预测中均带来性能提升。

Conclusion: TDS作为一种通用的实例级难度信号，能够有效改进多种数据挖掘流程，且通过SHAP聚类可识别出具有紧凑特征值范围的失败模式，辅助错误分析和定向数据采集。

Abstract: Gradient-boosted trees achieve strong performance on tabular data, yet often leave a long tail of poorly predicted instances. We introduce a Trajectory-based Difficulty Score (TDS), an instance-level difficulty estimator for boosted ensembles derived from per-tree cumulative prediction trajectories. For each instance, we compute interpretable trajectory descriptors (e.g., variance, oscillation peaks, sign switches, and tail stability) and train a lightweight regression model to predict held-out loss. An empirical CDF calibrates the resulting signal into a score in $[0,1]$ that supports ranking hard cases. Across diverse tabular benchmarks and ensemble sizes, TDS exhibits strong rank correlation with error and outperforms established instance-hardness and uncertainty baselines on classification, while remaining competitive on regression. We then show how a single difficulty signal improves multiple data mining workflows: difficulty-driven active learning for label-efficient training, difficulty-thresholded selective prediction for improved risk-coverage trade-offs, and TDS-stratified (Mondrian) conformal prediction for more uniform conditional coverage. Finally, clustering high-TDS instances using SHAP attributions reveals coherent failure modes characterized by compact feature-value ranges, supporting error analysis and targeted data acquisition.

</details>


### [58] [Hardware-Aware Federated Learning for Speech Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24712)
*Beyazit Bestami Yuksel,Emrah Dikbiyik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种硬件感知的联邦学习框架，用于IEMOCAP情感识别任务。该框架集成了硬件性能分析、top-K客户端选择和自适应本地轮次，在非IID设置下与FedAvg、FedProx和随机top-K选择方法进行比较。实验结果表明，该方法在50轮联邦训练和5次独立试验中，验证准确率达到0.352，总训练时间比FedAvg减少约36.5%，累计通信成本降低40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习实际部署中，分布式边缘设备存在处理能力、内存容量和通信延迟的异构性，导致训练轮次耗时增加和系统成本上升，现有方法未充分考虑硬件异构性对训练效率的影响。

Method: 提出一种硬件感知联邦学习框架，在统一训练循环中集成三项关键技术：硬件性能分析（hardware profiling）、top-K客户端选择（top-K client selection）和自适应本地轮次（adaptive local epochs），并在会话分区的IEMOCAP数据集上进行情感识别任务验证。

Result: 在50轮联邦训练和5次独立试验中，验证准确率达到0.352（与对比方法持平或略优），总训练时间相较于FedAvg减少约36.5%，累计通信成本降低40%。

Conclusion: 硬件感知的联邦学习框架能够在保持情感识别任务准确率的同时，显著降低训练时间和通信开销，有效解决了客户端异构性带来的效率问题。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables privacy-preserving collaborative training across distributed edge devices, but real deployments involve heterogeneous clients with different processing power, memory capacity, and communication latency, which often increase round duration and system cost. This paper proposes a hardware-aware federated learning framework for emotion recognition on session-partitioned IEMOCAP that integrates hardware profiling, top-K client selection, and adaptive local epochs within a unified training loop. We compare the method against FedAvg, FedProx, and random top-K selection under a non-IID setup and show that, across 50 federated rounds and 5 independent trials, the proposed approach achieves competitive validation accuracy (0.352), reduces total training time by about 36.5% compared to FedAvg, and lowers cumulative communication cost by 40%.

</details>


### [59] [Reinforcement Learning for Reachability: Guaranteeing Asymptotic Optimality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24740)
*Amogh Palasamudram,Jakub Svoboda,Suguman Bansal,Krishnendu Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对强化学习中的可达性规范问题，提出了一种基于PAC学习的迭代方法，在无需知道MDP内部参数的情况下，通过逐步精细化参数估计，实现了极限情况下的精确最优性，并在标准基准测试上验证了收敛动态的理论见解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作虽然实现了可达性规范下RL的渐近收敛到最优策略，但对收敛动态的洞察有限。本文旨在提供一种能更深入理解收敛动态的理论方法。

Method: 基于PAC学习框架，假设已知MDP内部参数（如最小转移概率）；但实际RL中这些参数未知，因此提出迭代式地精细化估计这些参数，逐步满足PAC条件，从而在极限情况下达到精确最优性。

Result: 在标准基准测试上的经验评估验证了所提出的收敛动态理论见解。

Conclusion: 通过迭代式PAC学习，无需预先知道MDP内部参数，也能在极限情况下实现精确最优策略，并为收敛动态提供了更深入的理论理解。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) for reachability specifications is fundamental in sequential decision-making, yet theoretical guarantees remain less explored. A recent work achieves asymptotic convergence to optimal policies. However, this approach provides limited insight into convergence dynamics. In this work, we present an alternative approach that provides deeper theoretical insights into convergence. Our approach builds on PAC learning with assumptions. PAC learning guarantees near-optimal policies with high confidence in finite time but requires knowing internal MDP parameters like minimum transition probability. We argue that while these parameters are unknown in RL, they can be iteratively refined and estimated with increasing accuracy. By iteratively satisfying PAC conditions, we show that exact optimality can be achieved in the limit. Empirical evaluations on standard benchmarks validate our theoretical insights into convergence dynamics.

</details>


### [60] [Aligning Molecular Graph Explanations with Chemical Identity via InChIfied Invariants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24742)
*Emanuele Guidotti,Sara Puglioli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于InChI的图不变量（InChIfied Invariants），使分子图表示在化学等价变换下保持一致，在99.62%的化学等价图上产生相同表示（标准Daylight不变量仅0.35%），同时保持预测性能并显著提升解释一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子图的非规范绘制方式会导致同一分子产生不同的图表示，进而引发预测和归因解释的不一致。现有方法缺乏对化学等价变换的鲁棒性，亟需一种能保持化学身份一致性的分子图特征表示方法。

Method: 基于国际化学标识符（InChI）构造节点、边和图级别的三类不变量特征（InChIfied Invariants），这些特征对保持化学身份的所有变换（如原子重编号、键顺序重排等）具有不变性。使用PubChem Substances百万级分子图进行表示一致性测试，并在MoleculeNet任务上评估预测性能和一致性。

Result: InChIfied Invariants在99.62%的化学等价图上产生完全一致的表示，而Daylight不变量仅0.35%。在MoleculeNet任务上保持了相当的预测性能，同时显著提升了不同图绘制方式下的预测一致性。归因分析表明标准方法在不同等价图间产生差异大的解释，而InChIfied Invariants强制保持解释一致。

Conclusion: InChIfied Invariants可作为标准分子图特征的即插即用替代方案，有效解决分子图机器学习中的一致性问题，兼顾预测性能与可解释性的一致性。

Abstract: Obtaining consistent explanations for machine learning on molecular graphs requires predictions and attributions to be aligned with chemical identity. However, chemically equivalent drawings of the same molecule can induce different molecular representations, leading to inconsistent predictions and explanations. Here, we introduce InChIfied Invariants, a class of node, edge, and graph features based on the International Chemical Identifier (InChI) and designed to be invariant under transformations that preserve chemical identity. Using one million molecular graphs from PubChem Substances, we show that InChIfied Invariants produce identical representations for chemically equivalent graphs in 99.62% of cases, whereas standard Daylight invariants do so in only 0.35% of cases. Across MoleculeNet tasks, InChIfied Invariants preserve predictive performance while significantly improving prediction consistency across alternative graph depictions of the same molecules. We further perform a quantitative attribution analysis and show that explanations produced with standard molecular featurization methods vary substantially across chemically equivalent graphs, while InChIfied Invariants enforce consistent attributions by construction. We release open-source software implementing InChIfied Invariants, which can be used as a drop-in replacement for standard molecular graph features.

</details>


### [61] [Bilevel Optimization of Synthetic Trajectories for Multi-Turn LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24743)
*Shresth Verma,Mauricio Tec,Cheol Woo Kim,Kai Wang,Milind Tambe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出BOOST框架，通过双层优化对合成轨迹进行加权训练，解决LLM在多轮交互中依赖低质量合成数据的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习训练LLM进行多轮交互时，性能受限于合成轨迹数据的质量异质性，均匀对待所有轨迹会降低性能。

Method: 采用双层优化：内层在加权数据上训练LLM，外层在真实验证任务上训练轻量级重加权头，为轨迹分配连续权重，无需外部评判器。

Result: 在多个基准上持续优于基线方法；分析表明该方法倾向于为与真实数据分布对齐且质量更高的合成轨迹赋予更高权重。

Conclusion: BOOST通过可学习的数据加权有效缓解了合成数据异质性问题，且PAC-Bayesian界揭示了多样性、任务偏移和有效样本量之间的三方权衡。

Abstract: While LLMs excel at single-turn generation, they struggle with long-horizon, multi-turn interactions. Offline reinforcement learning (RL) offers a scalable approach, yet its performance hinges on the availability and quality of multi-turn trajectory data. A common remedy is to augment training with synthetic trajectories generated by LLMs or simulators, but synthetic data is highly heterogeneous in quality, and naively treating all trajectories as equally informative can degrade performance. We propose BOOST, a bilevel optimization framework where the inner level trains the LLM on reweighted data and the outer level trains a lightweight reweighting head on held-out real validation tasks, assigning continuous trajectory-level weights without requiring an external judge. To ground this approach, we derive a PAC-Bayesian bound revealing a three-way trade-off: synthetic data increases diversity but risks task-shift, while concentrating weight on high-quality trajectories improves empirical performance at the cost of effective sample size. Empirically, our method consistently outperforms multiple baselines. Analysis reveals it upweights synthetic trajectories that align with the real data distribution and exhibit higher qualitative merit.

</details>


### [62] [A computational phase transition for learning-to-sample from Ising models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24752)
*Andrej Risteski,Thuy-Duong Vuong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对伊辛模型中的"学习-采样"任务，构造了恰超出谱阈值 $\lambda_{\max}(J)-\lambda_{\min}(J)=1$ 的常宽度伊辛模型族，并在标准密码学假设下证明了该任务的计算困难性，同时揭示了学习-采样与参数学习之间的难度差异，以及高效学习器必然存在的"记忆-幻觉"二分现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究生成建模中"学习-采样"这一基础算法任务的计算复杂性，特别是在伊辛模型这一标准测试框架下，明确谱阈值处的计算相变边界。

Method: 构造恰超出谱阈值 $\lambda_{\max}(J)-\lambda_{\min}(J)=1$ 的常宽度伊辛模型族，基于标准密码学假设证明学习-采样的计算困难性，并分析高效学习器在记忆与幻觉之间的权衡。

Result: (1) 在谱阈值以上，学习-采样在标准密码学假设下是计算困难的；(2) 结合先前结果，谱阈值处存在尖锐的计算相变；(3) 学习-采样比参数学习更困难；(4) 高效学习器必然呈现"记忆-幻觉"二分现象。

Conclusion: 确立了伊辛模型学习-采样任务在谱阈值处的计算相变，揭示了该任务相比参数学习的额外难度，并刻画了高效算法在记忆与泛化之间的本质权衡。

Abstract: We study \emph{learning-to-sample} -- a basic algorithmic task underlying generative modeling -- for Ising models, a standard testbed for algorithmic ideas in both theoretical computer science and machine learning. Given i.i.d. samples of an unknown target distribution, the goal of learning-to-sample is to learn a computationally efficient generation procedure that produces new samples following approximately the same distribution. We construct a family of Ising models of constantly bounded-width which lie just beyond the spectral threshold $λ_{\max}(J)-λ_{\min}(J)=1$, and show that learning-to-sample for this family is computationally hard under standard cryptographic assumptions, even when the learner is given both polynomially many i.i.d. samples from the model and explicit access to its parameters. Combined with results of [AJKPV24,KLV25] showing tractability of learning-to-sample below the spectral threshold, this establishes a sharp computational phase transition at the spectral threshold. Moreover, combined with prior results on parameter learning for bounded-width Ising models [KM17,WSD19,VML20], this shows that learning-to-sample can be more difficult than parameter learning. Finally, we show that any efficient learner for these hard instances exhibits a natural memorization-hallucination dichotomy: the learner must either output configurations that, after a simple transformation, match the (transformed) training data or place substantial mass on configurations of negligible probability under the target distribution.

</details>


### [63] [A Contractive Feedback Semantics for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24759)
*Zuyuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从组合视角重新审视折扣强化学习中的策略评估问题，将一步决策过程视为开放随机组件，通过收缩反馈闭环实现无限时域策略评估，建立了带类型的贝尔曼变换子语义，并提出了近似组件等价、状态抽象和契约语义三个理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的折扣强化学习通常基于闭马尔可夫决策过程的贝尔曼方程，缺乏组合性视角。本文旨在发展一种组合化观点，将策略评估问题分解为开放组件的交互与反馈闭环，从而为近似等价、状态抽象和安全性规约提供更系统的理论基础。

Method: 将一步决策过程视为开放随机组件，通过收缩反馈闭环实现无限时域策略评估；为开放组件赋予带类型的贝尔曼变换子语义，串/并联接线解释为变换子的复合与张量积，反馈解释为容许的带防护巴拿赫迹（由唯一不动点实现）。

Result: 建立了三个理论结果：(1) 近似组件等价在容许的带防护单孔上下文中构成上下文同余，局部算子误差受控；(2) 精确和近似状态抽象成为交换或近交换的余代数图，给出值保持性和显式上确界范数失真界；(3) 在单调ω-连续收缩变换子语义下，安全、风险和资源规约可表示为量子值契约，局部归纳界通过最小不动点推理提升。

Conclusion: 本文的核心主张并非所有RL态射构成全局迹幺半范畴，而是折扣贝尔曼评估在容许的带防护电路类上 admits 一种收缩反馈语义。

Abstract: Discounted reinforcement learning is usually presented through Bellman equations on closed Markov decision processes. This paper develops a compositional view: a one-step decision process is treated as an open stochastic component, and infinite-horizon policy evaluation is obtained by closing a contractive feedback loop. The resulting semantics assigns typed Bellman transformers to open components, interprets series and parallel wiring as composition and tensoring of transformers, and interprets feedback as an admissible guarded Banach trace realized by a unique fixed point. This perspective yields three theoretical consequences. First, approximate component equivalence is a contextual congruence for admitted well-typed guarded one-hole contexts: local operator error remains controlled after plugging the component into a surrounding circuit that uses the hole once and whose feedback nodes have certified uniform guardedness. Second, exact and approximate state abstractions become commuting or near-commuting coalgebraic diagrams, giving value-preservation and explicit sup-norm distortion bounds. Third, under monotone $ω$-continuous contract-transformer semantics, safety, risk, and resource specifications can be represented as quantale-valued contracts, where local inductive bounds lift through wiring and feedback by least-fixed-point reasoning. Its central claim is not that all RL morphisms form a global traced monoidal category, but that discounted Bellman evaluation admits a contractive feedback semantics on the admissible class of guarded circuits.

</details>


### [64] [Muon in Vision Transformers: Optimizer-Recipe Interactions and Gradient Spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24770)
*Ben S. Southworth,Shuai Jiang,Daniel McBride,Eric C. Cyr,Stephen Thomas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Muon优化器在ViT训练中优于AdamW，尤其在长尾数据集上；其优势依赖于数据增强策略，通过分析梯度奇异值结构发现，Muon在注意力投影中具有更宽的谱基，而完整的数据增强可防止深层前馈块中的模式坍塌。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon是一种新型矩阵感知优化器，在Transformer训练中表现优异，但其在视觉Transformer(ViT)中的行为尚不明确，因此需要系统研究。

Method: 在ImageNet-100和Pl@ntNet-300K上比较Muon与AdamW，使用mixup、cutmix、smoothing、随机增强和擦除等标准视觉策略；通过分析ViT中矩阵梯度的奇异值结构来理解优化器与数据增强的交互机制。

Result: Muon一致优于AdamW，在长尾Pl@ntNet数据上macro top-1提升尤为显著；Muon比AdamW更能从高级数据增强中获益；完整增强下Muon与AdamW的差异主要体现在QKV梯度上，AdamW梯度能量集中在更窄的基中，而Muon分布在更多奇异模式上；缺乏数据增强时Muon在深层MLP-down块出现训练后期谱集中和模式坍塌。

Conclusion: Muon在ViT中应被理解为优化器-数据增强的交互作用；在图像分割和掩码自编码器模型中Muon同样优于AdamW。

Abstract: Muon is a recently developed matrix-aware optimizer that has shown strong results in transformer training, but its behavior in vision transformers (ViTs) is not yet well understood. We study Muon for ViT training, largely on ImageNet-100 and Pl@ntNet-300K, comparing against AdamW under standard vision recipes involving mixup, cutmix, smoothing, and random augmentation and erasing. Muon consistently outperforms AdamW, with especially large gains on long-tailed Pl@ntNet macro top-1. These gains are also recipe-dependent, where Muon benefits much more than AdamW from advanced and significant data augmentation techniques. To understand this interaction, we analyze the singular-value structure of matrix gradients throughout the ViT. Within Muon training runs, removing heavy data augmentation induces a late-training spectral concentration and mode collapse in gradient matrices, primarily in deep MLP-down blocks. Under a fixed "full" augmentation recipe, the clearest Muon-AdamW contrast appears instead in QKV gradients, where AdamW gradient energy remains concentrated in a much narrower basis while Muon spreads energy across substantially more singular modes. Muon in ViTs is therefore best understood as an optimizer-recipe interaction. Under a fixed recipe, Muon differs from AdamW most clearly in attention projections, where its gradients consist of a broader spectral basis. Within Muon, a full training recipe is important for preventing late spectral concentration and mode collapse in deep feedforward blocks. We further demonstrate efficacy in training ViTs on image segmentation and masked autoencoder models, where Muon outperforms AdamW in all settings considered.

</details>


### [65] [Hermite-NGP: Gradient-Augmented Hash Encoding for Learning PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24774)
*Jinjin He,Zhiqi Li,Sinan Wang,Bo Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Hermite-NGP是一种梯度增强的多分辨率哈希编码方法，通过显式存储哈希网格顶点处的函数值和混合偏导数，利用Hermite插值实现全解析梯度、雅可比和海森矩阵计算，支持二阶微分算子，在神经PDE求解中比现有方法误差降低约20倍，收敛速度提升2-10倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于NGP的方法依赖于自动微分或有限差分来计算空间导数，存在不稳定性或高计算成本的问题，亟需一种既能保持NGP的高效性和空间自适应性，又能实现稳定、快速且准确的微分计算的编码方式。

Method: 提出Hermite-NGP，在哈希网格顶点处显式存储函数值及混合偏导数，通过Hermite插值实现梯度、雅可比矩阵和海森矩阵的全解析计算，支持二阶微分算子的解析求值；同时引入多分辨率课程训练策略，模拟多重网格V-cycle实现从粗到细的优化。

Result: 在多个2D和3D PDE基准测试中，Hermite-NGP相比先前的神经PDE方法误差降低约20倍，壁钟收敛时间缩短2-10倍，对于参数量高达1700万的模型，每轮训练时间仅需3.5毫秒。

Conclusion: Hermite-NGP通过显式存储导数值并利用Hermite插值实现解析微分，有效解决了现有NGP方法在微分计算上的不稳定和高成本问题，兼具效率、准确性和空间自适应性，为神经PDE求解器提供了一种高效且精确的替代方案。

Abstract: We propose Hermite-NGP, a gradient-augmented multi-resolution hash encoding designed to enable fast and accurate computation of spatial derivatives for neural PDE solvers. Unlike existing NGP-based approaches that rely on automatic differentiation or finite differences and suffer from instability or high cost, Hermite-NGP explicitly stores function values and mixed partial derivatives at hash grid vertices, allowing fully analytic evaluation of gradients, Jacobians, and Hessians via Hermite interpolation. This design preserves the efficiency and spatial adaptivity of NGP while supporting analytic differential operators up to second order. We further introduce a multi-resolution curriculum training strategy analogous to multigrid V-cycles to enable coarse-to-fine optimization. Across a range of 2D and 3D PDE benchmarks, Hermite-NGP achieves up to approximately 20 times lower error than prior neural PDE methods, and reduces wall-clock convergence time by 2 to 10 times compared to other solvers, with per-epoch training times as low as 3.5 ms for models with up to 17M parameters.

</details>


### [66] [Complement Submodular Information Measures for Balanced and Robust Data Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24779)
*Rishabh Iyer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Complement Submodular Information (CSI)，一类新的补集感知子模目标函数，通过量化子集与其补集间的共享结构信息，在保持覆盖、多样性和代表性的同时，显式保留子集与剩余数据间的结构平衡。理论证明CSI在有限曲率条件下具有近似单调性和近(1-1/e)贪婪近似保证，实验表明其在鲁棒隐藏切片感知子集选择中显著优于经典子模方法，尤其能更好地保留稀有/尾部语义结构并抑制噪声离群点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典子模优化仅优化所选子集，无法显式保留子集与剩余数据之间的结构信息。在许多现代机器学习应用（如训练/验证/测试集划分、基准构建、鲁棒子集选择）中，选择质量关键取决于所选子集及其补集之间的平衡结构保持。

Method: 提出Complement Submodular Information (CSI)框架，通过量化子集与其补集之间的共享结构信息，诱导多种经典子模函数（Facility Location、Graph Cut、LogDet、Saturated Coverage、Set Cover、Probabilistic Set Cover、Feature Based Functions）的补集感知变体。理论分析证明CSI目标在有限曲率条件下具有近似单调性，从而获得近(1-1/e)贪婪近似保证。

Result: CSI目标在鲁棒隐藏切片感知子集选择任务上持续优于标准子模目标。具体而言，CSI显著改善了相干稀有/尾部语义结构的保留，同时有效抑制噪声和孤立离群点，从而提升下游预测性能。合成实验展示了不同CSI实例如何捕获互补的表示性、多样性、连通性和平衡邻域保持概念。

Conclusion: 补集感知的子模优化是提升子集选择质量的有效范式，特别适用于需要平衡保留子集与补集结构信息的场景。CSI框架为经典子模函数提供了统一的理论扩展，并在实践中证明了其在鲁棒数据选择中的优势。

Abstract: Submodular optimization has become a fundamental paradigm for data selection, retrieval, summarization, and representation learning due to its ability to model coverage, diversity, and representativeness. However, classical submodular objectives optimize only the selected subset and do not explicitly preserve structural information between the selected subset and the remaining data. In many modern machine learning applications, including train/validation/test splitting, benchmark construction, and robust subset selection, the quality of a selection depends critically on preserving balanced structure across both the selected subset and its complement.
  In this work, we introduce Complement Submodular Information (CSI), a new class of complement-aware submodular objectives that quantify shared structural information between a subset and its complement. Our framework induces complement-aware variants of several classical submodular functions including Facility Location, Graph Cut, LogDet, Saturated Coverage, Set Cover, Probabilistic Set Cover, and Feature Based Functions. We analyze the theoretical properties of CSI objectives and show that they exhibit approximate monotonicity under bounded curvature conditions, leading to near-$(1-1/e)$ greedy approximation guarantees.
  Empirically, CSI objectives consistently outperform standard submodular objectives on robust hidden-slice-aware subset selection. In particular, CSI objectives significantly improve preservation of coherent rare/tail semantic structure while simultaneously suppressing noisy and isolated outliers, leading to substantially improved downstream predictive performance. Synthetic experiments further illustrate how different CSI instantiations capture complementary notions of representativeness, diversity, connectivity, and balanced neighborhood preservation.

</details>


### [67] [The Perception-Physics Paradox: Probing Scientific Alignment with TC-Bench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24782)
*Dingling Yao,Andrea Polesello,Adeel Pervez,Caroline Muller,Francesco Locatello*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出科学对齐（Scientific Alignment）概念，指出视觉基础模型（VFMs）在卫星图像上可能看起来正确但推理错误（感知-物理悖论），并通过结构同构（Structural Isomorphism）建立表征与物理系统的线性可重参数化对应关系。作者构建TC-Bench热带气旋基准数据集，证明当前VFMs依赖视觉捷径，在极端强度区域失效，科学对齐不会随模型规模扩大自然出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉基础模型在卫星图像预测任务上的表现可能源于视觉相关性而非底层结构不变性，导致感知层面的分布外准确率无法反映真实的科学效用。模型可能看起来正确但推理错误，这种感知-物理悖论亟需解决。

Method: 提出科学对齐作为科学领域表征学习的隐式目标；通过结构同构要求潜在表征在最多线性重参数化下唯一标识物理系统；推导必要条件层次体系，建立物理和因果可解释性的系统探针协议；构建TC-Bench基准数据集（含自动化构建流程）用于热带气旋研究。

Result: 当前VFMs在TC-Bench上依赖视觉捷径，在极端强度区域崩溃，表明科学对齐不会随模型规模扩大而自然涌现。

Conclusion: 需要将科学对齐作为显式目标纳入视觉基础模型的训练，仅靠规模扩展不足以使模型学会真正的物理推理。

Abstract: While Vision Foundation Models (VFMs) excel at predictive tasks on satellite imagery, their performance can arise from visual correlations rather than underlying structural invariants, making even perception-based out-of-distribution accuracy a poor proxy for scientific utility. As a result, models may look correct without reasoning correctly, a discrepancy we term the Perception-Physics Paradox. To address this gap, we introduce scientific alignment as an implicit objective for representation learning in scientific domains. We study a principled, testable aspect of scientific alignment through structural isomorphism, which requires latent representations to uniquely identify physical systems up to a linear reparameterization. This perspective induces a hierarchy of necessary conditions and yields a systematic probing protocol for physical and causal interpretability. To operationalize this framework, we release TC-Bench, a global, reproducible benchmark dataset with an automated construction pipeline for tropical cyclone research, and show that current VFMs rely on visual shortcuts that collapse in intense regimes, indicating that scientific alignment does not arise as a natural byproduct of scaling alone.

</details>


### [68] [CONF-KV: Confidence-Aware KV Cache Eviction with Mixed-Precision Storage for Long-Horizon LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24786)
*Yubo Li,Yidi Miao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CONF-KV利用模型解码时的当前不确定性(置信度)动态调整KV缓存预算,在模型不确定时保留更多上下文、确定时激进剪枝,结合累积注意力权重和时效性排序令牌,在保持接近固定512令牌滑动窗口内存占用的同时,困惑度仅差1.5-2.1点,长上下文检索准确率达91.4%,视觉任务保留95.3%成功率且峰值内存降低2.8倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文LLM推理中KV缓存成为主要GPU内存消耗,且逐令牌注意力计算日益昂贵。现有驱逐策略(如静态时效窗口或历史注意力)未利用解码步骤中可获得的模型当前不确定性信号,导致内存利用效率不高。

Method: 将下一个令牌预测分布转换为标量置信度分数,动态选择每步缓存预算(不确定时保留更多、确定时激进剪枝);在预算内采用累积注意力质量与时效性的复合排序;结合块级在线softmax注意力、混合FP16/INT8存储及逐层金字塔预算变体。

Result: 在四种模型家族和最长4K生成长度下,保持接近固定512令牌滑动窗口内存占用,困惑度仅差1.5-2.1点;32K长上下文检索准确率91.4%(vs 滑动窗口53.8%, H2O 80.6%);75个VisualWebArena任务保留全KV 95.3%成功率,峰值内存降低2.8倍。

Conclusion: CONF-KV通过利用模型不确定性信号动态调整缓存预算,在推理效率和长上下文性能之间取得了优于现有静态策略和基于历史注意力策略的平衡,证明置信感知的缓存管理是一种有效的KV缓存压缩方法。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM inference turns the key--value (KV) cache into the dominant GPU memory consumer and makes per-token attention increasingly expensive. Many common eviction policies use static recency windows or historical attention, leaving unused a signal computed on every decoding step: the model's current uncertainty. We introduce CONF-KV, a KV-cache manager that converts the next-token distribution into a scalar confidence score and uses it to choose the per-step cache budget, retaining more context when the model is uncertain and pruning aggressively when it is confident. Within each budget, tokens are ranked by a composite of accumulated attention mass and recency, while a protected recent window preserves local coherence. We combine the policy with blockwise online-softmax attention, mixed FP16/INT8 storage, and a pyramidal per-layer budget variant. Across four model families and generated lengths up to 4K, CONF-KV stays near the footprint of a fixed 512-token sliding window while remaining within 1.5--2.1 perplexity points of full KV. On Needle-in-a-Haystack up to 32K tokens, CONF-KV reaches 91.4% retrieval accuracy versus 53.8% for sliding windows and 80.6% for H2O; on 75 VisualWebArena tasks it retains 95.3% of full-KV success at 2.8 times lower peak memory.

</details>


### [69] [Active Learning for Stochastic Contextual Linear Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24803)
*Emma Brunskill,Ishani Karmarkar,Zhaoqi Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究随机上下文线性bandit问题，提出了一种主动上下文采样策略算法，通过战略性采样上下文-动作对的奖励来学习近似最优策略。理论证明该策略相比minimax速率最多可提升√d倍（d为线性维度），在warfarin剂量预测和笑话推荐等任务中实证减少了学习近似最优策略所需样本数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机上下文线性bandit算法在采样动作时具有策略性，但对上下文的采样是被动的，忽略了实践中可以主动采样或招募上下文的场景（如在线内容推荐、调查研究、临床试验）。本文旨在探索主动上下文采样在随机上下文线性bandit中的作用。

Method: 提出一种主动上下文采样策略算法，战略性采样上下文-动作对的奖励，利用上下文分布的先验知识来指导采样过程。

Result: 理论证明主动上下文采样策略相比minimax速率最多可提升√d倍，在warfarin剂量预测和笑话推荐任务中实证减少了学习近似最优策略所需的样本数。

Conclusion: 主动上下文采样能显著提升随机上下文线性bandit的学习效率，为实践中利用上下文先验知识提供了理论依据和有效算法。

Abstract: A key goal in stochastic contextual linear bandits is to efficiently learn a near-optimal policy. Prior algorithms for this problem learn a policy by strategically sampling actions but naively (passively) sampling contexts from the underlying context distribution. However, in many practical scenarios -- including online content recommendation, survey research, and clinical trials -- practitioners can actively sample or recruit contexts based on prior knowledge of the context distribution. Despite this potential for active learning, the role of strategic context sampling in stochastic contextual linear bandits is underexplored. We propose an algorithm that learns a near-optimal policy by strategically sampling rewards of context-action pairs. We prove instance-dependent theoretical guarantees demonstrating that our active context sampling strategy can improve over the minimax rate by up to a factor of $\sqrt{d}$, where $d$ is the linear dimension. We show empirically that our algorithm reduces the number of samples needed to learn a near-optimal policy, in tasks such as warfarin dose prediction and joke recommendation.

</details>


### [70] [Disentangled Double Machine Learning for Accurate Causal Effect Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24808)
*Guodu Xiang,Kui Yu,Yujie Wang,Richang Hong,Fuyuan Cao,Jiye Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对双机器学习（DML）在高维或有限样本下因混淆变量未解耦和残差依赖导致的偏差问题，提出解耦双机器学习（DDML），通过因果角色解耦策略和残差依赖正交化策略，显著提升因果效应估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DML在高维或有限样本场景下存在两个关键问题：(1) 使用全部协变量估计 nuisance 函数，未能解耦不同的潜在因子，导致 nuisance 函数估计不可靠；(2) 不精确的 nuisance 估计进一步引入治疗残差与剩余结果误差之间的依赖，削弱因果效应估计的准确性。

Method: 提出DDML算法，包含两项核心策略：(1) 因果角色解耦策略：将协变量解耦为混淆因子、治疗特异性因子和结果特异性因子，以实现更可靠的nuisance函数估计；(2) 残差依赖正交化策略：减轻由nuisance估计误差引起的残差依赖，提升因果效应估计精度。

Result: 在合成、半合成和真实数据集上，DDML在MAE和RMSE两项指标上显著优于13种最先进基线算法。

Conclusion: DDML通过解耦混淆变量和正交化残差依赖，有效解决了DML在高维和有限样本下的偏差和不稳定问题，显著提升了因果效应估计的精度和稳定性。

Abstract: Confounding bias is a key challenge in causal effect estimation from observational data. Double Machine Learning (DML) addresses this issue by estimating treatment and outcome nuisance functions, constructing treatment and outcome residuals, and estimating causal effects from the residuals. However, DML often produces biased and unstable estimates in highdimensional or finite-sample scenarios. One reason is that DML estimates nuisance functions using all covariates without disentangling distinct latent factors, resulting in unreliable nuisance function estimation. Another is that imprecise nuisance estimation further introduces residual dependence between the treatment residual and the remaining outcome error, undermining the accuracy of causal effect estimates. To address these issues, in this paper, we propose Disentangled Double Machine Learning (DDML), a novel algorithm that integrates two key strategies. First, a causal role disentanglement strategy decomposes covariates into confounders, treatment-specific factors, and outcomespecific factors for enabling reliable nuisance function estimation. And second, a residual dependence orthogonalization strategy mitigates residual dependence caused by nuisance estimation errors for enhancing the precision of causal effect estimates. Experimental results on synthetic, semi-synthetic, and real-world datasets demonstrate that DDML significantly outperforms 13 state-of-the-art baseline algorithms in both MAE and RMSE.

</details>


### [71] [Cross-Domain Energy-Guided Diffusion Generation for Off-Dynamics Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24810)
*Yu Yang,Yihong Guo,Anqi Liu,Pan Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CEDGE提出了一种跨域能量引导的轨迹扩散生成框架，通过在源域训练扩散模型并利用能量引导（包含回报、领域和行为三种能量分量）将生成轨迹适配到目标域，解决了离动态离线强化学习中轨迹级生成缺失的问题，在ODRL基准上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离动态离线强化学习方法受限于源数据集，无法合成新的目标行为来改善覆盖范围；基于模型的方法仅在转移级别生成经验，导致长程累积误差；亟需轨迹级别的生成方法。

Method: 在源域轨迹上训练扩散模型，通过能量引导（最小化源轨迹与目标轨迹分布不匹配）将生成样本适配到目标域，能量分解为回报、领域和行为三个分量；适配通过能量引导而非重训模型实现。

Result: 在ODRL基准上，轨迹级能量引导生成改善了动态偏移下的扩散规划性能，生成的合成数据提升了下游目标策略学习效果。

Conclusion: CEDGE通过能量引导实现高效的轨迹级跨域适配，避免了重训扩散模型，为离动态离线强化学习提供了新的有效范式。

Abstract: Off-dynamics offline reinforcement learning seeks to learn a target-domain policy from a large source dataset and a limited target dataset under mismatched transition dynamics. Existing approaches such as reward augmentation and data filtering are constrained to the source dataset and cannot synthesize new target behavior to improve coverage beyond the collected source trajectories. While recent model-based methods attempt to address this by learning target-aware dynamics, the generated experience is constructed only at the transition level, which leads to accumulated errors over long horizons. These limitations necessitate a shift toward trajectory-level generation for off-dynamics offline RL. We propose CEDGE, a Cross-domain Energy-guided Diffusion GEneration framework. CEDGE trains a trajectory diffusion model on source-domain trajectories and adapts the generated samples to the target domain through energy guidance. This guidance is derived by minimizing the distribution mismatch between the source and desired target-domain trajectories and is decomposed into return, domain, and behavior energy components. The resulting energy-guided trajectories are useful both for direct planning and as synthetic data for policy learning. Since target adaptation is achieved via energy guidance rather than retraining the diffusion model, CEDGE can be efficiently adapted to new target dynamics compared to previous methods. Experiments on the ODRL benchmark demonstrate that trajectory-level energy-guided generation improves diffusion planning under dynamics shifts and produces synthetic data that improves downstream target policy learning.

</details>


### [72] [DriftingMol: Decoder-Coupled Drift for One-Pass Property-Conditional Molecular Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24841)
*Jiangjie Qiu,Yijun Li,Wentao Li,Xiaonan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DriftingMol是一种两阶段分子生成框架，在SELFIES潜在空间上应用漂移模型。通过冻结的SELFIES beta-VAE提供潜在空间，并将解码器的隐藏表示作为漂移特征图。在解码器耦合漂移中，保持解码器权重固定，但将漂移梯度通过解码器特征图反向传播到DiT生成器，诱导出与分子解码对齐的拉回度量。在ZINC250K上，默认设置达到QED Spearman相关0.493，唯一性94.7%，最强解码器耦合条件达到0.510。四属性条件下平均Spearman相关最高达0.598。15个对照变体表明，保留通过解码器特征的梯度路径的模型优于潜在空间、随机特征和外部特征漂移变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现属性条件分子生成，要求同时满足有效性、多样性、对连续目标值的响应性以及低采样成本。

Method: 提出DriftingMol两阶段框架：第一阶段使用冻结的SELFIES beta-VAE构建潜在空间；第二阶段在解码器耦合漂移中，保持解码器权重固定，将漂移梯度通过解码器特征图反向传播到DiT生成器，诱导拉回度量。

Result: 在ZINC250K上，默认QED Spearman相关0.493，唯一性94.7%；最强解码器耦合条件达0.510；四属性条件下平均Spearman相关最高0.598。保留梯度路径的模型显著优于对照变体。

Conclusion: 解码器耦合漂移是一种低成本、有效的属性偏置分子生成机制，仅需一次生成器评估和一次冻结解码器前向传播。

Abstract: Property-conditional molecular generation should produce valid, diverse molecules while responding to continuous target values at low sampling cost. We introduce DriftingMol, a two-stage framework that adapts drifting models to a SELFIES latent molecular space. A frozen SELFIES beta-VAE provides the latent space, and the hidden representation of its decoder serves as the drift feature map. In decoder-coupled drift, decoder weights remain fixed, but drift gradients are backpropagated through the decoder feature map to a DiT generator, inducing a pullback metric aligned with molecular decoding. On ZINC250K, the default setting achieves QED Spearman correlation 0.493 with 94.7% uniqueness, while the strongest decoder-coupled condition reaches 0.510. Under protocol-matched four-property conditioning, decoder-coupled drift reaches mean Spearman correlation up to 0.598. Across 15 controlled variants, models that preserve the gradient path through decoder features achieve higher correlations than the tested latent-space, random-feature, and external-feature drift variants, while detached or stop-gradient decoder controls yield near-zero QED correlation and very low uniqueness. These results indicate that decoder-coupled drift is a useful low-cost mechanism for property-biased molecular generation, requiring one generator evaluation and one frozen decoder pass.

</details>


### [73] [Tiny Brains, Giant Impact: Uncovering the Keystone Neurons of LLM with Just a Few Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24846)
*Xiangtian Ji,Yuxin Chen,Zhengzhou Cai,Xiang Wang,An Zhang,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现Transformer模型中存在一类跨任务持续高度激活的“基石神经元”（keystone neurons），其移除会导致模型能力崩溃。这些神经元在预训练阶段建立，参数校准严格，基于此提出的仅更新基石神经元的微调方法在性能上可媲美甚至超越全参数微调，同时更好保留其他能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型虽然展现出强大的综合能力，但其内部机制尚不明确，本文旨在揭示模型内部关键神经元的特性及其对模型能力的影响。

Method: 通过跨多个能力维度任务的激活强度分析，从开放权重的Transformer模型中分离出一组稀疏的、跨任务持续高度激活的神经元子集（基石神经元），并通过移除实验验证其关键性；进而提出仅更新这些神经元的监督微调方法。

Result: 移除基石神经元会导致模型行为崩溃；基石神经元在预训练阶段建立，其参数在训练过程中被严格校准；仅更新基石神经元的微调方法在目标任务上可比拟甚至超越全参数微调，同时更好地保留其他能力维度的性能。

Conclusion: 基石神经元是模型中稳定且本质的神经元子集，对模型能力至关重要；基于这一发现，稀疏参数更新策略可兼顾微调效果与能力保留。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) display strong comprehensive abilities, yet the internal mechanisms that support these behaviors remain insufficiently understood. In this work, we show that across a wide range of open-weight Transformers, a subset of neurons remains consistently highly activated during inference across tasks of multiple capability dimensions. By probing along the cross-task activation strength, an extremely sparse subset is isolated, whose removal causes a collapse in model behavior, which we term keystone neurons. Our analysis reveals that keystone neurons are a stable and intrinsic neuron subset of the model that is largely established during pretraining. The parameters associated with these neurons are tightly calibrated during the training process, and their precise values are critical for the capabilities of the model. Building on these insights, we propose a supervised fine-tuning approach that updates only keystone neurons, achieving task gains comparable to or even better than full-parameter fine-tuning while better preserving performance in other capability dimensions, despite modifying a much smaller number of parameters.

</details>


### [74] [T2S-MPC: Time-Embedded Online Adaptive Model Predictive Control for Time-Varying Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24852)
*Zeyu Shen,Zhuoyuan Wang,Laixi Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出T2S-MPC框架，通过在线学习残差动力学模型并集成到MPC中，结合时间嵌入和双时间尺度更新策略，有效处理未知、非平稳的时变系统动力学，在四旋翼飞行器任务中优于经典和神经MPC方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的MPC方法主要处理特定或结构化的动力学变化，对更一般、未知且不可预测的时变动力学处理不足，需要一种能在线适应非平稳动态的方法。

Method: 提出T2S-MPC框架：(1) 在线学习残差动力学模型并与标称模型集成于MPC中；(2) 通过结构化时间嵌入显式编码时序信息使模型具有时间感知能力；(3) 采用双时间尺度更新机制，在快速适应与稳定学习之间取得平衡。

Result: 在2D四旋翼飞行器的稳定化和轨迹跟踪任务中，面对线性漂移和周期性扰动等多种时变干扰，T2S-MPC在控制性能上持续优于经典MPC、神经MPC及其消融变体，并在广泛扰动条件下展现出强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: T2S-MPC通过时间感知的残差学习与双时间尺度更新，有效应对未知非平稳动力学，无需额外调参即可适应多种时变扰动，为在线自适应MPC提供了通用有效的框架。

Abstract: Recent advances in learning-based model predictive control (MPC) have leveraged neural networks for online model learning, achieving strong performance when nonstationary system dynamics deviate from nominal models. However, existing approaches primarily address specific or relatively structured forms of dynamical variation, leaving more general, unknown, and unpredictable time-varying dynamics insufficiently handled. To tackle this challenge, we propose T2S-MPC, a framework that adaptively learns a residual dynamics model online and integrates it with the nominal model within the MPC framework to enable fast-evolving online planning. To make the model time-aware, we explicitly encode temporal information through a structured time embedding and employ a two-timescale update scheme, allowing the controller to capture nonstationary dynamics while balancing rapid adaptation with stable learning.
