We evaluate the proposed method on a 2D quadrotor across stabilization and trajectory tracking tasks under diverse time-varying disturbances, including linear drifting and periodic perturbations. Experimental results show that T2S-MPC consistently outperforms classical MPC, neural MPC, and ablated variants in control performance, while also demonstrating strong robustness across a wide range of disturbance conditions without additional tuning. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/Zeyuu0920/T2S_MPC

</details>


### [75] [The Concept Allocation Zone: Tracking How Concepts Form Across Transformer Depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24856)
*James Henry*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出概念分配区（CAZ）：Transformer语言模型中概念在残差流中跨连续深度区间逐步形成，而非单一层事件。通过三项逐层指标（分离度、概念一致性、概念速度）形式化定义CAZ，并在34个模型上验证发现分离曲线S(l)常呈多模态，引入"温和CAZ"检测器，93-100%因果活跃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释性方法仅识别概念分离度峰值层（"最佳层"），捕捉的是快照而非概念形成的连续过程，缺乏对概念在深度维度上几何表达形成区间的形式化刻画。

Method: 定义概念分配区（CAZ）为概念可测量分离的深度区间；提出三项逐层指标：分离度（Separation）、概念一致性（Concept Coherence）、概念速度（Concept Velocity）；设计基于评分的检测器识别标准峰值检测无法发现的"温和CAZ"；在34个模型、8个架构家族、7个概念上进行验证，并通过留一概念交叉验证检验跨架构对齐。

Result: 分离曲线S(l)频现多模态；"温和CAZ"在93-100%消融实验中因果活跃；七个可检验预测中四个得出明确结论（两个不支持、一个部分支持、一个支持），一个前提被数据否定，两个统计效力不足；跨架构对齐被证实为深度匹配而非整体对齐。

Conclusion: 概念形成是深度扩展的过程，CAZ框架比单层快照方法更准确地刻画了这一几何组织结构，提供了可检验的预测和跨模型比较工具。

Abstract: Concept formation in transformer language models is depth-extended, not a single-layer event: concepts emerge gradually across a contiguous region of the residual stream. Mechanistic interpretability methods identify the single layer of peak class separation -- the "best layer" -- capturing a snapshot rather than the process itself. We introduce the Concept Allocation Zone (CAZ): the depth interval within which a concept becomes measurably separable, the region allocated to its geometric expression. We formalize the CAZ through three layer-wise metrics (Separation, Concept Coherence, Concept Velocity) and derive principled boundary detection without manual layer sweeps. A CAZ is not a concept: it is the depth region within which the model organizes its geometry to make a concept separable. A single concept typically participates in multiple CAZes; multiple concepts may share one. Empirical validation across 34 models from 8 architectural families and 7 concepts reveals that the separation curve S(l) is frequently multimodal. A scored detector uncovers "gentle CAZes" -- subtle allocation regions invisible to standard peak detection but causally active in 93-100% of cases under ablation (16 of 34 models; 26 in the companion validation paper). The framework generates seven testable predictions; four yield clear verdicts (two not supported, one partially supported, one supported), one had its precondition invalidated by the data, and two are underpowered -- with cross-architecture alignment confirmed as depth-matched rather than monolithic under leave-one-concept-out cross-validation. Reference implementation: rosetta_tools v1.3.1 (doi:10.5281/zenodo.20361433).

</details>


### [76] [Unifying Value Alignment and Assignment in Cross-Domain Offline Reinforcement Learning with Heterogeneous Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24862)
*Zhongjian Qiao,Jiafei Lyu,Chenjia Bai,Peisong Wang,Siyang Gao,Shuang Qiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出V2A方法，针对异构跨域离线强化学习中存在的值分配错误问题，通过整合动态对齐、值对齐和值分配，在多个源域和多样化行为策略的通用设置下显著优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的跨域离线强化学习方法通常在单域或单一行为策略的源数据集上验证，忽略了更通用的异构跨域设置（源数据来自多个域和多种行为策略）。作者发现该设置下存在关键的值分配错误问题，该问题会破坏值对齐、误导数据过滤选择次优样本，从而降低智能体性能。

Method: 提出V2A框架：(1) 时序一致的多模态表示学习，从源数据集中提取动态模态；(2) 模态感知的优势学习，纠正值对齐；(3) 数据过滤范式，选择性共享源数据进行策略学习。

Result: 在通用异构跨域离线RL设置下，V2A显著优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: 值分配错误是异构跨域离线RL中一个关键但被忽视的问题，V2A通过整合动态对齐、值对齐和值分配有效解决了该问题。

Abstract: Cross-domain offline reinforcement learning (RL) aims to learn a policy in the target domain with a limited target domain dataset and a source domain dataset that exhibits a dynamics shift. Training directly on the original source dataset typically leads to performance collapse. Recent studies perform data filtering from the perspective of dynamics alignment or value alignment to enable efficient policy transfer. However, these studies are typically validated on single-domain or single-behavior-policy source datasets. In this work, we explore a more general heterogeneous cross-domain offline RL setting, where the source datasets may be collected from multiple source domains by diverse behavior policies. We first uncover a critical yet overlooked issue in this setting: value misassignment. Empirically and theoretically, we demonstrate that value misassignment can undermine value alignment, mislead data filtering toward selecting suboptimal samples, and loosen the suboptimality gap, thereby degrading the agent's performance. To address this issue, we propose V2A, which integrates dynamics alignment, value alignment, and value assignment. V2A first employs temporally-consistent modality representation learning to extract dynamics modalities from the source dataset, followed by modality-aware advantage learning to rectify value alignment. Finally, it adopts a data filtering paradigm to selectively share source data for policy learning. Empirical results show that V2A significantly outperforms strong baseline methods under general heterogeneous cross-domain offline RL settings.

</details>


### [77] [Cluster Frequency Conformal Prediction for Local Coverage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24872)
*Tomer Lavi,Bracha Shapira,Nadav Rappoport*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Cluster Frequency Conformal Prediction (CFCP)框架，通过聚类表示空间中的嵌入并利用聚类级别标签频率分布，为多类分类中的共形预测提供局部自适应，在15/16数据集中实现最佳类别覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准共形预测在多类分类中对特定类别或子群体覆盖不足，阻碍了在高风险场景中的安全部署。

Method: CFCP框架：1) 对学习到的嵌入进行聚类；2) 从校准数据估计聚类级别的标签频率分布；3) 对每个测试点，通过软混合邻近聚类分布（结合全局先验和可靠性感知收缩）构建样本特定的概率向量；4) 使用标准集构造器进行共形化。

Result: 在图像和文本基准上，CFCP在15/16个数据集/得分族比较中实现了最佳类别覆盖率，预测集大小效率具有竞争力，多个设置显著更高效。

Conclusion: 聚类频率信息为改善多类共形预测中的类别可靠性提供了有效的局部信号。

Abstract: Conformal prediction provides distribution-free coverage guarantees, but in many-class classification it may still under-cover specific classes or subpopulations, preventing safe deployment in high-stakes applications. We propose Cluster Frequency Conformal Prediction (CFCP), a plug-in framework that adapts conformal prediction to local structure in a learned representation space. CFCP clusters learned embeddings, estimates cluster-level label-frequency distributions from calibration data, and for each test point constructs a sample-specific probability vector by softly mixing nearby cluster distributions regularized with global-prior and reliability-aware shrinkage. This vector is then conformalized using standard set constructors. In the disjoint-split regime, CFCP inherits standard finite-sample marginal validity. Under additional assumptions, CFCP further admits a local-validity interpretation. Since representation clusters aggregate locally similar samples, their empirical class frequencies provide a stable estimate of local label ambiguity. Across image and text benchmarks, CFCP achieves the best class coverage in 15/16 dataset/score-family comparisons and a competitive prediction set size efficiency, with several settings substantially more efficient. Overall, our results show that cluster-frequency information provides an effective localized signal for improving classwise reliability in many-class conformal prediction.

</details>


### [78] [Efficient DP-SGD for LLMs with Randomized Clipping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24879)
*Enayat Ullah,Sai Aparna Aketi,Devansh Gupta,Huanyu Zhang,Meisam Razaviyayn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DP-SGD-RC，一种带随机裁剪的DP-SGD变体，利用随机迹估计方法（Hutchinson估计器和Hutch++）降低每样本梯度范数估计的内存开销，在长上下文微调任务中实现与确定性裁剪相当的效用，同时显著减少内存和计算需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DP训练实现依赖快速梯度裁剪技术，其内存开销为O(B min{T², d²})，随着模型规模和上下文长度增长，该开销变得难以承受。

Method: 提出DP-SGD-RC变体，使用随机裁剪替代确定性裁剪，采用Hutchinson估计器及其改进变体Hutch++进行随机迹估计，以降低每样本梯度范数估计的内存和计算复杂度。

Result: 在Llama 3.2-1B上对长上下文基准（分类、问答、摘要）进行微调实验，结果表明DP-SGD-RC在匹配基线效用的同时显著减少了内存和计算需求。

Conclusion: DP-SGD-RC通过随机裁剪与迹估计方法有效解决了DP-SGD在大模型长上下文场景下的内存瓶颈问题，且隐私分析表明其噪声倍数可与确定性裁剪竞争。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are trained on vast datasets that may contain sensitive information. Differential privacy (DP), the de facto standard for formal privacy guarantees, provides a principled framework for training LLMs with provable privacy protection. However, state-of-the-art DP training implementations rely on fast gradient clipping techniques with memory overhead $O(B \min\{T^2, d^2\})$, where $B$ is the batch size, $T$ is the sequence length, and $d$ is the model width. This becomes prohibitive as both model size and context length grow. We propose DP-SGD-RC, a novel variant of DP-SGD with randomized clipping that reduces memory and compute complexity. DP-SGD-RC leverages stochastic trace estimation methods, specifically Hutchinson's estimator[Hutchinson, 1989] and its improved variant, Hutch++[Meyer et al., 2021], to reduce the memory footprint of per-sample gradient norm estimation. We provide a tight privacy analysis showing that DP-SGD-RC achieves noise multipliers competitive with deterministic clipping. Experiments fine-tuning Llama~3.2-1B on long-context benchmarks spanning classification, question answering, and summarization tasks demonstrate that DP-SGD-RC matches baseline utility while significantly reducing memory and compute requirements.

</details>


### [79] [Factorize to Generalize: Retrieval-Guided Invariant-Dynamic Decomposition for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24911)
*Jinjin Chi,Lei Feng,Lulu Zhang,Yongcheng Jing,Yiming Wang,Ximing Li,Jialie Shen,Leszek Rutkowski,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出检索引导的时不变-动态分解框架（RIDDE），通过将检索序列视为隐式环境样本来分解时不变与动态成分，在零样本时间序列预测中提升分布偏移鲁棒性，优于现有基础模型与检索基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索增强时间序列预测存在一个关键局限：检索会引入更多振荡性预测，在波动大的序列上表现好，但在平滑、趋势主导的序列上精度下降，说明检索信息融合时未区分稳定的时间结构与实例特异性变化，导致分布偏移下鲁棒性不足。

Method: 提出RIDDE框架：1）通过注意力聚合构建检索感知表示；2）设计检索引导路由机制，将表示分解为捕捉稳定共享结构的时不变成分和建模上下文相关变化的动态成分；3）分别预测两成分后融合得到最终预测；4）设计促进不变学习与解耦的训练目标；5）理论证明检索聚合可降低方差并在无显式环境监督下近似不变表示学习。

Result: 实验表明该方法在分布偏移下鲁棒性一致提升，在零样本预测设置中优于现有TSFMs和检索基方法。

Conclusion: 通过检索引导的显式时空分解，可有效平衡检索增强的预测灵活性与跨环境泛化能力，为构建更鲁棒的零样本时间序列预测模型提供了新范式。

Abstract: Time series foundation models (TSFMs) have recently achieved strong zero-shot forecasting performance through large-scale pretraining and retrieval-augmented prediction. However, our empirical analysis reveals a non-trivial limitation of retrieval-based forecasting: retrieval tends to induce more oscillatory predictions, improving performance on highly fluctuating series while degrading accuracy on smoother, trend-dominated ones. This suggests that retrieved information may be fused into prediction without explicitly distinguishing stable temporal structure from instance-specific variations, which can reduce robustness under distribution shifts. We propose a Retrieval-guided Invariant-Dynamic DEcomposition framework for time series forecasting. Rather than using retrieval as auxiliary predictive context, we leverage retrieved sequences as implicit samples from related environments to guide representation decomposition. Specifically, we first construct a retrieval-aware representation via attention-based aggregation, and then introduce a retrieval-guided routing mechanism to decompose it into an invariant component capturing stable shared structure and a dynamic component modeling context-dependent variations. These two components are forecast separately and fused for final prediction, enabling the model to preserve transferable patterns while remaining adaptive to evolving dynamics. We further design training objectives that encourage invariant learning and disentanglement, and provide theoretical insight showing that retrieval aggregation reduces variance and approximates invariant representation learning without explicit environment supervision. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method consistently improves robustness under distribution shifts and outperforms existing TSFMs and retrieval-based baselines in zero-shot forecasting settings.

</details>


### [80] [Explainable Retinal Imaging for Prediction of Multi-Organ Dysfunction in Type 2 Diabetes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24912)
*Mini Han Wang,Liting Huang,Wei Hong,Boonthawan Wingwon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究基于1,195名患者的常规实验室生物标志物，构建了系统级异常指数来量化T2DM的多系统功能障碍，并运用可解释机器学习模型（梯度提升模型AUC=1.000）实现了精准预测，揭示了高血糖、肾功能损伤、血脂异常和炎症是多系统风险的主要驱动因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: T2DM日益被认为是一种涉及代谢、肾脏、血脂和炎症等多系统协调功能障碍的系统性疾病，但现有临床评估往往未能捕捉这种多维度疾病负担，因此需要一种能够量化系统性负担的数据驱动方法。

Method: 对1,195名患者进行回顾性研究，利用常规收集的实验室生物标志物构建系统级异常指数以量化器官特异性功能障碍，将两个及以上系统异常定义为多系统受累；采用逻辑回归、随机森林和梯度提升三种监督学习模型进行训练，并使用SHAP进行模型可解释性分析。

Result: 梯度提升模型表现接近完美（AUC=1.000），显著优于逻辑回归（AUC=0.925）；特征归因分析显示高血糖、肾功能损伤、血脂异常和炎症是多系统风险的主要驱动因素；部分依赖分析中的剂量-反应关系进一步支持了模型预测的生物学合理性。

Conclusion: 本研究提出了一个可解释的、数据驱动的框架来量化T2DM的系统性疾病负担，通过将常规生物标志物与多器官功能障碍联系起来，兼具预测准确性和机制洞察力，有望改善糖尿病护理中的风险分层和精准医疗。

Abstract: Background: Type 2 diabetes mellitus (T2DM) is increasingly recognised as a systemic disease characterised by coordinated dysfunction across metabolic, renal, lipid, and inflammatory pathways. Existing clinical assessments often fail to capture this multi-dimensional burden.
  Methods: We conducted a retrospective study of 1,195 patients using routinely collected laboratory biomarkers. System-level abnormality indices were constructed to quantify organ-specific dysfunction, and multi-system involvement was defined as abnormalities in two or more systems. Supervised machine learning models, including logistic regression, random forest, and gradient boosting, were trained to predict multi-system dysregulation. Model interpretability was achieved using SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP).
  Results: The gradient boosting model demonstrated near-perfect discrimination (AUC = 1.000), significantly outperforming logistic regression (AUC = 0.925). Feature attribution analysis revealed that hyperglycaemia, renal impairment, dyslipidaemia, and inflammation were the dominant drivers of multi-system risk. Dose-response relationships observed in partial dependence analyses further supported the biological plausibility of model predictions.
  Conclusion: This study presents an interpretable, data-driven framework for quantifying systemic disease burden in T2DM. By linking routine biomarkers to multi-organ dysfunction, our approach provides both predictive accuracy and mechanistic insight, offering potential for improved risk stratification and precision medicine in diabetes care.
  The data and code used in this study are openly available on GitHub at: https://github.com/MiniHanWang/Type-2-Diabetes-1.git

</details>


### [81] [Quaternion Self-Attention with Shared Scores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24920)
*Shogo Yamauchi,Tohru Nitta,Hideaki Tamori*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种共享得分四元数自注意力机制，使用四元数内积计算单个实值得分并在所有分量上共享注意力分布，相比分量级注意力减少了75%的得分计算乘法和75%的softmax操作，在语音增强任务中GPU推理时间降低44.3%、CPU降低58.1%，且在视觉和NLP任务中保持性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有四元数自注意力机制对每个分量独立计算得分并独立应用softmax，导致计算成本高且注意力分布在不同分量间可能发散，需要更高效且统一的注意力机制。

Method: 提出共享得分四元数自注意力机制：使用四元数内积计算单个实值得分，在所有四元数分量上共享同一注意力分布；理论证明了当查询和键由诱导分量预混合的四元数线性投影产生时，分量级得分与共享得分位于同一交互子空间中。

Result: 得分计算乘法减少75%，softmax操作从4次降至1次；语音增强任务中GPU推理时间减少44.3%，CPU减少58.1%；在视觉和NLP任务中保持一致的性能趋势。

Conclusion: 共享得分机制在显著提升计算效率的同时保持模型质量，且理论分析表明分量级注意力并未扩展特征交互空间，本质上是对相同交互的重参数化。

Abstract: Quaternion neural networks are parameter-efficient and model multidimensional dependencies by representing four related features as a single entity. However, existing quaternion self-attention computes component-wise scores and applies independent softmax operations to each component, which increases the computational cost and allows attention distributions to diverge across components. We propose a shared-score quaternion self-attention mechanism that computes a single real-valued score using the quaternion inner product and applies a shared attention distribution across all components. This reduces score-computation multiplications by 75% and the number of softmax operations from four to one. We prove that, when queries and keys are produced by quaternion linear projections that induce component pre-mixing, the component-wise and shared scores lie in the same interaction subspace, indicating that independent component-wise attention primarily re-parameterizes the same interactions rather than expanding the feature interaction space. In speech enhancement, our method reduces inference time by up to 44.3% on a GPU and 58.1% on a CPU while maintaining quality, with consistent trends across vision and natural language processing.

</details>


### [82] [BandVQ: Band-Wise Vector-Quantized EEG Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24921)
*Jamiyan Sukhbaatar,Satoshi Imamura,Toshihisa Tanaka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BandVQ，一种基于频带向量量化的EEG基础模型，将EEG分解为五个频带，分别训练VQ-VAE分词器，再通过共享Transformer编码器对离散码索引进行预训练，在71个公共EEG语料库上预训练后，在六个分类任务上取得了领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG基础模型中的掩码建模方法通常使用宽带连续片段或单一离散表示，难以充分表征不同频带特有的神经活动模式，亟需一种能保留频率特异性信息的跨任务、跨记录可迁移表示学习方法。

Method: 1) 将EEG信号分解为delta、theta、alpha、beta、gamma五个频带；2) 为每个频带独立训练VQ-VAE分词器，生成离散VQ码索引；3) 使用共享Transformer编码器对掩码码索引、量化绝对对数功率、通道/时间嵌入及元数据前缀（参考、频带、任务族、阶段）进行预训练；4) 引入基于区域的掩码策略以减少空间相邻电极的平凡重建。

Result: 在71个公共语料库（>9,200名被试，357,000单通道小时）上预训练，在六个被试独立分类数据集上评估：三个认知任务取得最高报告结果，三个运动想象任务取得具有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 频带级向量量化策略能有效提升EEG基础模型的跨任务迁移能力，尤其是在认知任务上达到当前最优。

Abstract: A central challenge in electroencephalography (EEG) foundation modeling is learning transferable representations across recordings with diverse tasks, montages, references, and spectral characteristics. Existing masked modeling approaches often rely on broadband continuous patches or a single discrete representation, which may underrepresent frequency-specific activity. This paper proposes BandVQ, a band-wise vector-quantized EEG foundation model that decomposes EEG into delta, theta, alpha, beta, and gamma bands, trains an independent VQ-VAE tokenizer for each band, and pretrains a shared Transformer encoder on the resulting discrete VQ code indices. The encoder uses masked code tokens, quantized absolute log-power tokens, channel and temporal embeddings, and metadata prefix tokens representing reference, band, task family, and phase. Region-based masking is also introduced to reduce the trivial reconstruction of spatially adjacent electrodes. The model is pretrained on 71 public EEG corpora comprising over 9,200 subjects and 357,000 single-channel hours and evaluated on six subject-independent classification datasets. Under the current evaluation setting, the proposed model achieves strong transfer performance, with the highest reported results on three cognitive tasks and competitive performance on three motor imagery tasks.

</details>


### [83] [Global linear convergence of entropy-regularized softmax policy gradient beyond tabular MDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24939)
*Ziyue Chen,David Šiška,Lukasz Szpruch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了无限时域熵正则化马尔可夫决策过程（MDPs）中策略梯度方法的全局收敛性，在连续状态和动作空间下，采用log-linear softmax策略与线性函数近似。通过建立非均匀Polyak--Łojasiewicz不等式，并在两种特征机制（全仿射跨度和单纯形值特征）下证明非均匀常数有界，实现了目标的全局线性收敛率$\mathcal{O}(e^{-Ct})$。该工作将熵正则化softmax策略梯度的全局收敛理论从表格设置扩展到了函数近似设置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将熵正则化softmax策略梯度的全局收敛理论从离散表格设置推广到更具实际意义的连续状态-动作空间，并引入线性函数近似以处理大规模或连续问题。

Method: 采用log-linear softmax策略类，利用线性函数近似；建立非均匀PŁ不等式，并通过分析Fisher信息矩阵和未中心化特征协方差矩阵的最小特征值下界，在两种特征机制下证明常数的有界性。

Result: 在全仿射跨度和单纯形值两种特征机制下，梯度流沿正则化目标实现全局线性收敛，次优性衰减为$\mathcal{O}(e^{-Ct})$。

Conclusion: 本文成功将熵正则化softmax策略梯度的全局收敛理论扩展至带线性函数近似的连续状态-动作空间场景，为更复杂实际问题的理论保证奠定了基础。

Abstract: We study the global convergence of policy gradient for infinite-horizon entropy-regularized Markov decision processes (MDPs) with continuous state and action spaces. We consider log-linear softmax policies with linear function approximation, which extend the tabular softmax parameterization while retaining a tractable policy class. Under $Q^π_τ$-realizability for the regularized state-action value function, we first establish a non-uniform Polyak--Łojasiewicz (PŁ) inequality. The non-uniformity arises through degeneracy of constants associated with the policy geometry, namely the Fisher information matrix or an uncentered feature covariance matrix. We then identify two feature regimes under which this non-uniform constant can be bounded along the gradient flow. For full-affine-span features, we prove radial unboundedness of the KL regularizer and show that the smallest eigenvalue of the Fisher information matrix remains bounded below by an initialization-dependent positive constant. For simplex-valued features, we prove an analogous radial unboundedness result in the subspace orthogonal to the all-ones vector and obtain a uniform lower bound for the smallest eigenvalue of the uncentered covariance matrix. These results imply global linear convergence of the regularized objective along the gradient flow, i.e. suboptimality decaying as $\mathcal{O}(e^{-Ct})$ for some $C>0$. Our analysis extends the global convergence theory of entropy-regularized softmax policy gradient beyond the tabular setting of Agarwal et al. (2020); Bhandari and Russo (2024); Mei et al. (2020).

</details>


### [84] [Riemannian-Manifold Steering: Geometry-Aware Generative Autoencoders for Label-Free Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24942)
*Narmeen Oozeer,Shivam Raval,Philip Quirke,Manikandan Ravikiran,Jeff Phillips,Shriyash Upadhyay,Amirali Abdullah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出黎曼流形框架统一了语言模型中的流形操控方法，通过输出空间Hellinger距离拉回到激活空间作为度量，并利用概念-标记模式学习编码器进行无标签、无拓扑先验、无任务拟合的操控，在算术基准任务上有效驱动模型到达目标类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流形操控方法需要带标签的类中心以及预设的循环或顺序结构，这些假设限制了其应用范围。作者希望构建一个更通用的框架，无需标签中心、无需边界条件预设，统一现有的线性与非线性操控方法。

Method: 将流形操控重新表述为激活空间上的黎曼测地线计算，将线性和标记样条操控视为特定度量下的测地线；提出输出空间Hellinger距离拉回到激活空间作为原则性度量，通过训练一个编码器在小规模概念-标记模式上学习输出距离来近似该度量，无需每提示标签、拓扑先验或逐任务曲线拟合。

Result: 在标准四任务语言模型算术基准上，该方法在所有任务中均能可靠地将模型驱动到目标类，且在较小输出空间上比基线方法遵循更符合行为自然的轨迹。

Conclusion: 提供了一个统一的黎曼流形操控框架，并给出了基于模式监督、免标签的实例化方法，无需标记类中心或预设边界条件，拓展了流形操控的适用范围。

Abstract: Steering a language model - intervening on its internal activations to change downstream behaviour - has recently expanded beyond linear interpolation to nonlinear methods such as angular and kernelized steering, which define intervention transformations without learning an explicit geometry over paths in activation space. Freshly introduced geometry-aware manifold methods do learn such a geometry, but require labelled class centroids together with prescribed cyclic or sequential structure. These assumptions restrict where manifold steering can be applied, since existing constructions require labelled centroids and compatible boundary conditions. We recast manifold steering more broadly as \textbf{Riemannian geodesic computation} on activation space, recovering linear and labelled-spline steering as geodesics under particular choices of metric. A principled metric within this framework is the output-space Hellinger distance pulled back to activations; we approximate this with a learned encoder trained on output distances over a small concept-token schema - no per-prompt labels, no topology prior, and no per-task curve fitting. Empirically, the method reliably drives the model onto the target class across all tasks in a standard four-task language-model arithmetic benchmark, while following more behaviourally natural trajectories than baselines on smaller output spaces. We thereby provide a unified Riemannian framework for manifold steering together with a schema-supervised, label-free instantiation that operates without labelled centroids or prescribed boundary conditions.

</details>


### [85] [RealBench: Benchmarking Data-Driven Numerical Weather Forecasting Under Operational Conditions and Extreme Event Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24945)
*Ruize Li,Zhibin Wen,Tao Han,Hao Chen,Fenghua Ling,Wei Zhang,Song Guo,Lei Bai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RealBench——一个面向AI天气预报的下一代基准测试，通过使用2025年后的严格无分布外测试集、低延迟业务分析数据和全球超1万个站点的实地观测数据，在业务运行条件下评估模型。实验表明，基于再分析产品（如ERA5）的传统基准与真实世界表现存在显著差异，尤其在极端天气事件评估中更为突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有天气预报模型基准过度依赖再分析产品（如ERA5），这些产品通过延迟数据同化生成，无法反映实时业务预报的约束条件，导致基准性能与真实预报之间存在系统性偏差。

Method: RealBench采用三个关键设计：1）2025年后的严格无分布外测试集以消除数据泄漏；2）融合低延迟业务分析数据和全球超10,000个站点的地面观测数据；3）针对热浪、寒潮、热带气旋等极端事件提供事件特定评估指标。

Result: 评估结果显示，基于再分析的指标与真实世界性能之间存在显著差异，在极端事件评估上差异尤为突出，证明了现有基准的局限性。

Conclusion: RealBench建立了更忠实于业务需求的评估范式，为下一代AI天气预报系统提供了严谨的评估基础，推动了天气预报评估从再分析导向向运行导向转变。

Abstract: Accurate evaluation of weather forecasting models is critical for their reliable deployment in real-world applications. However, existing benchmarks predominantly rely on reanalysis products such as ERA5, which are generated through delayed data assimilation and do not reflect the constraints of real-time operational forecasting, thereby resulting in a systematic mismatch between benchmark performance and real-world forecasting. In this work, we introduce RealBench, a next-generation benchmark for AI weather forecasting that emphasizes realistic evaluation under operational conditions. RealBench features a strictly out-of-distribution test set spanning 2025 to eliminate data leakage and capture recent atmospheric regimes. It integrates multiple data sources, including low-latency operational analysis and a large-scale global in-situ observation dataset comprising over 10,000 stations, enabling direct evaluation against real atmospheric measurements. Beyond standard global metrics, RealBench provides a comprehensive evaluation framework for high-impact extreme events, including heatwaves, cold surges, and tropical cyclones, using event-specific metrics that better reflect real-world forecasting priorities. The evaluation results reveal substantial discrepancies between reanalysis-based metrics and real-world performance, particularly concerning extreme events. By highlighting the limitations of existing benchmarks, this work establishes a more faithful and operationally relevant evaluation paradigm, providing a rigorous foundation for advancing next-generation AI weather forecasting systems. The benchmark implementation is available at: https://github.com/lixruize-del/NWP-Benchmark.

</details>


### [86] [MedMamba: Multi-View State Space Models with Adaptive Graph Learning for Medical Time Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24961)
*Da Zhang,Bingyu Li,Zhiyuan Zhao,Hongyuan Zhang,Junyu Gao,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MedMamba是一个结合状态空间模型与领域特定归纳偏置的端到端架构，通过多尺度卷积嵌入、三分支差分状态空间编码器和空间图Mamba模块，分别解决局部-全局动态建模、非平稳性处理和潜在通道交互捕获问题，在五个医疗时序数据集上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以联合建模医疗时序数据的局部-全局动态，无法有效处理基线漂移等非平稳性问题，且常忽视潜在通道间的交互关系。

Method: 1) 多尺度卷积嵌入模块捕获判别性局部形态；2) 三分支差分状态空间编码器处理原始、时间差分和频域视图并融合，抑制漂移；3) 空间图Mamba模块学习有向依赖结构，通过稀疏性和无环性正则化发现潜在通道相关性，无需预定义图。

Result: 在五个真实数据集上达到SOTA性能，计算复杂度呈线性，消融实验验证了各模块的有效性。

Conclusion: MedMamba通过将状态空间模型与医疗时序的领域知识相结合，有效解决了非平稳性和通道交互问题，为医疗时间序列分析提供了高效、可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Medical time series are central to healthcare, enabling continuous monitoring and supporting timely clinical decisions. Despite recent progress, existing methods struggle to jointly model local-global dynamics and handle nonstationarities like baseline drift, while often failing to capture latent channel interactions. To address these challenges, we propose MedMamba, an end-to-end architecture that integrates state space models with domain-specific inductive biases. Specifically, MedMamba first employs multi-scale convolutional embeddings to capture discriminative local morphology. Second, to mitigate nonstationarity, we introduce a tri-branch differential state space encoder that processes raw, temporal-difference, and frequency-domain views, fusing them to emphasize informative patterns while suppressing drift. Furthermore, to uncover latent channel correlations, we design a spatial graph Mamba module that learns a directed dependency structure regularized toward sparsity and acyclicity, which obviates the need for predefined graphs. Extensive experiments on five real-world datasets demonstrate that MedMamba achieves state-of-the-art performance while maintaining linear computational complexity, and ablation studies validate each component's contribution.Code is available at https://github.com/zhangda1018/MedMamba.

</details>


### [87] [OSDTW: Optimal Shared Depth and Task Weighting for Long-Tailed Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24969)
*Chang Chu,Qingyue Zhang,Shao-Lun Huang,Junxiong Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OSDTW框架，将长尾识别分解为头部任务和尾部任务，通过共享编码器和任务特定解码器，结合Fisher信息矩阵的三阶段训练流程和偏差-方差分解，实现可计算的超参数选择，在标准基准上超越强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长尾识别中存在头尾权衡问题，现有方法（重加权、解耦训练、多专家）缺乏关于表示共享和任务权重分配的理论指导，设计选择多基于启发式经验。

Method: 提出OSDTW任务分解框架：1) 将单标签识别分解为头任务和尾任务，采用共享编码器+任务特定解码器；2) 引入因子化模型，基于KL散度推导泛化误差分解；3) 三阶段训练：独立任务训练（估计任务最优和Fisher信息矩阵）、加权联合训练（学习共享编码器）、分支组装（构建最终解耦模型）；4) 在块对角Fisher近似下推导二阶泛化误差展开，实现偏差-方差分解用于选择共享深度和任务权重。

Result: 在标准长尾识别基准上，该方法超越了现有强基线方法，验证了框架的有效性。

Conclusion: OSDTW为长尾识别中的头尾权衡提供了理论驱动的解决方案，通过偏差-方差分解实现了可计算的超参数选择，是一种有原则的任务分解框架。

Abstract: Long-tailed recognition suffers from a persistent head--tail trade-off: improving tail performance often degrades head accuracy and can increase training instability. Despite strong empirical results from re-weighting, decoupled training, and multi-expert methods, key design choices about representation sharing between head and tail classes and supervision weighting across class groups remain largely heuristic. In this work, we propose OSDTW, a principled task-decomposition framework that partitions the original single-label recognition problem into a head task and a tail task, implemented with a shared encoder and task-specific decoders. To handle the mutual exclusivity and statistical dependence between the two label groups, we introduce a factorized model and show that the resulting Kullback--Leibler divergence-based generalization error can be written as the sum of task-wise terms up to an additive constant, yielding a well-defined task-wise objective. We further develop a three-stage training pipeline: independent task training to estimate task-wise optima and the Fisher information matrix, weighted joint training to learn a shared encoder, and branch assembly to construct the final decoupled model. Under a block-diagonal Fisher approximation, we derive a computable second-order expansion of the expected generalization error, decomposing it into encoder variance, encoder bias, and decoder variance. This bias--variance decomposition provides a computable proxy to select the shared depth and task weights, enabling efficient hyper-parameter search. Experiments on standard long-tailed benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach over strong baselines.

</details>


### [88] [Benchmarking non-conformity score functions in conformal prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24983)
*Sol Erika Boman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了共形预测中非一致性评分函数的性质与有效性，提出了新的评估方法，并在类别不平衡条件下进行了比较分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形预测中非一致性评分函数的选择对预测集大小和实用性至关重要，但现有文献缺乏对其性质和有效性的系统性研究。

Method: 综述了现有非一致性评分函数的性质，引入了原创性修改；提出了一种评估共形预测器预测集大小的原创方法；在类别不平衡条件下对不同非一致性评分函数进行了比较。

Result: 给出了非一致性评分函数的性质概述，提供了现有及改进后的评分函数示例，并通过提出的评估方法对多种评分函数进行了比较分析。

Conclusion: 非一致性评分函数的选择显著影响共形预测的性能，特别是在类别不平衡条件下，不同评分函数的表现存在明显差异。

Abstract: Conformal prediction is a useful and versatile alternative to model calibration in machine learning classification. It replaces single-class prediction with prediction sets, guaranteeing that the \textit{a priori} probability of the prediction sets containing the true class is larger than or equal to a pre-specified rate. The size and usefulness of the prediction sets relies heavily on the choice of the non-conformity score function. The scientific literature contains many examples of non-conformity score functions but there is an absence of studies examining their properties and effectiveness. In this paper, we give an overview of properties of non-conformity score functions. We give examples of non-conformity score functions in the existing literature and introduce original modifications. We introduce an original method of evaluating the prediction set sizes of conformal predictors and use it to provide a comparison between non-conformity score functions. We also examine efficacy of different non-conformity score functions for class-conditional conformal prediction in a setting with imbalanced classes.

</details>


### [89] [Mitigating Gradient Pathology in PINNs through Aligned Constraint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25001)
*Yichen Luo,Peiyu Zhu,Dongxiao Hu,Jia Wang,Tailin Wu,Dapeng Lan,Yu Liu,Zhibo Pang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对PINNs训练中梯度冲突导致的病理问题，提出Constraint-Aligned loss with Manifold Lifting (CAML)方法，通过对齐约束和流形提升策略有效缓解梯度冲突，在复杂PDE问题上显著提升数值稳定性与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINNs训练中PDE残差与边界条件的梯度相互对抗，导致模型陷入局部最优，现有方法（如自适应加权、硬约束）无法从根本上解决这一病态问题。

Method: 从损失景观和优化动力学角度系统分析梯度病理成因；提出CAML方法：将所有零阶项重构成对齐约束以缓解梯度冲突，并引入延迟因子帮助优化器跳过高曲率区域。

Result: 在高度复杂的PINN问题上，CAML显著提升了数值稳定性与训练效率。

Conclusion: CAML有效解决了PINNs中的梯度病理问题，代码已开源。

Abstract: While Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) are powerful for solving Partial Differential Equations (PDEs), their training is often paralyzed by gradient pathology. The gradients from the PDE residuals and boundary constraints oppose each other, trapping the model in local minima. Current solutions, such as adaptive weighting or hard constraints, either fail to fundamentally resolve this ill-conditioning or are limited to simple geometries. In this study, we systematically analyze the possible causes of this gradient pathology from the perspectives of loss landscapes and optimization dynamics. Based on the obtained conclusion, we propose Constraint-Aligned loss with Manifold Lifting (CAML). By reformulating all zeroth-order terms into aligned constraints, our method effectively mitigates gradient conflicts. In addition, we introduce a delay factor to help the optimizer skip the high-curvature area. Experiments demonstrate that our CAML significantly enhances numerical stability and efficiency in highly complex PINN problems. Our code is open-sourced on https://github.com/YichenLuo-0/CAML.

</details>


### [90] [A perspective on fluid mechanical environments for challenges in reinforcement learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25011)
*Shruti Mishra,Michael Chang,Vamsi Spandan,Shmuel M. Rubinstein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将流体力学问题及其仿真作为强化学习（RL）智能体在高维、演化环境中高效交互的测试平台，特别关注非线性不稳定性（如液滴破碎、界面混合、海洋怪波）等科学挑战。作者描述了两种智能体与流体环境交互的问题设定，明确了状态/动作空间、奖励函数以及环境的非平稳性与不变性，并利用Dedalus和JAX-CFD等开源仿真器进行环境生成，初步展示了在平稳流体环境中训练RL导航智能体的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发能够高效交互高维、演化的开放世界的实用RL智能体，并指出流体力学中的非线性不稳定性问题（如液滴破碎、界面混合、怪波）是极具挑战性的测试平台，兼具科学价值与工业应用背景。

Method: 提出了两种智能体与流体力学环境交互的问题描述，定义了状态空间、动作空间和奖励函数，明确了环境的非平稳性与保留的不变性，采用Dedalus和JAX-CFD作为开源仿真器，并在Dedalus生成的平稳环境中训练RL导航智能体作为初步验证。

Result: 成功利用Dedalus生成了平稳流体环境，并训练了能够在该环境中导航的RL智能体，验证了基于流体仿真进行RL环境生成的可行性。

Conclusion: 该工作为未来开发能够与模拟自然与工业流体力学科学挑战的环境进行有意义交互的RL智能体奠定了基础。

Abstract: We consider the challenge of developing agents that efficiently interact with high-dimensional, evolving environments, towards a view of practical reinforcement learning (RL) agents interacting with open worlds, of which they witness and affect only a small part. We argue that canonical fluid mechanics problems, and their simulations, present a compelling testbed for the development of such methods. These problems arise in nonlinear instabilities, where small disturbances can grow to transform the dynamics of a system. Nonlinear instabilities represent several open scientific challenges with industrial applications -- the droplet breakup of a liquid jet, mixing at an interface between two fluids, and the appearance of unusually tall rogue waves in the ocean. In these settings, agents may leverage preserved representations across the changing dynamics to learn efficiently.
  We present two problem descriptions of agents interacting with a fluid mechanical environment, and describe the state and action spaces, and reward functions, for these agents. For these examples, we specify the aspects of the environment which are nonstationary and the preserved invariances. We note Dedalus and JAX-CFD as open-source simulators that can be used for the development of reinforcement learning methods (Burns et al., 2016; Kochkov et al., 2021)) We demonstrate the use of Dedalus for environment generation by creating RL agents that learn to navigate in a stationary environment that is simulated using Dedalus. This sets the stage for future development of RL agents that learn to meaningfully interact with simulated environments that represent scientific challenges in natural and industrial flows.

</details>


### [91] [MimirRAG: A Multi-Agent RAG Framework for Financial Data Retrieval with Metadata Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25030)
*Magnus Samuelsen,Wilmer Nyström,Somnath Mazumdar,Mansoor Hussain,Mikkel Strange*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了MimirRAG，一种面向金融分析的多智能体检索增强生成系统，通过元数据集成、表格感知分块和智能体工作流等关键技术，在FinanceBench上达到89.3%的准确率，并经过金融分析师专家验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统虽能减少LLM幻觉并提升答案准确性，但在从混合金融文档中提取有意义的洞察并整合到分析师工作流中仍面临挑战，需要设计专门针对金融领域的RAG系统。

Method: 提出MimirRAG，采用模块化流水线架构，包括：结构保持的PDF解析、表格感知的分块、元数据提取、基于智能体的检索（含查询规划和混合搜索）、验证以及支持数值推理的上下文感知生成。

Result: 在FinanceBench上达到89.3%准确率，优于原始基准基线；消融实验识别出元数据集成、表格感知分块和智能体工作流三个关键技术推动因素；专家验证还指出成功部署需要校准信任、综合数据集成和用户个性化。

Conclusion: 将多智能体RAG架构与以人为中心的设计原则相结合，可以改善金融分析中有意义洞察的提取。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems offer a promising approach to reduce hallucinations and improve answer accuracy in large language models (LLMs), a requirement for reliable, financial analysis where answers must be grounded in verifiable evidence from filings rather than generated from model priors. However, designing RAG systems that extract meaningful insights from mixed financial documents and integrate into analyst workflows remains challenging. This paper introduces MimirRAG (Metadata-Integrated Multi-Agent Information Retrieval), a multi-agent RAG system developed iteratively to address these challenges. MimirRAG features a modular pipeline encompassing structure-preserving parsing of PDF filings, table-aware chunking, metadata extraction, agent-based retrieval with query planning and hybrid search, validation, and context-aware generation with numerical reasoning support. Our ablation study identifies three key technical enablers for effective financial RAG: metadata integration, table-aware chunking, and an agentic workflow. MimirRAG was evaluated quantitatively using FinanceBench and qualitatively through expert validation with four financial analysts. The system achieved 89.3% accuracy on FinanceBench, outperforming the original benchmark baselines. Expert feedback highlighted that successful deployment also requires calibrated trust, comprehensive data integration, and user personalization. We conclude that combining multi-agent RAG architecture with human-centric design principles can improve the extraction of meaningful insights in financial analysis.

</details>


### [92] [Leveraging Gauge Freedom for Learning Non-Gradient Population Dynamics of Stochastic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25107)
*Jules Berman,Tobias Blickhan,Benjamin Peherstorfer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种非梯度推断流（NGIF）算法，通过连续性方程的弱形式推断非梯度种群动力学，克服了传统方法局限于梯度流和最小动能选择的限制，在低维和高维物理问题上均提升了分布精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有种群动力学推断工作大多局限于梯度势场驱动的流，且梯度流虽在动能最小化意义下最优，但无法捕捉非势输运现象。作者希望利用规范自由度，提出更通用的非梯度推断框架。

Method: 利用连续性方程的弱形式参数化一般向量场，摆脱梯度流限制；通过选择其他准则（而非最小动能）来消除规范自由度，从而推断非梯度种群动力学。

Result: 在多种低维和高维物理问题中，NGIF方法相比仅限梯度流的基线方法，在分布精度上更优，能更好地捕捉非势输运行为。

Conclusion: NGIF算法通过弱形式连续性方程和灵活的选取准则，有效拓展了种群动力学推断的能力，尤其适用于非梯度输运场景。

Abstract: Existing work on population dynamics inference often focuses on flows arising from vector fields that are the gradients of scalar potentials. Among all admissible flows that are compatible with the population dynamics, gradient flows are optimal in a specific sense: they minimize kinetic energy. The selection of fields based on different criteria corresponds to a gauge freedom when determining population dynamics, which we leverage in this work. We propose Non-Gradient Inference Flows (NGIF), an algorithm to infer non-gradient population dynamics using a weak formulation of the continuity equation. This allows us to parameterize general vector fields and choose other selection criteria beyond minimal kinetic energy. We demonstrate on a variety of low- and high-dimensional physics problems that this more general approach improves distributional accuracy over gradient-restricted baselines and better captures non-potential transport.

</details>


### [93] [Optimizing Multidimensional Scaling in Gini Metric Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25124)
*Cassandra Mussard,Stéphane Mussard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Gini多维缩放（Gini MDS）框架，通过引入基于值和秩的Gini伪距离（含可调超参数），实现对噪声和异常值鲁棒的降维嵌入，在16个UCI数据集和MNIST图像上优于传统欧氏MDS，并利用PyTorch张量实现实现GPU加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统欧氏MDS对噪声和异常值敏感，实际应用中数据常被污染，需要一种更鲁棒的多维缩放方法。

Method: 提出Gini MDS框架，引入基于数值及其排序的Gini伪距离，该距离含有一个可微调的超参数，允许灵活探索潜在配置以最佳匹配观测到的相异度。

Result: 在16个含异常值的UCI数据集和含噪声的MNIST图像上，Gini MDS优于欧氏MDS；基于PyTorch的张量实现比sklearn标准MDS计算效率更高且支持GPU加速。

Conclusion: Gini MDS是对欧氏MDS的鲁棒扩展，通过Gini伪距离有效抵抗噪声和异常值，且具有高效的GPU实现，适合实际应用。

Abstract: The Gini Multidimensional Scaling (Gini MDS) framework extends the Euclidean multidimensional scaling. We introduce a Gini pseudo-distance based on values and their ranks that depends on a fine-tunable hyperparameter. This pseudo-distance allows flexible exploration of latent configurations, enabling embeddings that best match observed dissimilarities. The Gini MDS is shown to be robust to noise and outliers, making it well-suited for real-world applications. We provide experiments on 16 UCI datasets with outliers and on MNIST images with noise to show that the Gini MDS outperforms the Euclidean MDS on noisy data. Finally, a tensor-based implementation in \texttt{PyTorch} provides GPU acceleration and efficient computation compared to the standard MDS of the \texttt{sklearn} library.

</details>


### [94] [Theoretical Analysis of Sparse Optimization with Reparameterization, Weight Decay, and Adaptive Learning Rate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25134)
*Huangyu Xu,Jingqin Yang,Qianqian Xu,Jiaye Teng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对 ℓₚ 正则化在 0<p<1 时梯度无界导致的优化不稳定问题，提出基于重参数化、权重衰减和自适应学习率的 ReWA 方法，在保持分类精度的同时显著提升稀疏性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ℓₚ (0<p<1) 正则化在稀疏优化中效果良好，但存在梯度无界导致的优化不稳定问题，亟需一种既能保持稀疏性又能稳定优化的方法。

Method: 提出 ReWA 方法，结合重参数化（Reparameterization）、权重衰减（Weight decay）和自适应学习率（Adaptive learning rate），通过改变优化景观来缓解不稳定问题。

Result: 在 CIFAR-10 和 ImageNet 上使用 ResNet 的实验表明，ReWA 相比 ℓ₁ 正则化显著提升了稀疏性，同时保持测试精度不变。

Conclusion: ReWA 是一种有效的稀疏优化新方法，与 ℓₚ 正则化有密切联系，但通过重构优化景观解决了梯度无界带来的不稳定性，实际应用中在稀疏性与精度之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Sparse optimization is a fundamental challenge in various practical applications. A popular approach to sparse optimization is $\ell_p$ regularization. However, it may encounter optimization instability due to the unbounded gradients when $0<p<1$. In this paper, we introduce a novel approach to sparse optimization termed ReWA, based on Reparameterization, Weight decay, and Adaptive learning rate. ReWA is closely connected to $\ell_p$-regularization, yet it unveils a distinct optimization landscape that helps mitigate instability issues. Experiments on CIFAR-10 and ImageNet with ResNets demonstrate that ReWA leads to significant sparsity improvements over the $\ell_1$-regularization approach while preserving test accuracy.

</details>


### [95] [Abduction-Deduction Entanglement: Domain Generalization via Representation Transplants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25156)
*Kasra Jalaldoust,Elias Bareinboum*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于因果不变性的领域泛化方法，通过参数化“表示移植”来探索合理的目标分布，并在理想优化下实现极小极大最优目标预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 源分布上训练好的预测模型往往无法泛化到目标分布，有效的跨域推断需要依赖因果机制的不变性，但这些结构性不变性仅从源数据无法识别。

Method: 将最优预测分解为溯因映射（从观测推断未观测变量）和演绎映射（从观测和推断量预测标签），利用源数据约束合法的溯因-演绎组合；通过“表示移植”（表示空间中的特定线性变换）参数化这一约束族，在保持演绎成分的同时操控溯因内容；在因果机制不变假设下，通过学习者-对抗者游戏搜索合理的目标分布空间。

Result: 理论证明在理想优化下该方法可达到极小极大最优的目标预测；实验验证了方法在领域泛化基准上的竞争力。

Conclusion: 源分布下最优预测的部分可识别性可以经由溯因-演绎纠缠来利用，结合因果不变性假设，能够有效泛化到目标领域。

Abstract: Prediction models trained under the source distribution do not generalize well to a different target distribution. A valid inference about an unseen data distribution must be anchored by the invariance of certain causal mechanisms that generate the source and target data, however, these structural invariances are non-identifiable from the source data alone. Under mild causal assumptions about the data, we show that the optimal prediction in the target is in fact partially identifiable by the source distribution. The result rests on a simple observation: In any domain, the optimal prediction can be factorized into what we call a pair of abduction and deduction maps, where the abduction map makes inference about some unobserved variables (possibly confounders) from the observed variables and the deduction map predicts the label using both the observed and inferred quantities. Access to large source data pins down the optimal prediction, thus constrains the valid abduction-deduction ensembles that produce it -- a non-identifiability that we call the abduction-deduction entanglement. To leverage this, we parameterize the constrained family using what we call a representation transplant, that is a specific linear transformation in the representation space that manipulates the abduction content of the representation while retaining the deduction component. Invariance of the causal mechanism generating the label implies existence of an invariant deduction map between source and target. Thus, we can search the space of plausible target distributions via a parametric transplant. We use this scheme in a learner-adversary game that, under an idealistic optimization, provably terminates with the learner having the minimax-optimal target prediction. Evaluations verify the theory, showing that the method is competitive in DG benchmarks.

</details>


### [96] [AME-TS: Anchored Mixture-of-Experts for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25166)
*Rui Wang,Renhao Xue,Ray Razi,Huan Song,Hannah R. Marlowe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AME-TS，一种结构引导的稀疏时间序列基础模型，通过可解释的时间结构特征（可预测性、季节性、趋势、稀疏性）引导专家路由，实现条件计算和专家专业化，在GIFT-Eval基准上取得了优越的精度-效率平衡，并展现出更稳定的微调性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列预测模型通过大规模Transformer骨干扩展，但大多数方法对所有序列采用共享密集计算路径，忽略了时间结构的异质性。标准MoE路由存在专家专业化识别弱、下游适配不稳定的问题。

Method: AME-TS包含轻量级状态预测器，用于估计序列级别的描述符（可预测性、季节性、趋势、稀疏性），并将其映射为专家软结构先验。该先验在训练过程中引导token级路由，实现结构对齐的专业化。

Result: 在GIFT-Eval基准上，AME-TS在不同模型规模下实现了良好的精度-效率权衡：小规模时显著优于现有时间序列基础模型，大规模时保持竞争力，且通过稀疏路由激活更少的参数。在M5数据集微调时，AME-TS展现出更可解释的路由几何和更稳定的专家专业化。

Conclusion: 结构感知的路由是实现稀疏专家模型在时间序列预测中优势的有效且可靠方式。

Abstract: Time series forecasting models are increasingly scaled through large Transformer backbones, yet most existing approaches process all series through a shared dense computation path despite substantial heterogeneity in temporal structure. Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) offers a natural alternative by enabling conditional computation, but standard MoE routing leaves expert specialization weakly identified and often unstable during downstream adaptation. We propose AME-TS, a structure-guided sparse time series foundation model that aligns expert routing with interpretable temporal structure. AME-TS first uses a lightweight regime predictor to estimate series-level descriptors, including forecastability, seasonality, trend, and sparsity, and maps them to a soft structural prior over experts. This series-level prior guides token-level routing during training, encouraging structure-aligned specialization. On the GIFT-Eval benchmark, AME-TS delivers a strong accuracy-efficiency tradeoff across model scales: it substantially outperforms existing time series foundation models at small model scales and remains competitive with the strongest models at larger scales, while activating substantially fewer parameters through sparse routing. We further show that AME-TS learns more interpretable routing geometry and substantially more stable expert specialization than standard MoE during fine-tuning on the M5 dataset. These results suggest that structure-aware routing is an effective and reliable way to realize the benefits of sparse expert models for time series forecasting.

</details>


### [97] [Learning Treatment Effects during Resource Allocation via Priority-Queue Randomization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25169)
*JungHo Lee,Johnna Sundberg,Pim Welle,Bryan Wilder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于优先队列的实验设计框架，用于在有限资源且存在不确定性的公共服务项目中，通过风险评估分数将申请人随机分入不同优先队列，在优先满足高需求个体的同时，实现治疗效应的可信评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共服务项目常在资源有限且收益不确定的情况下运作，需要随机化来支持可信评估，但实际中申请人常进入等候名单，资源按需求优先级分配，使得直接随机化难以实施。作者旨在设计一种既能优先服务高需求者，又能实现因果效应识别的实验框架。

Method: 提出了优先队列分配（priority-queue allocation）的实验设计框架：申请人基于评估风险评分被随机分配到不同优先队列，然后按队列优先级和队列内先到先得的原则分配资源。当到达过程外生时，处理是条件随机化的，标准估计量可识别；当到达过程内生时，队列随机化作为处理的工具变量，识别由排队过程引起的局部处理效应。此外，开发了优化队列分配设计，在统计效率与优先高需求之间进行权衡。

Result: 理论上：刻画了优先队列分配下可识别的因果效应；证明了尽管设计导致处理分配存在依赖性，但通常的独立同分布效率界限仍是合理的设计目标。实证上：使用美国某大型县住房分配项目的数据展示了所提出设计的应用。

Conclusion: 优先队列随机化设计能有效兼顾公平（优先高需求）与因果识别（可信评估），为实际公共服务中的资源分配与评估提供了可行的实验方法论。

Abstract: Public service programs often allocate limited resources under uncertainty about their benefits, creating a need for randomization to support credible evaluation. In practice, however, applicants commonly enter waitlists where resources are prioritized toward individuals judged to have higher need through tiered priority queues, making direct randomization difficult. Motivated by this, we develop an experimental design framework for learning treatment effects while treating those most in need where incoming applicants are randomized into priority queues based on their assessed risk scores. Treatments are then provided across queues in priority order and first-in-first-out within queue as budget becomes available. Our contributions are two-fold. First, we characterize what causal effects are identified under this priority-queue allocation. When arrivals are exogenous, treatments are conditionally randomized, and hence standard estimands are identified; when arrivals are endogenous, queue randomization instead provides an instrument for treatment, identifying local treatment effects induced by the queuing process. Second, we develop optimized queue-assignment designs that trade off statistical efficiency against prioritizing higher-need applicants. We show in the process that, despite dependence in treatment assignments induced by the design, usual iid efficiency bounds remain well-justified design objectives. We illustrate the proposed designs using data from a housing allocation program in a large U.S. county.

</details>


### [98] [Localization then Neutralization: Gradient-guided Token Suppression against Visual Prompt Injection Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25194)
*Dongpeng Zhang,Ke Ma,Yangbangyan Jiang,Gaozheng Pei,Longtao Huang,Qianqian Xu,Qingming Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出梯度令牌掩码(GTM)方法，通过梯度分析定位关键图像令牌并掩码，以接近零的攻击成功率防御多模态大语言模型中的对抗性提示注入攻击，同时保持模型效用和极低计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗性图像通过提示注入对多模态大语言模型构成严重安全威胁，现有防御缺乏对底层机制的原理性理解，且难以平衡效率与防御效用。

Method: 提出梯度令牌掩码(GTM)：1) 发现成功攻击不依赖整张图像，而是依赖于一小部分关键图像令牌；2) 利用隐状态梯度范数(Hidden-State Gradient Norm)分数进行生成影响归因；3) 理论证明该排序与完整对抗损失梯度的排序一致；4) 只需单次前向-后向传播即可定位并清零少量高得分令牌。

Result: 在提示注入和多模态越狱攻击的广泛实验中，该方法将攻击成功率降至接近零，同时保持模型效用，且计算开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 基于关键图像令牌局部性的洞察，GTM通过梯度归因与掩码提供了一种既有理论保证又高效实用的对抗防御方案，有效解决了安全性与可用性之间的权衡。

Abstract: Adversarial images pose a severe security threat to multimodal large language models through prompt injection. Existing defenses largely lack a principled understanding of the underlying mechanisms and struggle to balance efficiency and defense utility. In this work, we show that successful adversarial attacks do not rely on the entire image uniformly but instead depend on a small subset of critical image tokens. Based on this insight, we propose Gradient Token Masking (GTM), which localizes these tokens via gradient analysis and neutralizes them through masking. We find that attribution based on the first generated token's output probability fails when attacks preserve the predicted token. To overcome this, GTM utilizes the Hidden-State Gradient Norm score for generation-influence attribution under adversarial inputs. We prove that its ranking is consistent with that of the full adversarial loss gradient, providing a theoretical guarantee for accurate localization. Our method requires only a single forward-backward pass to identify and zero out a small number of high-scoring tokens, effectively disrupting the adversarial attack path. Extensive experiments on prompt injection and multimodal jailbreak attacks demonstrate that our approach reduces attack success rates (ASR) to near zero while preserving model utility with negligible computational overhead.

</details>


### [99] [Multi-Objective Learning for Diffusion Models: A Statistical Theory under Semi-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25210)
*Ziheng Cheng,Yixiao Huang,Hanlin Zhu,Haoran Geng,Somayeh Sojoudi,Jitendra Malik,Pieter Abbeel,Xin Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种半监督多目标学习框架，通过先训练轻量级专家模型再蒸馏为通用模型的方式，在少量标注数据下实现扩散模型的多目标帕累托最优，理论证明所需配对样本量仅取决于专家模型复杂度，并在机器人控制和图像修复任务中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型部署中常需同时处理多个目标分布（如不同文本提示域的图像生成、机器人多环境策略），构成多目标学习问题。好的帕累托折衷需要容量远大于单任务的通用模型，导致样本复杂度随模型复杂度增长。为调和这一矛盾，发展有限数据下的扩散模型多目标学习框架。

Method: 提出两阶段训练流程：第一阶段从有限配对数据中拟合轻量级专家模型；第二阶段通过生成伪样本将这些专家模型蒸馏进一个通用模型。该方法运行在半监督机制下（配对样本稀缺但未标注条件数据丰富）。

Result: 建立了泛化界，证明所需配对样本量仅取决于专家模型类的复杂度。将理论扩展到顺序决策的扩散策略，以处理在线 rollout 中的分布偏移。在机器人控制和图像恢复任务上进行了广泛实验验证。

Conclusion: 提出的半监督多目标学习框架能有效降低对配对数据的需求，使扩散模型在有限数据下仍能实现良好的多目标帕累托折衷，理论结果与实验验证一致。

Abstract: Diffusion models are increasingly used as powerful conditional generators, yet real deployments often involve multiple target distributions arising from different tasks, e.g., diverse prompt domains in text-to-image generation, or multiple environments in robotics with diffusion policies. This naturally leads to a multi-objective learning (MOL) problem. A key challenge is that achieving good Pareto trade-offs can require a generalist model class with substantially larger capacity than what suffices for solving any individual task, thereby increasing statistical cost since sample complexity typically scales with the model complexity. To reconcile this, we develop a principled MOL framework for diffusion models with limited data: a semi-supervised regime where paired (labeled) samples are scarce, but (unlabeled) condition data are abundant. We propose a two-stage training procedure that first fits lightweight specialist models from limited paired data, and then distills them into a generalist model by generating pseudo-samples. We establish generalization bounds showing that the required number of paired samples only depends on the complexity of the specialist model classes. We further extend the theory to diffusion policies for sequential decision making to account for distribution shift in on-policy rollouts. Extensive experiments on robotic control and image restoration tasks are conducted to verify our theoretical results.

</details>


### [100] [Continuous-Depth Field Theory for Transformer Patching and Mechanistic Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25225)
*David N. Olivieri,Antonio F. Pérez Rodríguez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种场论框架来组织和预测Transformer中的激活干预（如激活修补、因果追踪等），将残差流视为深度-令牌场，通过局部源插入、灵敏度场预测、格林函数响应传播和伴随变分问题来形式化修补操作。在GPT-2风格模型上的实验验证了前向响应理论，识别了有界局部线性区间，并建立了灵敏度、传播场和格林算子切片作为组织修补实验的实用语言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机械可解释性方法（激活修补、因果追踪、路径修补、引导方向等）缺乏一个统一的理论框架来组织和预测这些干预操作的效果，需要一种更系统的数学语言来描述残差流中的扰动及其传播。

Method: 将残差流建模为深度-令牌场，修补视为局部源插入，修补效应通过灵敏度场预测，下游传播通过经验格林函数响应刻画，修补选择通过伴随变分问题求解。在GPT-2风格自回归Transformer上进行实验验证。

Result: 识别了有界局部线性区间；从一阶灵敏度预测了修补效应；测量了跨深度和令牌位置的结构化各向异性传播；从高灵敏度站点和切片格林算子构建了响应描述；证明了提示诱导的残差位移可以迁移答案行为。

Conclusion: 建立了灵敏度、传播场和格林算子切片作为组织修补实验的实用语言，并为修补站点推断和跨尺度迁移提供了前向数学基础。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability often uses activation patching, causal tracing, path patching, and steering directions to reveal behaviorally meaningful directions in Transformer activation space. This paper develops a field-theoretic framework for organizing and predicting such interventions. Treating the residual stream as a depth-token field, we formulate patching as localized source insertion, patch effects as sensitivity-field predictions, downstream propagation as empirical Green-function response, and patch selection as an adjoint variational problem. Empirically, we test the forward response theory in GPT-2-style autoregressive Transformers by applying localized residual-field interventions and observing the induced residual-field differences and logit-difference responses. We identify a bounded local linear regime; predict patch effects from first-order sensitivities across residual sites; measure structured anisotropic propagation across depth and token position; construct response descriptions from high-sensitivity sites and sliced Green operators; and show that prompt-induced residual displacements can transfer answer behavior. These results establish response objects, namely sensitivities, propagated fields, and Green-operator slices, as a practical language for organizing patching experiments and as the forward mathematical basis for formulating patch-site inference and cross-scale transfer.formulated.

</details>


### [101] [Latent Q-Barrier Shielding for Safe In-Context Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25267)
*Minjae Kwon,Amir Moeini,Shangtong Zhang,Lu Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出潜变量Q-Barrier屏障，用于安全上下文强化学习(ICRL)中，在不更新参数的情况下，通过推断上下文、潜动力学和集成成本评论家，在部署时根据剩余预算和预测未来成本过滤或软重加权候选动作，从而在OOD分布偏移下改善奖励-安全权衡。在五个安全ICRL基准测试中，该方法在四个基准上获得更高回报，同时在所有五个基准中匹配或降低平均情节成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练的安全ICRL在分布外部署偏移下表现不佳，因为剩余预算仅通过冻结策略条件影响行为，而非对预测未来成本进行显式的动作级检查，导致奖励-安全权衡较差。

Method: 提出潜变量Q-Barrier屏障：学习上下文表示、潜动力学和集成成本评论家；部署时从历史中推断上下文，利用剩余预算和预测未来成本对候选动作进行过滤或软重加权；并证明了条件性、误差分解的屏障裕度结果。

Result: 在五个安全ICRL基准测试中，Q-Barrier屏障在短时间内上下文窗口后，四个基准上获得更高回报，所有五个基准中匹配或降低平均情节成本。

Conclusion: Q-Barrier屏障能够有效改善部署时的奖励-安全权衡，在不需要参数更新的情况下实现更安全、更高效的在线适应。

Abstract: Safe in-context reinforcement learning (ICRL) adapts online from interaction history without test-time parameter updates while controlling episode cost under a safety budget. Under out-of-distribution (OOD) deployment shifts, pretraining-only safe ICRL can give poor reward-safety tradeoffs because the remaining budget affects behavior only through frozen policy conditioning, not an explicit action-level check against predicted future cost. We propose a latent Q-Barrier shield that learns a context representation, latent dynamics, and an ensemble cost critic before deployment. Without parameter updates, the shield infers context from history and filters or softly reweights candidate actions using the remaining budget and predicted future cost. We prove a conditional, error-decomposed barrier-margin result: a Q-Barrier-satisfying action leaves the next latent-budget state with an approximately budget-safe continuation under the learned critic, up to Bellman and latent-prediction errors. Across five safe ICRL benchmarks, the shield improves deployment-time reward-safety tradeoffs over a strong safe-ICRL baseline: after a short context window, it achieves higher return in four of five benchmarks while matching or lowering average episode cost in all five.

</details>


### [102] [When Interpretability Becomes a Liability: Adversarial Attacks on CBM Concept Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25304)
*Aditya Sridhar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地研究了概念瓶颈模型（CBM）在概念层面的对抗性脆弱性，并提出SPECTRA防御方法，将成功攻击所需的最小扰动范数从0.46提升至4200以上，同时保持分类精度下降在2.2%以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概念瓶颈模型通过显式概念激活提供可解释的中间表示，但这种可解释性引入了一个此前未被探索的攻击面——概念瓶颈层本身，亟需系统性研究其对抗性脆弱性并开发防御方法。

Method: 1) 建立量化概念空间鲁棒性的理论框架，提出新的评估指标；2) 提出SPECTRA防御方法，通过基于语义扰动的概念训练进行稳定性正则化，强化语义表示空间。

Result: 标准CBM在CUB-200-2011数据集上表现出严重的概念级操纵脆弱性；SPECTRA将成功攻击所需的最小扰动范数从0.46提升至4200以上，分类精度仅下降2.2%。

Conclusion: 概念级攻击是一种本质上不同的威胁模型，该工作为可解释机器学习与对抗鲁棒性的交叉领域开辟了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs) have emerged as a cornerstone approach for interpretable machine learning, providing human-understandable intermediate representations through explicit concept activations. However, this interpretability fundamentally introduces a critical, previously unexplored attack surface: the concept bottleneck layer itself. We present a comprehensive, systematic study of concept-level adversarial vulnerabilities in CBMs, revealing that targeted, minimal perturbations operating on input pixels can induce catastrophic misclassification by manipulating semantic representations. We develop a rigorous theoretical framework to quantify concept-space robustness, establishing novel metrics that expose the vulnerability landscape of these architectures. Our extensive analysis on the CUB-200-2011 dataset demonstrates that standard CBMs exhibit severe susceptibility to concept-level manipulation. To address this critical weakness, we introduce SPECTRA (Semantic Perturbation-based Concept Training for Robustness against Attacks), a principled stability regularization defense. SPECTRA effectively hardens the semantic representation space, increasing the minimal perturbation norm required for a successful attack from 0.46 to over 4,200, rendering targeted concept manipulation computationally prohibitive. Furthermore, SPECTRA preserves baseline classification accuracy to within 2.2%. By establishing concept-level attacks as a fundamentally distinct threat model, this work opens a new research frontier at the intersection of interpretable machine learning and adversarial robustness.

</details>


### [103] [Electricity Consumption Forecasting: An Approach Using Cooperative Ensemble Learning with SHapley Additive exPlanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25305)
*Eduardo Luiz Alba,Gilson Adamczuk Oliveira,Matheus Henrique Dal Molin Ribeiro,Érick Oliveira Rodrigues*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为弱分离器助推器（WSB）的协同集成学习方法，利用LSTM、RF、SVR和XGBoost四种机器学习模型，结合SHAP特征选择、GA和PSO超参数优化，对巴西联邦学院（IFPR）两个校区未来12个月的电力消耗进行预测。结果显示WSB方法表现最优，在Palmas校区和Coronel Vivida校区的sMAPE分别为13.90%和18.72%。SHAP分析表明，滞后时间序列值对预测影响最大，而气候变量影响较小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高校电力消耗随机构扩张快速增长，且对环境有显著影响，因此需要有效的电力费用管理。传统预测方法难以应对电力资源的多种影响因素，亟需更精确的预测模型来支持高校能源管理决策。

Method: 使用LSTM、RF、SVR和XGBoost四种机器学习模型，基于IFPR两个校区过去7年的历史电力消耗数据和气候变量，预测未来12个月的用电量。采用SHAP进行特征选择，使用GA和PSO进行超参数优化，并提出了一种名为WSB的协同集成学习方法。

Result: WSB集成学习方法在两个校区均表现最优：Palmas校区sMAPE为13.90%、MAE为1990.87 kWh；Coronel Vivida校区sMAPE为18.72%、MAE为465.02 kWh。SHAP分析显示两个校区特征重要性模式不同，但共同点是滞后时间序列值影响最大，气候变量影响最小。

Conclusion: 提出的WSB协同集成学习方法能有效预测高校电力消耗，滞后时间序列值是关键影响因素，气候变量作用有限。该方法可用于高校电力费用管理和节能决策支持。

Abstract: Electricity expense management presents significant challenges, as this resource is susceptible to various influencing factors. In universities, the demand for this resource is rapidly growing with institutional expansion and has a significant environmental impact. In this study, the machine learning models long short-term memory (LSTM), random forest (RF), support vector regression (SVR), and extreme gradient boosting (XGBoost) were trained with historical consumption data from the Federal Institute of Paraná (IFPR) over the last seven years and climatic variables to forecast electricity consumption 12 months ahead. Datasets from two campuses were adopted. To improve model performance, feature selection was performed using Shapley additive explanations (SHAP), and hyperparameter optimization was carried out using genetic algorithm (GA) and particle swarm optimization (PSO). The results indicate that the proposed cooperative ensemble learning approach named Weaker Separator Booster (WSB) exhibited the best performance for datasets. Specifically, it achieved an sMAPE of 13.90% and MAE of 1990.87 kWh for the IFPR-Palmas Campus and an sMAPE of 18.72% and MAE of 465.02 kWh for the Coronel Vivida Campus. The SHAP analysis revealed distinct feature importance patterns across the two IFPR campuses. A commonality that emerged was the strong influence of lagged time-series values and a minimal influence of climatic variables.

</details>


### [104] [UWM-JEPA: Predictive World Models That Imagine in Belief Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25313)
*Santosh Kumar Radha,Oktay Goktas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出UWM-JEPA，一种使用密度矩阵潜变量和酉预测器的世界模型，在部分可观测环境中能保持联合态谱不变，实现盲推演下的不确定性保持，优于向量潜变量基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分可观测环境下的世界模型需要想象多种兼容的隐藏未来状态并在反事实动作下进行操控，而现有JEPA模型使用向量值潜变量缺乏内部结构来表示盲推演过程中的隐藏延续信念。

Method: 提出UWM-JEPA，采用联合系统-环境空间的密度矩阵潜变量（保留联合态谱结构）和可学习的酉预测器（在推演过程中保持谱不变性，防止不确定性耗散），训练目标包含反事实目标而非教师强制目标。

Result: 在隐藏速度指示任务上，UWM-JEPA达到0.77准确率且随动作扰动单调下降，而LSTM-JEPA坍缩到多数类准确率0.53；盲推演下UWM-JEPA短视界探针R²损失<10点，向量潜变量基线损失41和68点；动作敏感性依赖于反事实目标训练。

Conclusion: 对于JEPA世界模型在部分可观测条件下的想象能力，潜变量几何结构和预测器动力学比冻结的上下文编码能力更重要；反事实目标训练是实现动作敏感性的关键，该发现适用于超越酉参数化的更广泛场景。

Abstract: World models for partially observed environments must imagine multiple compatible hidden futures and steer between them under counterfactual actions. Joint Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) do this in latent space, but a vector-valued latent has no internal structure for carrying the belief over hidden continuations through blind rollout. We introduce the Unitary World Model JEPA (UWM-JEPA), a JEPA world model with a density-matrix latent on a joint system-environment space and a learned unitary predictor. The construction preserves the joint-state spectrum exactly during rollout, so the predictor itself cannot dissipate the represented uncertainty.
  On a hidden-velocity indicator task requiring five-step forward simulation under a given action sequence with the target observation masked, UWM-JEPA reaches 0.77 accuracy and degrades monotonically as actions are perturbed; a parameter-matched LSTM-JEPA trained under the same counterfactual-target objective and action head collapses to majority-class accuracy (0.53) under every action condition. Under blind rollout, UWM-JEPA loses fewer than ten points of probe R^2 at short horizons while vector-latent baselines lose forty-one and sixty-eight; both nevertheless tie on a held-out context probe, locating the separation in the predictor rather than the encoder. Action sensitivity itself requires training against counterfactual rather than teacher-forced targets, a finding that applies beyond the unitary parameterisation. For JEPA world models to imagine under partial observability, latent geometry and predictor dynamics matter, not frozen context-encoding capacity alone.

</details>


### [105] [CausalFlow: Causal Attribution and Counterfactual Repair for LLM Agent Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25338)
*Akash Bonagiri,Devang Borkar,Gerard Janno Anderias,Setareh Rafatirad,Houman Homayoun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CausalFlow 提出一种基于反事实干预的框架，将 LLM 智能体失败的执行轨迹转化为最小化修复和可复用的监督信号，从而提升多步任务可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体在多步任务（推理、工具使用、环境交互）中频繁失败，现有方法仅记录或启发式重试，未能结构化地利用失败轨迹中的信息来定位和修复错误步骤。

Method: CausalFlow 将执行轨迹建模为步骤间依赖的序列链，通过步骤级反事实干预计算因果责任分数（CRS）来识别导致失败的步骤，并生成最小编辑的修复（wrong step → corrected step），形成验证过的对比对。

Result: 在数学推理、代码生成、问答和医学浏览四个基准上，CausalFlow 将失败执行转化为验证过的最小修复，具有高最小性和因果一致性分数；在复杂检索设置中优于启发式改进，同时产生更局部化的修复。

Conclusion: 对结构化执行轨迹进行干预分析，为将智能体失败转化为可靠性提升和可学习的监督信号提供了一种原则性且可扩展的机制。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents frequently fail on multi-step tasks involving reasoning, tool use, and environment interaction. While such failures are typically logged or retried heuristically, they contain structured signals about where execution broke down. We introduce CausalFlow, an interventional framework that converts failed agent traces into minimal counterfactual repairs and reusable supervision. CausalFlow models execution traces as sequential chains of dependent steps and computes Causal Responsibility Scores(CRS) via step-level counterfactual intervention to identify failure-inducing steps. For these steps, we generate minimally edited repairs that flip the final outcome to success, producing validated contrastive pairs of the form (wrong step, corrected step). CausalFlow supports two complementary uses: targeted test-time repair that recovers from failures with minimal behavioral drift, and training-time supervision suitable for offline preference optimization or reward modeling. Across four benchmarks spanning mathematical reasoning, code generation, question answering, and medical browsing, CausalFlow converts failed executions into validated minimal repairs with high minimality and causal-consensus scores, and demonstrates that causal attribution is necessary for reliable improvement across diverse agent tasks, outperforming heuristic refinement in complex retrieval settings while producing more localized repairs throughout. These results demonstrate that interventional analysis over structured execution traces provides a principled and scalable mechanism for transforming agent failures into reliability gains and learning-ready supervision.

</details>


### [106] [Certified Robustness from Approximate Gaussian Mixture Structures in Pretrained Latent Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25352)
*Konstantinos Emmanouilidis,Tianjiao Ding,Nghia Nguyen,Nicolas Loizou,René Vidal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用数据表示的潜在结构设计可认证鲁棒分类器，通过证明预训练编码器将输入映射到接近高斯混合的潜在分布时，认证精度会优雅下降，从而在无需精确分布假设的情况下实现可认证鲁棒性，在CIFAR-10和ImageNet上取得了最优或具有竞争力的认证精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型易受对抗扰动影响，经验性防御缺乏形式化保证，而现有认证方法由于无法利用复杂数据分布的结构，往往给出过于保守的边界。

Method: 首先分析高斯混合设定，推导鲁棒分类器存在性的充要条件并构建具有闭式认证证书和泛化保证的分类器；核心贡献是证明精确结构并非必需——若预训练编码器将输入映射到与高斯混合KL散度不超过ε的潜在分布，则认证精度会优雅下降，并给出真实分布与近似分布下鲁棒性之间的显式边界。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和ImageNet上达到最优或具有竞争力的认证精度，同时保持强干净性能和低计算开销。

Conclusion: 近似潜在结构是实现可认证鲁棒性的一种实用且有原则的途径。

Abstract: Deep learning models are vulnerable to adversarial perturbations, raising important concerns for safety-critical deployment. Empirical defenses can achieve strong robustness in practice, but lack formal guarantees, motivating the need for certifiably robust classifiers. While certified methods provide formal guarantees, they often yield overly conservative bounds due to their inability to exploit structure in complex data distributions. In this work, we propose a framework for designing certifiably robust classifiers that leverages latent structure in data representations. We first analyze the Gaussian mixture setting, deriving necessary and sufficient conditions for the existence of robust classifiers and constructing a classifier with a closed-form robustness certificate and generalization guarantees. Our main contribution is to show that exact structure is not required: we prove that if a pretrained encoder maps inputs to a latent distribution that is $\varepsilon$-close (in KL divergence) to a Gaussian mixture, then certified accuracy degrades gracefully, with an explicit bound relating robustness under the true and approximate distributions. This result enables the direct use of pretrained models without requiring exact distributional assumptions. Empirically, our method achieves state-of-the-art or competitive certified accuracy on CIFAR-10 and ImageNet, while maintaining strong clean performance and low computational overhead. Overall, our work establishes approximate latent structure as a practical and principled route to certifiable robustness.

</details>


### [107] [Not only where, But when: Temporal Scheduling for RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25381)
*Jinghao Zhang,Ruilin Li,Feng Zhao,Jiaqi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出在RLVR训练中引入时间维度的信用分配标准调度，通过优先优化特定策略行为对应的token并逐渐过渡到通用优化，实现更稳定高效的策略学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR训练中，全局标量奖励对不同token的同质化处理忽略了策略行为的异质性，而现有的信用分配方法（如token级优势重加权、选择性token优化）其分配标准在整个训练过程中固定不变，限制了策略的弹性演化。

Method: 提出时间维度上的信用分配标准调度（temporal scheduling），即优先针对特定策略行为对应的token进行优化，然后逐渐衰减过渡到通用优化。利用简单轨迹百分位数区分策略行为，结合时间调度机制实现动态信用分配。

Result: 标准优化在同时适应异质行为时会显著牺牲策略熵，而时间调度产生更健康的策略演化动态。在数学和通用推理基准上均取得一致性改进。

Conclusion: 信用分配标准的调度时机（when）与分配位置（where）同样重要，时间调度是RLVR训练中一个有前景的优化维度。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become a core technique for post-training of Large Language Models (LLMs). While policy optimization is driven by all sampled tokens under a globally broadcast scalar reward, the heterogeneous policy behaviors exhibited along trajectories are largely overlooked without differentiation. Existing works address this by credit allocation, including token-level advantage reweighting, and selective token optimization, however, the allocation criterion are principally stagnant throughout training, limiting resilient policy evolution. In this work, we argue that \textit{when} learning signals are scheduled can be as important as \textit{where} they are allocated across tokens, and introduce the temporal dimension that scheduling the credit allocation criteria over the course of RLVR optimization. We find that prioritizing targeted tokens emphasized with specific policy behaviors, and gradually attenuating toward general optimization leads to more stable and efficient learning dynamics. Furthermore, we show that simple trajectory percentiles provide a natural perspective for distinguishing policy behaviors, and works effectively with temporal scheduling. Our analysis reveals that standard optimization substantially sacrifices policy entropy when simultaneously accommodating heterogeneous behaviors, whereas temporal scheduling yields healthier policy evolution dynamics. Experiments across mathematical and general reasoning benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements, suggesting that temporal scheduling constitutes a promising optimization dimension.

</details>


### [108] [ViroBench: Benchmarking Nucleotide Foundation Models on Viral Genomics Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25388)
*Dongxin Ye,Fang Hu,Han Hu,Shu Hu,Yang Tan,Wanli Ouyang,Stan Z. Li,Jie Cui,Nanqing Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ViroBench是首个针对病毒基因组学的核苷酸基础模型(NFM)综合基准，涵盖18个场景、4类任务，评估66个模型后得出三个关键结论：NFMs在系统发育和时间偏移下性能下降；生成任务中统计似然与功能有效性脱钩带来生物安全风险；预训练数据的分类多样性比参数规模更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 病毒基因组分析对生物医学至关重要，但核苷酸基础模型(NFMs)领域缺乏统一的病毒基因组学标准化基准，且现有研究未充分考虑生物安全约束。

Method: 构建ViroBench基准，涵盖生物学理解与潜在生物安全风险两个关键维度，包含4类任务类型下的18个多样化场景，并对66个不同架构的NFM进行全面评估。

Result: 1) NFMs在系统发育和时间偏移下生物学理解性能下降，外推能力弱；2) 生成任务中统计似然与生物学功能有效性脱钩，存在潜在生物安全风险；3) 预训练数据的分类多样性比参数规模更关键，轻量级模型在多样化数据上训练可获得67.5%的性能提升。

Conclusion: ViroBench为病毒核苷酸基础模型提供了可解释、诊断性的评估与可复现的测量框架，有助于推动社区发展并加强生物安全监管。

Abstract: Nucleotide sequences constitute the fundamental genetic basis of biological systems, rendering viral genomic analysis critical for biomedical advancement. Despite progress in biological foundation models, specifically nucleotide foundation models (NFMs), the field lacks a unified standard for viral genomics to facilitate community development and enforce biosecurity constraints. To address this, we introduce ViroBench, the first comprehensive and large-scale benchmark specifically designed for NFMs in viral settings. ViroBench evaluates models across two critical dimensions: biological understanding and latent biosecurity risk, covering 18 diverse scenarios within 4 task types. Extensive evaluation of 66 NFMs across diverse architectures yields three critical conclusions. Firstly, NFMs exhibit a performance degradation in biological understanding under phylogenetic and temporal shifts, indicating weak extrapolation capabilities. Secondly, generation tasks reveal a decoupling between statistical likelihood and biological functional validity, posing latent biosecurity risks. Thirdly, controlled ablation studies reveal that taxonomic diversity in pretraining data outweighs parameter scale. Specifically, a lightweight baseline trained on diverse data achieves a 67.5% performance gain over its original model. Overall, ViroBench provides interpretable, diagnostic evaluations and a reproducible measurement framework for future research on viral nucleotide foundation models. The datasets and code are publicly available at https://github.com/QIANJINYDX/ViroBench.

</details>


### [109] [EMA-Nesterov: Stabilizing Nesterov's Lookahead for Accelerated Deep Learning Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25395)
*Chung-Yiu Yau,Dawei Li,Athanasios Glentis,Valentyn Boreiko,Hoi-To Wai,Mingyi Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EMA-Nesterov方法，用指数移动平均(EMA)替代标准Nesterov动量中的短视一步前瞻方向，通过低通滤波捕捉优化轨迹的低频趋势，在语言模型预训练等深度学习中比现有前瞻方法更稳定有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Nesterov前瞻加速方法在深度学习训练中因随机梯度噪声和非凸损失景观而变得不可靠，其依赖的短视单步更新信号本质上是噪声的，导致外推方向不稳定。作者希望利用优化轨迹的低频趋势而非噪声的一步更新来实现有效的加速。

Method: 将标准Nesterov前瞻方向替换为参数更新的指数移动平均(EMA)，通过低通滤波器捕获并利用训练轨迹的演化趋势，同时通过EMA的几何加权结构保持对渐进变化的适应性。

Result: 在凸问题中保留了类似Nesterov加速梯度法的理论加速收敛率；在语言模型预训练中，EMA-Nesterov在多种基础优化器（Adam、SOAP、Muon及NanoGPT上SOTA优化器）上均适用，且优于先前的前瞻方法。

Conclusion: EMA-Nesterov通过避免短视前瞻的不稳定性和长视前瞻的非自适应性，在深度学习优化中取得了更好的性能。

Abstract: Lookahead-based acceleration methods, such as Nesterov's momentum, are widely used in optimization, but they often become unreliable in deep learning training mainly due to stochastic gradient noise and non-convex loss landscapes. In particular, standard lookahead relies on short-horizon update signals (e.g., differences between consecutive iterates), which are inherently noisy and can lead to unstable extrapolation directions. This work revisits Nesterov's acceleration from a trajectory perspective and argues that effective acceleration in deep learning should harness the low-frequency trends of optimization trajectories rather than extrapolating noisy one-step updates. Leveraging this insight, we propose EMA-Nesterov, a simple modification that replaces the standard Nesterov's lookahead direction with an exponential moving average (EMA) of parameter updates. This yields a stabilized lookahead direction that captures and harnesses the evolving trend of the training trajectory through a low-pass filter, while remaining adaptive to progressive changes via the geometric weighting structure of EMA. We show that EMA-Nesterov retains a theoretical accelerated convergence rate in convex problems that is analogous to Nesterov's accelerated gradient method. Furthermore, we provide empirical evidence on language model pre-training to verify that EMA-Nesterov is broadly applicable across a range of fine-tuned base optimizers, including Adam, SOAP, Muon, as well as complex optimizers that achieve state-of-the-art performance on optimization benchmarks (NanoGPT). Compared to prior lookahead methods, EMA-Nesterov achieves better performance by avoiding the instability of short-horizon lookahead and the non-adaptivity of long-horizon lookahead.

</details>


### [110] [Autoregression-Free Neural Operators for Time-Dependent PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25413)
*Jiaquan Zhang,Caiyan Qin,Haoyu Bian,Libin Cai,Yi Lu,Chaoning Zhang,Wei Dong,Yuanfang Guo,Yang Yang,Hen Tao Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自回归自由神经算子(AFNO)，通过将PDE时间演化映射到潜空间并利用流匹配学习连续时间向量场，避免自回归 rollout，实现长期稳定预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于神经算子的时间依赖PDE求解方法使用自回归 rollout 在物理场空间进行预测，导致长期预测中误差累积严重，且缺乏连续时间建模能力。
