Method: 将PDE时间演化映射到潜空间，利用流匹配(flow matching)学习潜空间中的连续时间向量场，通过显式条件化物理参数捕捉不同参数配置下的动力学行为，完全避免自回归 rollout。

Result: 在6个PDE基准上的实验表明，AFNO相比基线方法显著提升了长期预测稳定性，并持续降低了 rollout 误差。

Conclusion: AFNO通过潜空间连续时间建模和避免自回归策略，有效解决了神经算子在长期PDE预测中的误差累积问题。

Abstract: Neural operators learn mappings from function-dependent inputs to solutions, providing an effective framework for solving partial differential equations (PDEs). For time-dependent PDEs, existing methods typically perform long-horizon prediction through autoregressive rollout directly in high-dimensional physical field spaces, where each predicted state is recursively fed back as the input for the next step. Although effective for short-term prediction, this autoregressive rollout and the lack of continuous-time modeling lead to progressive error accumulation over long-horizon rollouts. In this work, we propose Autoregression-Free Neural Operators (AFNO), which map the time evolution of PDEs into a latent space and model continuous-time vector fields within it. AFNO uses flow matching to learn the latent vector field, thereby enabling continuous evolution over extended horizons, avoiding autoregressive rollout and capturing dynamics under varying parameter configurations through explicit conditioning on physical parameters. Theoretical analysis and extensive experiments on six PDEs demonstrate that AFNO improves long-horizon prediction stability and consistently reduces rollout errors compared with the baselines.

</details>


### [111] [SeqRoute: Global Budget-Aware Sequential LLM Routing via Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25424)
*Zhongling Xu,Shunan Zheng,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SeqRoute是一个将多轮LLM路由建模为有限时域马尔可夫决策过程并通过离线强化学习求解的框架，通过将剩余预算纳入状态空间、使用CQL训练、提出HBR数据增强技术以及动态λ-sweep机制，在全局预算约束下实现成本与质量的最优平衡，将破产率控制在1%以下并降低6.0-73.5%运营成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM路由框架将查询视为独立事件，忽略了真实用户会话的序列性质和全局计算预算约束，导致短视路由策略在早期交互中耗尽资源（预算破产），使后续更复杂的查询被迫使用不充分的模型。

Method: (1) 将多轮路由形式化为有限时域MDP，将剩余预算纳入状态空间；(2) 使用Conservative Q-Learning (CQL)进行离线强化学习训练；(3) 提出Hindsight Budget Relabeling (HBR)技术，通过在不同假设预算下回溯模拟历史轨迹，将10,000条原始会话扩展为238万条包含破产信号的转移数据；(4) 部署时采用动态λ-sweep机制实现零样本Pareto前沿导航。

Result: 降低运营成本6.0-73.5%同时保持或提升质量；破产率控制在1%以下；在整个Pareto前沿上严格优于行为克隆、预算感知启发式方法和静态基线。

Conclusion: SeqRoute通过离线强化学习结合预算感知的状态设计和数据增强技术，有效解决了全局预算约束下的多轮LLM路由问题，实现了成本与质量的最优权衡。

Abstract: Existing LLM routing frameworks treat queries as independent events, neglecting the sequential nature of real-world user sessions constrained by global computational budgets. This mismatch inevitably leads to budget bankruptcy: myopic routing policies exhaust resources on early interactions, forcing subsequent and often more complex queries onto inadequate models. We introduce SeqRoute, a framework that formulates multi-turn routing as a finite-horizon Markov Decision Process and solves it via offline reinforcement learning. By incorporating the remaining budget into the state space and training with Conservative Q-Learning (CQL), SeqRoute learns delayed gratification to strategically preserve resources for high-stakes turns later in the session. To overcome data starvation, we propose Hindsight Budget Relabeling (HBR). This technique retrospectively simulates historical trajectories under diverse hypothetical budgets, expanding 10,000 raw sessions into 2.38 million transitions enriched with critical bankruptcy signals. At deployment, a dynamic $λ$-sweep mechanism enables zero-shot navigation of the cost-quality Pareto frontier without retraining. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that SeqRoute reduces operational costs by 6.0-73.5% while maintaining or improving quality, and suppresses bankruptcy rates to under 1%, strictly dominating behavior cloning, budget-aware heuristics, and static baselines across the entire Pareto frontier.

</details>


### [112] [Rethinking Feature Alignment in Generalist Graph Anomaly Detection: A Relational Fingerprint-based Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25429)
*Yujing Liu,Yixin Liu,Yu Zheng,Alan Wee-Chung Liew,Xiaofeng Cao,Shirui Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对通用图异常检测中跨域特征语义对齐问题，提出基于关系指纹（ReFi）的通用GAD方法，通过语义感知的上下文-结构编码与SNR引导的域自适应机制，在14个数据集上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用GAD方法主要依赖PCA投影对齐不同数据域的异构特征，虽统一了特征维度但忽略了特征语义，导致模型无法学习可迁移的语义知识，在未见过的图上甚至出现负迁移。

Method: 提出ReFi-GAD方法：1) 设计关系指纹(ReFi)，从上下文和结构两个视角编码异常指示性线索，实现异构原始特征的语义对齐；2) 构建基于指纹的通用GAD模型，包含Transformer编码器捕获域不变知识，以及SNR引导的精化模块实现域特定自适应。

Result: 在14个数据集上的大量实验表明，ReFi-GAD显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 通过语义感知的关系指纹对齐代替维度对齐，结合域不变与域自适应的双模块设计，能有效解决通用图异常检测中的负迁移问题，提升跨域泛化能力。

Abstract: Generalist graph anomaly detection (GAD) aims to detect anomalies on unseen graphs without graph-specific retraining. Nevertheless, existing approaches primarily focus on aligning heterogeneous features across different data domains via PCA-based projection, which harmonizes feature dimensions ignores feature semantics. As a result, GAD models fail to learn transferable semantic knowledge, and even exhibit negative transfer on unseen graphs. To address this issue, we propose a Relational Fingerprint-based generalist GAD approach (ReFi-GAD for short), aligning heterogeneous raw features with a universal and semantics-aware Relational Fingerprint (ReFi) that encodes anomaly-indicative cues from both contextual and structural perspectives. Building on ReFi, we design a fingerprint-grounded generalist GAD model, which combines a transformer-based encoder to capture domain-invariant knowledge with an SNR-guided refinement module for domain-specific adaptation. Extensive experiments on 14 datasets demonstrate that ReFi-GAD significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [113] [Missing Pattern Recognized Diffusion Imputation Model for Missing Not At Random](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25439)
*Gyuwon Sim,Sumin Lee,Heesun Bae,Byeonghu Na,Doyun Kwon,Ju-Hee Hwang,Jae-Young Lim,Il-Chul Moon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于扩散模型的缺失数据插补框架PRDIM，通过显式捕捉缺失模式（MNAR）并在EM算法框架下最大化观测值和缺失掩码的联合分布似然，实现跨模态的鲁棒插补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界中的缺失数据往往是非随机缺失（MNAR），即缺失与否依赖于不可观测值本身，传统插补方法难以有效处理此类复杂缺失模式。

Method: PRDIM框架包含一个模式识别器来近似底层缺失模式，在期望最大化（EM）算法下迭代最大化观测值与缺失掩码的联合分布似然，利用扩散模型进行插补。

Result: 在跨多种数据模态（时间序列、图像等）的MNAR设置下，PRDIM consistently取得了优异的插补性能。

Conclusion: PRDIM通过显式建模缺失模式并结合EM算法，有效解决了MNAR场景下的数据插补问题，且具有良好的跨模态泛化能力。

Abstract: Missing data frequently arises across diverse domains, including time-series and image domains. In the real world, missing occurrences often depend on the unobservable values themselves, which are referred to as Missing Not at Random (MNAR). In this work, we introduce the Missing Pattern Recognized Diffusion Imputation Model (PRDIM), a novel framework that explicitly captures the missing pattern and precisely imputes unobserved values. PRDIM iteratively maximizes the likelihood of the joint distribution for observed values and missing mask under an Expectation-Maximization (EM) algorithm. In this sense, we first employ a pattern recognizer, which approximates the underlying missing pattern and provides guidance during every inference toward more plausible imputations with respect to the missing information. Through extensive experiments, we demonstrate that PRDIM consistently achieves strong imputation performance under MNAR settings across multiple data modalities.

</details>


### [114] [BigMac: Breaking the Pareto Frontier of Compute and Memory in Multimodal LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25451)
*Zili Zhang,Chengxu Yang,Shenglong Zhang,Chenyu Wang,Yufan Zhang,Tuo Dai,Zhouyang Li,Yuhong Ge,Chao Jin,Xin Jin,Yuliang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BigMac提出了一种新的多模态大语言模型训练流水线，通过嵌套编码器和生成器计算到原始LLM流水线中，形成依赖安全的嵌套流水线结构，将编码器和生成器的激活内存复杂度降至O(1)，同时保持LLM的激活内存复杂度不变，突破了计算效率与内存使用之间的帕累托边界，实现了1.08×-1.9×的训练加速且内存使用随批大小保持稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM训练系统受困于模型和数据异构性，在计算效率和内存使用之间存在帕累托边界，优化一方必然牺牲另一方，亟需一种能同时优化计算和内存效率的训练方案。

Method: BigMac的核心思想是将编码器和生成器的计算嵌套到原始LLM流水线中，形成依赖安全的嵌套流水线结构，从而将编码器和生成器的激活内存复杂度降至O(1)，同时保持LLM的激活内存复杂度不变。

Result: 在多个MLLM和训练负载上评估，BigMac相比基线系统实现了1.08×-1.9×的训练加速，且随着批大小增加，内存使用保持稳定。

Conclusion: BigMac突破了计算效率与内存使用之间的帕累托边界，能够在MLLM训练中同时优化计算和内存效率，为多模态大模型的高效训练提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Training multimodal large language models (MLLMs) is challenged by both model and data heterogeneity. Existing systems redesign the training pipeline to address these challenges, but remain bound by a Pareto frontier between compute and memory efficiency, improving one only at the expense of the other. We present BigMac, a new training pipeline for multimodal LLMs. The core idea of BigMac is to elegantly nest the encoder and generator computation into the original LLM pipeline, forming a dependency-safe nested pipeline structure. With this design, BigMac reduces the activation memory complexity of the encoder and generator to O(1) while keeping the activation memory complexity of the LLM unchanged. At the same time, it achieves the same computational efficiency as the idealized setting with unlimited memory. As a result, BigMac breaks the Pareto frontier between computational efficiency and memory usage, enabling simultaneous optimization of both computation and memory in MLLM training. We evaluate BigMac on multiple MLLMs and training workloads. Experimental results show that BigMac achieves a 1.08$\times$-1.9$\times$ training speedup over baseline systems while maintaining stable memory usage as batch size increases.

</details>


### [115] [From Simulation to Enaction: Post-trained language models recognize and react to their own generations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25459)
*Asvin G.,Jack Lindsey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现后训练（post-training）的语言模型能够隐式识别自身的生成（on-policy），表现为输出分布熵显著低于非自身生成（off-policy），并揭示其内部机制与输入惊讶度（input surprise）的因果调控有关；同时，模型也能通过显式语言报告区分自身生成，但与隐式识别分属不同机制路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型预训练阶段作为被动预测器，缺乏对自身输出后果进行建模的激励；而post-training改变了这一状况，模型在生成自身回答时能从中获益。本文旨在探究post-trained模型是否以及如何识别自身的on-policy生成。

Method: 通过对比on-policy与off-policy下的输出分布熵差异（发现on-policy熵低3-4倍），分析内部表示中"输入惊讶度"（模型对当前token的意外程度，由其先前预测决定）对输出熵的因果调制作用；通过开放提示实验观察后训练模型在首个输出token前对话题的不确定性坍塌现象，以及通过显式语言报告测试对比隐式与显式识别机制。

Result: 1) 跨模型家族和规模，on-policy输出分布熵比off-policy低3-4倍；2) 内部存在对输入惊讶度的表示，因果性地调制输出熵；3) 后训练模型在开放提示的首个输出token前已坍塌对即将生成话题的不确定性，若用不同话题预填充（prefill）会破坏该缓存意图，导致输出熵升高；4) 模型能通过显式语言报告区分on-policy与prefill，但该显式识别与隐式识别分属不同机制。

Conclusion: Post-trained模型具备隐式识别自身生成的能力，该能力编码于输出分布中，由输入惊讶度作为中间表示因果调控。同时，显式报告能力存在但机制独立，表明模型对on-policy的识别涉及多层次、多路径的认知过程。

Abstract: Language models are pretrained as passive predictors with no incentive to model the consequences of their own outputs. Post-training changes this: a model producing its own responses can benefit from recognizing that it is on-policy. We present evidence that post-trained models recognize their on-policy generations, and this recognition is implicitly encoded in their output distributions. In particular, on-policy output distribution entropy is 3--4$\times$ lower than off-policy entropy, across model families and size classes. We trace part of this effect to an internal representation of input surprise, tracking the unlikeliness of the most recent input token according to the model's prior predictions, that causally modulates output entropy. One example of these phenomena can be observed in response to open-ended prompts; post-trained models (unlike pretrained models) collapse their uncertainty over the topic of their upcoming response before the first output token; violating this cached intention with a different-topic prefill results in higher output entropy. We also tested whether models can distinguish on-policy contexts from prefills via explicit verbal report. We find that they can, but that interestingly, this explicit recognition routes through a different mechanism than implicit recognition.

</details>


### [116] [JacQuant: STE-Free Quantization-Aware Training via Learned Jacobian Surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25469)
*Kai Yi,Vignesh Vivekraja,Harshit Khaitan,Steven Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: JacQuant提出了一种基于学习模型局部参数敏感度替代物的QAT框架，替代了传统STE方法，在超低比特LLM量化中实现更高精度且计算开销可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统STE方法在量化边界处梯度不准确，训练不稳定且与低精度模型实际行为对齐差，亟需更精确的梯度估计方法。

Method: 学习一个轻量级（对角或块对角）、数据驱动的局部敏感度替代物，在方差缩减优化器内稳定和加速训练；兼容常见权重量化器和激活量化器，无需修改前向量化器。

Result: 在≤2比特的LLM基准上，JacQuant持续达到比STE-based QAT更高的准确率；在多种模型上的运行时分析表明，在实际分组大小下额外计算开销可忽略。

Conclusion: JacQuant是一种即插即用的QAT框架，理论上证明了非凸目标的收敛性和PL条件下的线性收敛率，并通过简单标定论证将学习到的敏感度与端到端输出保真度关联起来。

Abstract: Quantization-aware training (QAT) is widely deployed but typically relies on the Straight-Through Estimator (STE), which passes gradients through non-differentiable quantizers by fiat. This often makes training brittle near bin boundaries and weakly aligned with the actual behavior of the low-precision model. We introduce JacQuant, a QAT framework that learns a lightweight surrogate of the model's local sensitivity to parameter changes and uses it to stabilize and accelerate training within standard variance-reduced optimizers. The surrogate is inexpensive (diagonal or block-diagonal), data-driven, and compatible with common weight and activation quantizers. On code-preserving training phases, we prove convergence for non-convex objectives and obtain linear rates under a PL condition, and we relate the learned sensitivity to end-to-end output fidelity via a simple calibration argument. Across LLM benchmarks at $\leq 2$ bits, JacQuant consistently reaches higher accuracy than STE-based QAT, and the runtime analyses on various models show that the added cost remains negligible under practical group sizes. The method is drop-in and requires no changes to the forward quantizers; our empirical claims are scoped to ultra-low-bit LLM QAT.

</details>


### [117] [SafetyRepro: Configuration-Conditional Rank Instability on Alignment Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25492)
*Yanhang Li,Zhichao Fan,Zexin Zhuang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现，基于基础模型基准测试的成对模型比较（如“A比B更安全”）严重依赖于未明确指定的测试配置（harness choices），而这些配置选择足以翻转原有的严格排序结论。作者提出了一种有限包络命题，将可测量的成对分歧率与严格排序能否被配置对反转联系起来，并通过时间戳评估协议在多个对齐基准上验证，发现配置选择本身即可翻转成对比较结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基础模型基准测试在进行成对比较时，其结果高度依赖于未被充分指定的测试配置，导致“A比B更安全”这类定量结论缺乏稳健性，需要一种理论方法来量化并暴露这种不稳定性。

Method: 提出一个有限包络命题，将可测量的成对分歧率与严格排序是否存在配置对反转联系起来；并设计了一个带有时间戳提交的评估协议（commit-stamped evaluation protocol），在多个广泛引用的对齐基准上操作化该命题。

Result: 在测试的所有基准上，仅配置选择本身即可翻转成对比较的结论；该命题能够隔离出这种严格反转的失效模式。

Conclusion: 基准测试的配置选择对模型比较结论有决定性影响，必须明确规范化和记录这些选择，否则成对比较结论不可靠。

Abstract: Pairwise model comparisons drawn from foundation-model benchmarks ("A is safer than B") are read as quantitative verdicts but hinge on harness choices benchmark papers under-specify. We close one theory-benchmark loop on this primitive: a finite-envelope proposition tying a measurable pairwise-disagreement rate to whether the strict ordering admits a configuration-pair reversal, paired with a commit-stamped evaluation protocol that operationalises it on widely cited alignment benchmarks. On every benchmark we test, configuration choice alone can flip the pairwise verdict; the proposition isolates this strict-reversal failure mode.

</details>


### [118] [Accelerated Dynamic Importance Weighting with Versatile Divergence-Minimizing Estimators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25499)
*Tongtong Fang,Nan Lu,Gang Niu,Kenji Fukumizu,Masashi Sugiyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出加速动态重要性加权（ADIW）框架，通过轻量级投影梯度下降和统一散度最小化框架，在保持高性能的同时大幅提升联合分布偏移下深度学习的计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态重要性加权（DIW）方法存在两大局限：一是每小批量需将核均值匹配（KMM）优化问题求解至收敛，计算开销巨大；二是仅依赖KMM一种估计方法，而IW文献中存在多种基于不同散度度量的估计方法未被利用。

Method: ADIW采用两项关键改进：1）使用少量轻量级投影梯度下降更新，并从先前更新的权重热启动，显著提升效率；2）将DIW推广为统一的散度最小化框架，以即插即用方式支持多种权重估计方法（KL散度、平方距离、Wasserstein-1距离）。

Result: 在温和条件下建立了ADIW的收敛保证，实验结果表明ADIW在实现最先进IW性能的同时，效率大幅提升。

Conclusion: ADIW通过统一框架和高效优化策略，解决了DIW的计算瓶颈和方法单一性问题，为联合分布偏移下的深度学习提供了一种高效且通用的重要性加权方案。

Abstract: Importance weighting (IW) is a golden solver for joint distribution shift, where the joint distributions differ between the training and test data. To solve this problem, IW estimates test-to-training density ratios as importance weights and reweights the training losses accordingly. Recent advances in dynamic IW (DIW) integrate weight estimation into model training, enabling scalable IW for deep models and achieving strong performance on large modern datasets. Despite its promise, DIW remains limited in two aspects. First, it incurs substantial computational overhead by solving a kernel mean matching (KMM)-induced optimization problem to convergence in every mini-batch. Second, it relies solely on KMM for weight estimation, whereas the IW literature contains diverse estimation methods based on different divergence measures. In this paper, we propose accelerated DIW (ADIW), a unified and efficient IW framework for deep learning under joint distribution shift. ADIW performs a few lightweight projected gradient descent updates that warm-start from previously updated weights, substantially improving efficiency. Moreover, ADIW generalizes DIW into a unified divergence-minimization framework that supports diverse weight-estimation methods in a plug-and-play manner, including those based on the Kullback-Leibler divergence, squared distance, and Wasserstein-1 distance. We establish convergence guarantees for ADIW under mild conditions, and empirical results demonstrate that ADIW achieves state-of-the-art IW performance while being substantially more efficient.

</details>


### [119] [SAE-FD: Sparse Autoencoder Feature Distillation for Continual Learning of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25525)
*Mingxu Zhang,Yuhan Li,Lujundong Li,Dazhong Shen,Hui Xiong,Ying Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SAFD方法，利用稀疏自编码器将密集表示分解为稀疏过完备基，减少特征叠加导致的表示纠缠，从而在持续学习中实现更具针对性的正则化，在三个模型架构的基准测试上优于现有方法，平均准确率高达52.70%，后向迁移仅-0.46。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习中基于正则化的方法在权重、梯度或输出空间中约束模型更新时，面临特征叠加（多个概念编码在重叠维度）的问题，难以在不阻碍新任务学习的前提下选择性保护已有知识。

Method: 提出SAFD（稀疏自编码器特征蒸馏），利用预训练稀疏自编码器将密集激活分解为稀疏过完备基，在稀疏特征空间中进行表示锚定和蒸馏，减少表示纠缠，实现更精准的正则化。

Result: 在两个持续学习基准和三个模型架构上，SAFD一致优于现有正则化方法，平均准确率最高达52.70%，后向迁移仅为-0.46。

Conclusion: 在稀疏特征空间中进行正则化可有效缓解特征叠加问题，在保护已有知识的同时减少对新任务学习的干扰，为持续学习中的表示解耦提供了新方向。

Abstract: Continual learning enables large language models to adapt to evolving tasks without retraining from scratch, yet catastrophic forgetting remains a central obstacle. Among continual learning methods, regularization-based approaches are widely used to constrain model updates and reduce forgetting, operating in weight space, gradient space, or output space. However, these dense representation spaces suffer from feature superposition, where multiple concepts are encoded in overlapping dimensions, making it difficult to selectively protect previously learned knowledge without impeding new-task learning. To address this issue, we propose \method (Sparse Autoencoder Feature Distillation), which anchors model representations in the sparse feature space of a pre-trained Sparse Autoencoder, where dense activations are decomposed into a sparse overcomplete basis that reduces representational entanglement, enabling more targeted regularization with less interference to new-task learning. Experiments on two continual learning benchmarks across three model architectures show that \method consistently outperforms existing regularization-based methods, achieving up to 52.70% average accuracy with only -0.46 backward transfer.

</details>


### [120] [Learning Permutation from Structure Without Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25551)
*Ran Eisenberg,Ofir Lindenbaum*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种熵自适应Gumbel-Sinkhorn方法，通过局部调节温度来处理置换学习中不同赋值的不确定性差异，在排序、拼图重建和路由等任务中提升了训练稳定性和置换质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Gumbel-Sinkhorn方法使用单一全局温度控制所有赋值的锐化与扩散，无法处理学习中不同赋值置信度非均匀变化的问题，导致大规模任务下训练不稳定。

Method: 提出熵自适应Gumbel-Sinkhorn，根据每个赋值的局部不确定性动态调节温度，使得置信度高的赋值提前离散化，同时保留不确定性高区域的探索能力。

Result: 在排序、拼图重建和路由任务中，相比固定温度基线，自适应熵控制在问题规模和赋值歧义增加时显著提升了训练稳定性和最终置换质量。

Conclusion: 局部温度调节机制能有效应对置换学习中非均匀的不确定性，是一种更稳定、更高质量的置换学习方法。

Abstract: Many learning problems require uncovering a hidden ordering that reveals structure in unordered data, such as monotonicity in sorting or spatial continuity in jigsaw reconstruction. In these settings, permutations can be learned as latent operators by optimizing objectives defined directly on the reordered output, often without access to ground-truth orderings. Differentiable relaxations such as Gumbel-Sinkhorn make this approach practical by approximating permutation matrices with doubly stochastic matrices. However, learning from structure without supervision induces a non-uniform uncertainty: some assignments become confident early, while others remain ambiguous. Existing methods control this process using a single global temperature, forcing all assignments to sharpen or diffuse simultaneously and leading to instability at scale. We introduce an entropy-adaptive formulation of Gumbel-Sinkhorn that locally modulates temperature based on assignment uncertainty. This allows confident assignments to discretize early while preserving exploration where uncertainty remains. Across sorting and jigsaw reconstruction tasks and in routing-style settings, adaptive entropy control improves training stability and final permutation quality relative to fixed-temperature baselines, particularly as problem size and assignment ambiguity increase.

</details>


### [121] [RotMoLE: Enhancing Mixture of Low-Rank Experts through Rotational Gating Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25565)
*Mengyang Sun,Maochuan Dou,Tao Feng,Dan Zhang,Yihao Wang,Junpeng Liu,Yifan Zhu,Jie Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对MoE-LoRA传统门控仅对标量重加权的局限，本文提出RotMoLE框架，通过引入旋转门控机制对每个选中的低秩专家进行旋转操作，增强专家利用与 specialization，在多任务和多语言场景下验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE-LoRA的门控机制仅对选中的专家施加标量重加权，限制了低秩适配器的表示能力和泛化能力，难以充分学习复杂多样化的知识。

Method: 提出RotMoLE框架，利用MoE-LoRA中的低秩结构，在传统门控基础上增加旋转门控（rotation gate），对每个选中的专家执行旋转操作而非简单缩放。

Result: 在复杂多任务和多语言训练场景下的实证结果表明，RotMoLE能够实现更优的专家利用与 specialization，有效提升了模型性能。

Conclusion: 旋转门控机制是MoE-LoRA中一种有效的专家交互方式，尤其在专家候选数量有限时，能更好地学习多样化数据。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) are commonly fine-tuned to handle domain-specific tasks before being applied to vertical applications, adapting them to complex scenarios with diverse specialized knowledge remains challenging. Meanwhile, Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architecture has risen as a crucial paradigm for training LLMs, and some recent works have also incorporated MoE into Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT) to propose the Mixture of Low-rank Experts (MoE-LoRA), to enhance the power of low-rank adapters for learning complicated knowledge. However, conventional gating mechanisms in MoE typically apply only a scalar reweighing to selected experts, thereby limiting their underlying capacity of representation and generalization. Motivated and enabled by the low-rank structures in MoE-LoRA, we propose RotMoLE, a specialized MoE framework for low-rank experts featuring an additional rotation gate. Beyond simple scaling, RotMoLE implements a rotation mechanism for each selected expert, enabling superior expert exploitation and specialization for learning diverse data, especially when expert candidates are limited. Empirical results on complex multi-task and multilingual training scenarios validate our effectiveness.

</details>


### [122] [Learning Latent Dynamical Causal Processes for Single-Cell Perturbation Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25581)
*Wenkang Jiang,Yuhang Liu,Erdun Gao,Ehsan Abbasnejad,Lina Yao,Javen Qinfeng Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种潜在动态因果生成模型（CITE-VAE），用于单细胞扰动预测，同时捕捉潜在细胞程序、扰动条件机制和时间演化，并在可识别性理论分析基础上实现分布外泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在两方面的不足：潜在因果方法将扰动效应视为静态结果，而时序模型虽描述基因表达随时间的变化，但未能显式恢复驱动这些变化的潜在因果生成机制。实际上，扰动效应既是潜在的（通过未观测的细胞程序起作用）又是动态的（这些程序状态随时间演化）。因此需要联合建模潜在细胞程序、扰动条件机制和时间演化的统一框架。

Method: 提出CITE-VAE：一个潜在动态因果生成模型，联合建模潜在细胞程序、扰动条件机制和时间演化；提供可识别性分析，证明在适当条件下潜在因果变量可恢复至标准等价类；基于此理论指导设计VAE框架，从单细胞测序数据中恢复潜在细胞程序及其扰动驱动动态。

Result: 在Causal-3DIdent合成数据集上验证了理论结果和方法的有效性；在真实CRISPR单细胞扰动数据上，相比现有最先进基线方法，对未见扰动的泛化能力有显著提升。

Conclusion: 通过联合建模潜在因果结构和时间动态，CITE-VAE能够更好地恢复细胞响应扰动的生成机制，显著提升了单细胞扰动预测中分布外泛化的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Single-cell perturbation prediction aims to infer how cells respond to unseen interventions and to achieve out-of-distribution (OOD) generalization, providing a computational route to understanding how perturbations reshape cellular programs over time. Existing machine learning methods have made important progress, but typically capture only one side of the response. Latent causal approaches seek mechanisms that support generalization and interpretation, yet often treat perturbation effects as static outcomes. Temporal models describe how gene expression changes across time, but usually do not explicitly recover the latent causal generative mechanisms driving these changes. In practice, perturbation effects are both latent and dynamical: interventions act through unobserved cellular programs, whose states evolve over time and give rise to observed expression profiles. Motivated by this view, we propose a latent dynamical causal generative model for single-cell perturbation data that jointly captures latent cellular programs, perturbation-conditioned mechanisms, and temporal evolution. We further provide an identifiability analysis showing that, under suitable conditions, the latent causal variables are recoverable up to standard equivalence classes. Guided by this analysis, we develop CITE-VAE, a learning framework for recovering latent cellular programs and their perturbation-driven dynamics from single-cell sequencing data. Experiments on Causal-3DIdent validate the theoretical results and the effectiveness of the proposed method in controlled settings. Additional experiments on real-world CRISPR-based single-cell perturbation data show improved generalization to unseen perturbations compared with state-of-the-art baselines, highlighting the practical robustness of our approach.

</details>


### [123] [Extreme Region Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25582)
*Changyu Chen,Xiting Wang,Rui Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ERPD框架，通过两阶段策略解耦强化学习中的样本效率与KL效率：第一阶段在固定数据上进行弱约束的离策略优化以最大化提取训练信号，第二阶段将这些信号蒸馏回基础策略并施加信任域约束，从而在数学推理任务上取得优于纯在线策略的性能，且KL散度显著更小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的强化学习面临样本效率与渐近性能之间的根本性权衡：严格的在策略方法单次更新后丢弃轨迹造成样本浪费，而离策略复用导致分布偏移，现有信任域方法主要通过保守优化来缓解这一问题，但丰富的训练信号往往未被充分利用。

Method: ERPD两阶段框架：第一阶段在固定数据上进行弱约束的离策略优化，最大化提取训练信号；第二阶段将这些信号（token级监督）蒸馏回基础策略，在信任域约束下过滤有害漂移同时保留有用信号。

Result: （1）过度多步优化带来快速初始增益，但过多更新导致轨迹概率偏离和熵崩溃，性能早期停滞；（2）收紧KL约束只降低上限而不解决退化问题；（3）ERPD蒸馏策略在显著更小的KL散度下达到相当或更优的性能；（4）即使在激进优化未产生更强策略时，退化教师也能通过替代信号构建策略提供有效监督。

Conclusion: ERPD有效解耦了样本效率与KL效率，验证了第一阶段的大量KL散度主要花费在非必要的漂移而非真正的改进上。该方法在数学推理任务上对强基础模型（在线策略训练停滞时）有效，且在使用弱教师时也能带来可靠的改进。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning for large language models faces a fundamental trade-off between sample efficiency and asymptotic performance: strictly on-policy methods discard trajectories after a single update, while off-policy reuse introduces distribution mismatch that existing trust-region techniques mitigate primarily by enforcing conservative optimization, often leaving rich training signals underutilized. To investigate this, we perform extensive off-policy updates on fixed data. Our experiments reveal that aggressive multi-step optimization brings rapid initial gains, but excessive updates cause trajectory probabilities to deviate and entropy to collapse, with performance plateauing early. Tightening KL constraints merely lowers the ceiling without resolving the degradation. This motivates Extreme Region Policy Distillation (ERPD), a two-stage framework that decouples sample efficiency from KL efficiency. The first stage performs weakly constrained off-policy optimization on fixed data to maximally extract training signals. The resulting policy provides token-level supervision. In the second stage, we distill these signals into the base policy under trust-region constraints, filtering harmful drift while preserving useful signals. The distilled policy achieves comparable or better performance with substantially smaller KL divergence, indicating that much of the first-stage divergence was spent on unnecessary drift rather than genuine improvement. Crucially, ERPD accommodates both strong and weak teachers: when aggressive optimization yields no stronger policy, even degenerate teachers provide effective supervision via alternative signal construction strategies. We validate ERPD on mathematical reasoning, showing gains for strong base models where on-policy training plateaus, and reliable improvements with weak teachers.

</details>


### [124] [Generalized Evidential Deep Learning: From a Bayesian Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25599)
*Yuanye Liu,Yibo Gao,Yuanyang Chen,Xiahai Zhuang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出广义证据深度学习（GEDL），在贝叶斯框架下统一了现有EDL变体，并通过理论分析和实验验证了其在分类、不确定性估计和OOD检测上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EDL变体虽多，但缺乏统一的理论基础；本文旨在建立EDL的贝叶斯理论框架，厘清各变体间的联系与区别。

Method: 将EDL置于广义贝叶斯框架下，涵盖先验设定、后验更新和训练目标；通过渐近分析将证据不确定性解释为贝叶斯分布不确定性；提出GEDL框架，显式解耦各组件并系统关联现有变体。

Result: 在分类、不确定性估计和OOD检测任务上，GEDL取得了与现有方法相当的结果，并具有理论支撑。

Conclusion: GEDL为EDL提供了一个统一且可扩展的理论框架，有助于更系统地理解和改进证据深度学习。

Abstract: Evidential Deep Learning (EDL) has emerged as an efficient, sampling-free strategy for uncertainty estimation. A series of EDL variants have been proposed to address specific limitations of the original framework, achieving notable success. However, the underlying theoretical structure of EDL and the relationships among these variants have received limited systematic investigation. In this work, we establish a principled theoretical foundation for EDL by interpreting it within a generalized Bayesian framework that includes prior specification, posterior update, and training objective. We further characterize evidential uncertainty from a Bayesian distributional uncertainty viewpoint, established via asymptotic analysis. Building on this perspective, we further propose Generalized Evidential Deep Learning (GEDL), a unified and extensible framework that explicitly disentangles the roles of individual components and systematically relates GEDL to existing variants. Extensive experiments demonstrate that GEDL yields comparable results on classification, uncertainty estimation and OOD detections, with theoretical grounding.

</details>


### [125] [Courtroom Analogy: New Perspective on Uncertainty-Aware Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25616)
*Taeseong Yoon,Heeyoung Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出法庭类比框架，将不确定性感知分类建模为类别特定辩护人之间的结构化辩论，每个辩护人形成狄利克雷分布意见，通过输入依赖的合理性权重聚合得出最终判决。基于此提出MoDEX（混合狄利克雷专家）架构，实现高效、可解释的不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单次不确定性量化方法主要关注增强分布的表示能力，但对预测不确定性的结构和聚合方式缺乏深入洞察，导致可解释性较弱。

Method: 提出法庭类比框架：每个类别辩护人形成概率意见（狄利克雷分布），其浓度参数分解为共享证据和类别特定辩护两部分；通过输入依赖的合理性权重聚合意见，形成结构化狄利克雷混合分布。MoDEX以单次前馈方式预测上述法庭参数。

Result: 在多个基准上达到最先进的不确定性量化性能，生成具有明确语义的可解释不确定性估计。

Conclusion: MoDEX具备良好的理论性质，能够显式建模不确定性聚合过程，兼顾表达能力和可解释性，在UQ任务上优于现有方法。

Abstract: Single-pass uncertainty quantification (UQ) methods for classification represent uncertainty by predicting a tractable distribution over the class probability vector. While existing approaches primarily focus on enhancing the expressiveness of this distribution, they often provide limited insight into how predictive uncertainty is structured and aggregated, resulting in weak interpretability. We introduce the courtroom analogy, which conceptualizes uncertainty-aware classification as a structured debate among class-specific advocates. Each advocate forms a probabilistic opinion, and a final verdict is reached by aggregating these opinions using input-dependent plausibility weights. In this framework, each advocate's opinion is modeled as a Dirichlet distribution whose concentration parameter is decomposed into shared evidence and class-specific advocacy. This yields a structured mixture of Dirichlet distributions with semantically interpretable parameters. To instantiate this formulation, we propose Mixture of Dirichlet EXperts (MoDEX), a single-pass neural architecture that predicts the courtroom parameters, enabling efficient and expressive UQ while explicitly modeling uncertainty aggregation. We demonstrate that MoDEX enjoys strong theoretical properties and achieves state-of-the-art UQ performance across diverse benchmarks, yielding interpretable uncertainty estimates with meaningful semantics.

</details>


### [126] [Opportunistic Target Selection: Early Directional Commitment for Query-Efficient Black-Box Adversarial Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25663)
*Florent Tariolle,Florian Yger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Opportunistic Target Selection (OTS) 方法，将无目标黑盒攻击在早期动态切换为目标攻击，有效解决类别漂移问题，在随机搜索攻击上显著提升成功率，但在梯度估计攻击和对抗训练模型上效果有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑盒无目标攻击仅最小化真实类别的置信度，会导致类别漂移（class drift）：扰动在特征空间中漫无目的地游走，无法锁定特定对抗类别，浪费查询在弥散、无方向的进展上。

Method: 提出 Opportunistic Target Selection (OTS)，一个轻量级包装器，在攻击轨迹早期将无目标攻击切换到有目标攻击目标，锁定当前置信度最高的非真实类别。OTS 不需要修改底层攻击架构、不需要梯度信息、也不需要预先知道目标类别。

Result: 在三个基于分数的攻击（SimBA、Square Attack with cross-entropy loss、Bandits）和五个标准 ImageNet 分类器上验证（4,500 次运行）。随机搜索攻击上，OTS 紧密跟踪 oracle 性能，在 ResNet-50 上成功率达 +27 pp 提升，审查平均迭代次数相对减少 43%。梯度估计攻击（Bandits）和 margin loss 攻击上 OTS 冗余（负面结果）。对抗训练模型上，双峰难度分布消除了 OTS 的适用场景。

Conclusion: OTS 在随机搜索攻击中作为 margin loss 的轻量替代有效提升性能，但在已有 margin 效应或数据分布极端的情况下作用有限。

Abstract: Black-box adversarial attacks that minimize only the ground-truth confidence suffer from class drift: perturbations wander through the feature space without committing to a specific adversarial class, wasting queries on diffuse, undirected progress. We introduce Opportunistic Target Selection (OTS), a lightweight wrapper that switches an untargeted attack to a targeted objective early in its trajectory, locking onto whichever non-true class currently leads. OTS requires no architectural modification to the underlying attack, no gradient access, and no a priori target-class knowledge.
  We validate OTS on three score-based attacks (SimBA, Square Attack with cross-entropy loss, and Bandits) across five standard ImageNet classifiers (4,500 runs). On random-search attacks, OTS closely tracks oracle performance, with gains up to +27 pp in success rate and 43% relative reduction in censored-mean iterations on ResNet-50. On gradient-estimation attacks (Bandits) and attacks with margin loss, OTS is redundant, a negative result that reinforces our interpretation of OTS as a margin-loss surrogate. On adversarially-trained models, a bimodal difficulty distribution eliminates the regime where targeting helps.

</details>


### [127] [Don't Retrain, Just Reuse: Recovering Dual-Target Molecules from Single-Target Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25681)
*Qingyuan Zeng,Pengxiang Cai,Zixin Guan,Ziyang Chen,Anglin Liu,Lang Qin,Xinyao Lai,Jintai Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出REUSE框架，通过层级进化搜索冻结的单目标扩散模型的输入空间，实现无需修改模型参数或去噪过程的双目标分子生成，在双靶点亲和力上相比现有方法提升20.9个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双靶点分子生成方法要么需要重新训练生成器（成本高、不稳定），要么需要在扩散过程中进行干预（目标平衡敏感、更新方向竞争），因此探索一种保持预训练先验不变的生成器替代方案是必要的。

Method: 将双靶点候选分子生成形式化为约束多目标优化问题，提出REUSE框架：一种层级进化输入空间搜索方法，结合配对条件探索和结构化多阶段选择，以强制执行双靶点亲和力、化学质量和多样性。

Result: 与修改扩散过程的方法相比，REUSE在双靶点亲和力和平衡性上持续改进，在Dual High Affinity指标上相比最强基线提升20.9个百分点，同时保持有竞争力的分子质量。

Conclusion: 在冻结的单目标扩散模型输入空间中搜索可以有效生成双靶点分子，避免了修改模型参数或去噪动力学的需要，为多靶点药物设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Designing a single molecule that modulates two targets is a promising strategy for polypharmacology, but it remains substantially harder than standard single-target generation because one candidate must satisfy two binding requirements while preserving drug-likeness and synthesizability. Existing dual-target generative methods typically introduce dual-target capability by either retraining the generator or intervening in the diffusion process during sampling. The former can be costly and difficult to stabilize when dual-target supervision is sparse, while the latter may be sensitive to denoising-time target balancing and competing update directions. These limitations motivate a generator-preserving alternative that keeps the pretrained prior intact: can dual-target candidates instead be recovered from the input space of a frozen single-target diffusion model, without modifying its parameters or denoising dynamics? We formulate this task as a constrained multi-objective optimization problem and propose REUSE, a hierarchical evolutionary input-space search framework that combines pair-conditioned exploration with structured multi-stage selection to enforce dual-target affinity, chemical quality, and diversity. Experiments show that, compared with methods that modify the diffusion process, REUSE consistently improves dual-target affinity and balance, achieving a 20.9-percentage-point gain in Dual High Affinity over the strongest prior baseline while maintaining competitive molecular quality.

</details>


### [128] [Latent Representation Alignment for Offline Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25740)
*Hyungkyu Kang,Byeongchan Kim,Min-hwan Oh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LAVL算法，通过潜在对齐价值学习解决离线目标条件强化学习中价值函数的错误泛化问题，在OGBench的22个数据集上20个达到最优，尤其在长程任务和轨迹拼接任务中表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线GCRL中学习可靠的目标条件价值函数在长程任务中面临挑战，作者识别出价值函数的错误泛化是根本瓶颈，需要引入合适的归纳偏置来解决。

Method: 提出Latent-Aligned Value Learning (LAVL)，将基于潜在表示的价值泛化与层次化规划集成到统一框架中。

Result: 在OGBench的22个数据集上，LAVL在20个上取得最优性能，在长程任务和轨迹拼接数据集上显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: LAVL通过潜在对齐价值学习有效缓解了价值函数的错误泛化问题，为离线GCRL提供了一种统一且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Offline goal-conditioned reinforcement learning (GCRL) provides a practical framework for obtaining goal-reaching policies from fixed datasets. However, learning a reliable goal-conditioned value function in long-horizon tasks remains challenging. In this paper, we identify erroneous generalization in goal-conditioned value functions as a fundamental bottleneck, and demonstrate that appropriate inductive bias in the value function is crucial for addressing the bottleneck. Building on these findings, we propose Latent-Aligned Value Learning (LAVL), an offline GCRL algorithm that integrates latent-representation-based value generalization with hierarchical planning in a unified framework. Extensive experiments on OGBench demonstrate that LAVL consistently outperforms existing offline GCRL methods, achieving the highest performance on 20 out of 22 datasets. Notably, LAVL exhibits strong performance in long-horizon tasks and trajectory stitching datasets, where prior methods suffer significant performance degradation. Our code is available at https://github.com/oh-lab/LAVL.git.

</details>


### [129] [MDGMIX: Boundary-Aware Subgraph Mixing for Multi-Domain Graph Pre-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25771)
*Ziyu Zheng,Yaming Yang,Ziyu Guan,Wei Zhao,Xinyan Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MDGMIX框架，通过边界感知子图混合与层次化判别解决多领域图预训练中的数据冗余问题，在少样本分类任务中实现高效且性能优异的知识迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨领域图预训练方法依赖联合训练所有源域图数据，计算成本高昂，且并非所有源域数据都对迁移有同等贡献，存在数据冗余问题。

Method: MDGMIX包含三个核心组件：(1) 边界感知子图混合：选择边界节点构建具有挑战性的混合域子图；(2) 层次化判别：粗粒度域判别与细粒度域分解损失解耦共享模式与域特定模式；(3) 轻量级提示加权机制：在适配阶段实现源域知识迁移。

Result: 在少样本分类任务中，MDGMIX持续优于强基线方法，同时展现出更优的时间和内存效率。

Conclusion: 通过实证发现多领域图预训练中存在显著的数据冗余，并据此设计的MDGMIX框架能够有效降低计算成本并提升跨域迁移性能。

Abstract: Multi-domain graph pre-training is a crucial step in constructing foundational graph models with cross-domain generalization capabilities. However, existing methods predominantly rely on jointly training all source domain graphs, resulting in high computational costs. Furthermore, it remains unclear whether all source domain graph data contribute equally to effective transfer. This paper empirically reveals significant data redundancy in multi-domain graph pre-training. Based on this finding, we propose the Multi-domain Graph Pre-training Framework, MDGMIX, which combines boundary-aware subgraph mixing with hierarchical discrimination. By selecting boundary nodes to construct challenging mixed-domain subgraphs, MDGMIX employs coarse-grained domain discrimination and fine-grained domain decomposition losses to decouple shared patterns from domain-specific patterns. During adaptation, MDGMIX employs a lightweight prompt weighting mechanism to transfer source domain knowledge. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MDGMIX consistently outperforms strong baselines in few-shot classification tasks while exhibiting superior time and memory efficiency. The code is available at: https://github.com/zhengziyu77/MDGMIX.

</details>


### [130] [On the Benefits of Free Exploration for Regret Minimization in Multi-Armed Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25789)
*Yunlong Hou,Zixin Zhong,Vincent Y. F. Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了随机多臂老虎机中带有免费探索预算的遗憾最小化问题，提出了一种两阶段算法UFE-KLUCB-H，该算法在免费探索阶段自适应探索，在后续阶段最小化累积遗憾，理论分析表明该算法相比无免费探索的策略能获得严格的遗憾节省，并通过上下界揭示了遗憾随免费探索量的相变现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的遗憾最小化或纯探索范式无法捕捉智能体在产生遗憾之前获得免费探索预算的场景，因此需要形式化并研究这一新设定下的最优策略设计。

Method: 提出两阶段概率节省算法UFE-KLUCB-H：第一阶段采用原则性免费探索策略UFE进行自适应探索，第二阶段使用历史感知的遗憾最小化策略KLUCB-H。引入(α,β)-概率节省策略族来量化免费探索阶段节省的遗憾量。

Result: 推导了UFE-KLUCB-H的实例依赖上界，证明其累积遗憾严格低于无免费探索的策略；基于多实例摄动论证推导了实例依赖下界，证明算法在二值老虎机上的近最优性；上下界揭示了累积遗憾随免费探索量的尖锐相变。

Conclusion: 强制探索和算法中的自适应性能够带来更大的遗憾节省，仿真实验验证了这一结论。

Abstract: We study a stochastic multi-armed bandit problem where an agent is granted a free exploration budget before regret accumulates, a setting not captured by the classic regret minimization or pure exploration paradigms. The goal is to design an adaptive policy that strategically explores the bandit instance in the initial free exploration phase and minimizes the cumulative regret in the subsequent phase. We formalize this regret minimization with free exploration problem and identify an interesting regime where the free exploration budget scales logarithmically with the time horizon. To quantify the amount of regret saved with high probability as a result of the availability of the free exploration phase, we introduce a novel set of policies known as $(α,β)$-probably saving policies. We propose a two-phase, probably saving algorithm, UFE-KLUCB-H, which consists of a principled free exploration policy, UFE, and a history-aware regret minimization policy KLUCB-H. Instance-dependent upper bounds on UFE-KLUCB-H are derived, showing that UFE-KLUCB-H accumulates strictly less regret than policies that do not have access to a free exploration phase. Complementarily, we derive instance-dependent lower bounds based on novel multi-instance perturbation arguments tailored to the free-exploration setting, demonstrating the near-optimality of UFE-KLUCB-H for two-valued bandits. Our upper and lower bounds reveal sharp phase transitions in the accumulated regret depending on the amount of available free exploration. Simulations are conducted to demonstrate that forced exploration and adaptivity in the algorithm lead to greater regret savings.

</details>


### [131] [On Reliability of Efficient Membership Inference Vulnerability Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25819)
*Joonas Jälkö,Gauri Pradhan,Ossi Räisä,Antti Honkela*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了当前高效成员推断攻击评估流程中的两个关键缺陷：1）跨个体拼接MIA分数评估低FPR下的TPR会导致FPR校准偏差，使差分隐私审计不可靠；2）LiRA实现中存在有限总体偏差，导致样本脆弱性正向偏差。并提出了一种后处理方法用于校准FPR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成员推断攻击（MIA）是评估训练数据隐私泄露的常用方法，但在低FPR下估计TPR需要大量目标模型，计算成本高昂。现有高效评估流程通过跨个体平均MIA分数来降低计算开销，但作者发现该方法存在两个未被认识到的关键缺陷，影响评估的可靠性。

Method: 1）通过理论分析和实验证明跨个体拼接MIA分数评估TPR时，FPR在不同样本间未校准，导致低FPR下的TPR估计不可靠；2）提出一种后处理方法校准不同样本间的FPR；3）识别出Carlini等人2022年提出的高效LiRA实现中存在有限总体偏差，导致样本脆弱性被高估。

Result: 发现两个关键问题：跨个体FPR校准偏差使差分隐私审计不可信；LiRA的有限总体偏差导致正向偏差。

Conclusion: 现有高效MIA评估流程存在根本性缺陷，需要进行后处理校准和偏差修正，才能可靠地用于隐私泄露评估和差分隐私审计。

Abstract: Membership inference attacks (MIAs) are popular methods for empirically assessing the leakage of sensitive information in the training data through models or statistics learned from the data. The MIA vulnerability is often evaluated through false positive rate (FPR) and true positive rate (TPR) of a binary classifier that tries to predict whether a particular sample was in the training data. However, in order to reliably estimate the TPR especially for low FPR values, a lot of observations are needed, which in case of MIA translates to many target models, leading to large computational cost. To avoid excessive compute requirements, the MIA scores are often averaged over multiple individuals and multiple targeted models. We demonstrate two key weaknesses in this efficient MIA evaluation pipeline. First, we show that evaluating the TPR based on MIA scores concatenated across multiple individuals, commonly used to study vulnerabilities in the very low FPR regime, is not calibrated across the per-sample FPRs. This makes it unreliable as a tool for auditing differential privacy. To solve this, we propose a post-processing method to effectively calibrate the FPR across different samples. Second, we identify a finite population bias in the commonly used efficient likelihood-ratio attack (LiRA) implementation proposed by Carlini et al. 2022, leading to a positive bias in the per-sample vulnerability.

</details>


### [132] [Visual-Redundancy-Controlled Parallel Decoding for Diffusion-Based Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25820)
*Yulin Yuan,Hongshuo Zhao,Xiangming Meng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对基于扩散的多模态大语言模型（dMLLMs）在并行解码时存在的视觉冗余问题，提出了一种无需训练、推理时使用的视觉冗余控制解码方法（VRCD），通过token-to-image注意力优先选择视觉互补的位置，从而在多个多模态基准上提升解码准确率，最高相对提升18.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于置信度的解码策略在并行选择masked位置时独立排序，忽略了已提交token之间可能存在视觉归因重叠问题，导致视觉冗余，降低了后续解码步骤中可用的互补视觉信息。

Method: 1) 提出视觉冗余指数（VRI）量化并行提交token之间的视觉归因重叠程度；2) 提出VRCD解码方法，利用token-to-image注意力机制优先选择视觉互补的位置进行提交，无需额外训练。

Result: 在多个多模态基准上，VRCD降低了视觉冗余和剩余位置熵，在长解码实验中，在M^3CoT上相对准确率提升达18.8%，在MMBench上提升6.9%。

Conclusion: VRCD作为一种训练免费的推理时解码方法，能有效控制视觉冗余，提升dMLLMs在多模态任务上的解码质量，且计算开销适度。

Abstract: Diffusion-based multimodal large language models (dMLLMs) decode by iteratively predicting tokens at multiple masked positions in parallel. This turns each decoding step into a position-selection problem: the model must choose not only which predictions are reliable in isolation, but also which positions should be committed together as context for later decoding steps. Existing confidence-based decoding ranks masked positions independently and commits the top-K positions, largely ignoring whether the committed tokens provide complementary visual grounding. We identify a step-level limitation of this strategy in multimodal settings: high-confidence tokens selected in the same step can rely on overlapping visual grounding, introducing visual redundancy among the committed tokens and leaving less complementary visual grounding available for later decoding. To quantify this effect, we introduce the Visual Redundancy Index (VRI), which measures visual grounding overlap among tokens committed in parallel. To control this redundancy during decoding, we propose Visual-Redundancy-Controlled Decoding (VRCD), a training-free inference-time decoding method that uses token-to-image attention to prioritize visually complementary positions. Across diverse multimodal benchmarks, VRCD reduces visual redundancy and remaining-position entropy with modest runtime overhead. In longer decoding experiments, it also achieves relative accuracy gains of up to 18.8% on M^3CoT and 6.9% on MMBench over confidence-based decoding. Code will be released at https://github.com/infiniteYuanyl/VRCD.

</details>


### [133] [Context-Instrumental Data Distillation for Kubernetes Manifest Generation: Method and Experimental Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25835)
*Andrey Kozachok,Anatoliy Bakaev,Aleksandr Kozachok,Shamil Magomedov,Artem Noev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种上下文引导的数据蒸馏方法（Context-Instrumental Data Distillation），通过合成数据生成和反向指令生成构建高质量训练语料，结合外部验证器和领域上下文过滤，在资源受限条件下（CPU微调）将1.5B参数的SLM在Kubernetes YAML生成任务上达到了91.5%的pass@1准确率。关键发现是对于DSL生成任务，严格的输出格式约束比增加训练样本量更为重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索小参数规模（≤4B）的语言模型在领域特定语言（DSL）工件生成任务上的专业化能力，以Kubernetes清单生成为切入点，验证在资源受限条件下SLM是否能够通过高质量数据蒸馏达到实用性能。

Method: 提出上下文引导的数据蒸馏方法（Context-Instrumental Data Distillation），包含两种数据生成路径：(1) 合成生成——使用大模型教师（DeepSeek-V4 Flash API）生成Kubernetes YAML样例；(2) 反向指令生成——从真实Kubernetes YAML文件反向生成指令。所有训练样本需通过外部验证器（如kubectl validate）和领域上下文模型匹配过滤后才被纳入训练集，最终对Qwen2.5-Coder-1.5B-Instruct进行LoRA微调（在CPU上执行）。

Result: 在K8s-Distill-Pilot语料库（训练1200条、验证100条、测试200条）上，使用更严格的prompt格式和max_new_tokens=768，实现了full-pass@1 = 91.5%（183/200）。

Conclusion: 对于Kubernetes YAML这类DSL生成任务，结果质量更依赖于严格的输出格式约束，而非简单地增加训练样本数量。这表明在领域特定的结构化代码生成中，数据质量和格式规范比数据规模更为关键。

Abstract: This paper examines the specialization of Small Language Models (SLMs) with up to 4 billion parameters for generating artifacts in domain-specific languages (DSL). Kubernetes manifests are chosen as the target domain. We propose the context-instrumental data distillation method: the source corpus is formed through synthetic generation and, in an extended scheme, through reverse instruction generation from real Kubernetes YAML files, with pairs included in training only upon passing external validators and matching the domain context model. Unlike classical KL-divergence knowledge distillation, the baseline implementation reduces to supervised fine-tuning on instrumentally verified examples. The experimental section presents a pilot implementation under resource-constrained conditions: the DeepSeek-V4 Flash API serves as the teacher for synthetic generation, while Qwen2.5-Coder-1.5B-Instruct is fine-tuned via LoRA on CPU. On the K8s-Distill-Pilot corpus (train_1200, validation_100, test_200), we achieved full-pass@1 = 91.5% (183/200) with a stricter prompt formulation and max_new_tokens=768. The key empirical finding is that for Kubernetes YAML, result quality in the pilot depended more on strict output format requirements than on simply increasing the number of training examples.

</details>


### [134] [Geometric Evolution Maps: Extracting Stable Concept Probes from Transformer Residual Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25848)
*James Henry*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出几何演化图(GEMs)，通过追踪概念在残差流中的完整方向轨迹，识别旋转停止的"交接层"并提取稳定探针方向。实验表明，传统方法在概念分配区(CAZ)内入口到出口余弦相似度均值仅0.233，探针方向不稳定。GEM在391个实验对中66.2%严格优于峰值层探针，MHA模型优势更显著(78.3%)。自适应消融宽度规则在触发案例中提升75.9%，平均增益+7.44pp。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统在固定后期层或分离度峰值层提取概念探针的方法忽略了概念表征在组装阶段发生显著方向旋转这一根本结构特征，导致探针方向不可靠。

Method: 提出Geometric Evolution Maps(GEMs)，追踪概念在残差流激活中的完整方向轨迹，识别旋转停止的handoff层，从该层提取稳定探针方向。配合自适应消融宽度规则和方向特异性控制验证。

Result: 23种架构(70M-14B参数)、17种概念类型上，CAZ内入口-出口余弦相似度均值仅0.233。GEM在68.5%试验中不劣于峰值层探针，66.2%严格更优。MHA模型78.3% favour handoff，GQA仅47.1%。自适应消融在触发案例中75.9%提升，平均增益+7.44pp。方向特异性控制显示中位377倍抑制率。

Conclusion: 概念探针的可靠性取决于提取层的选择，GEM通过识别概念方向稳定化的交接层显著优于传统峰值层探针方法，但效果存在架构差异(MHA vs GQA)。

Abstract: Concept probes extracted from transformer residual streams are only as reliable as the layer from which they are extracted. The common practice of probing at a fixed late layer or at the peak of a separation score function ignores a fundamental structural feature: concept representations undergo substantial directional rotation during their assembly phase, and do not settle into a stable direction until a characteristic handoff layer after the primary Concept Allocation Zone (CAZ). We introduce Geometric Evolution Maps (GEMs), which track the full directional trajectory of a concept through residual stream activations, identify the handoff layer where rotation ceases, and extract the settled probe direction from that layer. Across 23 architectures spanning 70M to 14B parameters and 17 concept types, the entry-to-exit cosine similarity within CAZs has a mean of 0.233, showing that probe direction at CAZ entry does not reliably predict probe direction at exit. Ablation experiments across 391 concept x model pairs (23 models x 17 concepts) show that GEM-extracted probes are at least as precise as peak-layer probes in 268/391 trials (68.5%), and strictly outperform in 259/391 (66.2%). The architecture split is pronounced: MHA models favour the handoff in 173/221 trials (78.3%); GQA models favour the handoff in only 56/119 trials (47.1%). Model-level Wilcoxon: W=214, N=23, p=0.010 (one-sided). An adaptive ablation width rule targets the 79/391 near-final-layer cases: it improves probe quality in 60/79 triggered cases (75.9%), mean gain +7.44pp. A direction-specificity control confirms the ablation effect is concept-direction specific: median 377x suppression rate versus random-direction ablation (99.1% of concept directions beat all 10 random seeds). Reference implementation: rosetta_tools v1.3.1 (doi:10.5281/zenodo.20361433).

</details>


### [135] [When Self-Belief Misleads: Active Label Acquisition for Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25864)
*Li Wang,Xiaodong Lu,Xiaohan Wang,Yikun Ban,Jiajun Chai,Wei Lin,Tianhao Peng,Guojun Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RLAVR框架，通过主动选择少量样本获取真实标签并与伪标签结合，在有限标注预算下稳定训练；同时提出CAG指标和CARE策略，用于评估样本价值并实现高效的样本获取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）依赖真实标签计算奖励，但在实际中获取真实标签成本高昂。无监督RLVR使用伪标签容易训练崩溃，且不同样本的标注价值存在差异。现有方法缺乏对有限标注预算下如何高效选择有价值样本以稳定训练的系统性研究。

Method: 提出RLAVR框架，主动获取少量选定样本的真实标签并与伪标签集成。定义Corrective Advantage Gap (CAG)指标，从理论层面分析样本级监督价值。基于CAG提出Correction-Aware Reliability Estimation (CARE)策略，将理论CAG准则转化为实际可用的预查询获取策略，用于在训练前选择高价值样本进行标注。

Result: 在多个领域、模型族和模型规模上的大量实验表明，所提方法在有限标注预算下能有效提升训练稳定性和性能，验证了方法的有效性和通用性。

Conclusion: 通过主动获取少量高价值样本的真实标签并与伪标签协同训练，可以在显著降低标注成本的同时保持训练稳定性和模型性能。CARE策略成功将理论分析转化为实用方案，为有限标注资源下的RLVR训练提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable advancements in reasoning capabilities empowered by Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR). Nonetheless, RLVR intrinsically relies on ground-truth labels for reward computation, the acquisition of which is often prohibitively expensive in real-world scenarios. While unsupervised RLVR paradigms attempt to circumvent this by training on pseudo-labels, they are notoriously susceptible to training collapse. Moreover, different samples often exhibit varying annotation values. In this paper, we propose Reinforcement Learning with Active Verifiable Rewards (RLAVR), which actively acquires ground-truth labels for a small set of selected samples and integrates them with pseudo-labels, thereby stabilizing training dynamics and improving performance under limited annotation budgets. To identify valuable samples, we propose the Corrective Advantage Gap (CAG) metric and analyze the sample-level supervision value. Building on this, we introduce Correction-Aware Reliability Estimation for RLAVR (CARE), which translates the oracle CAG criterion into a practical pre-query acquisition policy to substantially improve training stability. Extensive experiments across diverse domains, model families, and model scales demonstrate the effectiveness and generality of our approach. Our code is available at https://github.com/Lumina04/CARE.

</details>


### [136] [UNATE: UNsupervised ATomic Embedding for crystal structures property prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25866)
*Laura Solà-Garcia,Àlex Solé,Javier Ruiz-Hidalgo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UNATE是一个基于无监督去噪自编码器和自监督对比学习的框架，通过从未标记晶体结构中提取原子嵌入来提升晶体属性预测性能，尤其在标签数据稀缺时效果显著（全数据提升2.7%，仅用25%标签数据时提升达10%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晶体属性预测受限于标记数据稀缺和理论计算成本高昂，现有方法难以充分利用未标记晶体结构中的结构信息。

Method: 提出UNATE框架，结合无监督去噪自编码器与自监督对比学习，从未标记晶体结构中学习鲁棒的原子表示，并将其作为下游属性预测的输入特征。

Result: 在全数据基线任务上提升2.7%；在仅使用25%标记数据时，提升幅度达到10%。

Conclusion: UNATE能够有效利用未标记数据学习原子嵌入，显著缓解标记数据稀缺问题，尤其在数据有限场景下优势更为突出。

Abstract: Accurately predicting crystal properties is critical for accelerating materials discovery, but it is often limited by scarce labeled data and costly theoretical calculations. To alleviate this, we propose UNATE (Unsupervised Atomic Embedding), a framework that leverages structural information extracted from unlabeled crystal structures. UNATE integrates an unsupervised denoising autoencoder with self-supervised contrastive learning to learn robust atomic representations, which are then used as input features for downstream property prediction. Experimental results show that replacing raw atomic numbers with UNATE-pretrained node embeddings yields a 2.7\% improvement over the full-data baseline. Notably, the benefits become more pronounced in scenarios with limited labeled data, reaching improvements of up to 10\% when only 25\% of the labeled data is used.

</details>


### [137] [The Quantization Benefits of Residual-Free Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25880)
*Yiping Ji,Mahalakshmi Sabanayagam,Peyman Moghadam,Hemanth Saratchandran,Simon Lucey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 残差连接会导致Transformer激活值偏离高斯分布，增加低比特量化的误差。而消除残差连接、使用正交初始化等技术的无残差Transformer虽然会略微降低全精度性能，但能保持激活值接近高斯分布，显著提升对低比特量化的鲁棒性，揭示了准确率与可压缩性之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模Transformer训练和部署中激活值、梯度和优化器状态的传输开销日益成为瓶颈，低比特量化是自然的解决方案，但Transformer激活值具有重尾和离群值主导的特点，简单量化会导致严重损失。

Method: 通过对比残差Transformer和无残差Transformer，利用超额峰度分析残差混合会放大非高斯性，而无残差Transformer中的密集混合会压缩非高斯性；采用正交初始化、谱优化/二阶优化、深度感知注意力温度缩放使无残差Transformer可训练。

Result: 在语言任务中，无残差Transformer在全精度下性能略有下降，但激活值保持近高斯分布，对低比特量化的鲁棒性显著提升。

Conclusion: 发现Transformer设计中存在准确率与可压缩性之间的权衡，激励从架构层面设计更利于量化的基础模型。

Abstract: Large-scale transformer training and deployment are increasingly constrained by the transfer of activations, gradients, and optimizer states across accelerators. Low-bit quantization offers a natural remedy, but transformer activations are often heavy-tailed and outlier-dominated, making simple quantization highly lossy. We show that this difficulty is not only a property of the quantizer, but also of the architecture. Specifically, residual connections can drive transformer activations away from Gaussianity during training. Using controlled comparisons between residual and residual-free transformers, we demonstrate that this effect leads to substantially higher quantization error and accuracy degradation at low precision in residual models. We explain the phenomenon through an excess kurtosis analysis, showing that residual mixing can amplify non-Gaussianity, whereas dense mixing in residual-free contracts non-Gaussianity. We then show that residual-free transformers can be made trainable using orthogonal initialization, spectral or second-order optimization, and depth-aware scaling of attention temperature. In language tasks, while there is a small drop in full precision performance, these models retain near-Gaussian activations and exhibit significantly improved robustness to low-bit quantization. Our results identify an accuracy--compressibility trade-off in transformer design and motivate architecture-level approaches to quantization-friendly foundation models.

</details>


### [138] [Conformalised imprecise inference for robust extrapolation under limited data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25882)
*Yu Chen,Scott Ferson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于共形预测的不精确推理框架，通过引入不精确性和距离感知，在分布外场景下仍能保持预测覆盖率的有效性，并在有限数据下表现出更强的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不确定性量化方法虽然区分了偶然不确定性和认知不确定性，但在训练域外泛化时缺乏严格的保证，需要一种统一且具有鲁棒性保证的框架。

Method: 提出共形化不精确推理框架（conformalised imprecise inference），是模型无关的，通过增强预测模型的不精确性和距离感知能力，生成概率盒（probability boxes）形式的不精确预测。

Result: 在分布偏移下仍能保持有效的覆盖率，并在外推区域自适应地扩大不确定性；在合成数据和基准数据集上，相比标准概率方法表现出更好的鲁棒性和可靠的覆盖性能，尤其在有限数据场景下。

Conclusion: 该框架为分布外泛化提供了有严格保证的不确定性量化方法，兼顾了模型无关性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Recent advances in uncertainty quantification increasingly emphasise the distinction between aleatory and epistemic uncertainty in machine learning, motivating the need for more unified frameworks. However, despite much progress in producing reliable predictions, existing methods often lack rigorous guarantees when generalising beyond the training domain. We propose a conformalised imprecise inference framework for robust extrapolation, which is model-agnostic and augments predictive models with imprecision and distance awareness. The proposed approach yields imprecise predictions (probability boxes) that remain valid under distributional shift, maintaining coverage while adaptively expanding uncertainty in extrapolation regimes. Experiments on synthetic and benchmark datasets demonstrate improved robustness and reliable coverage compared to standard probabilistic approaches, particularly under limited data.

</details>


### [139] [Merge-Bench: Resolve Merge Conflicts with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25890)
*Benedikt Schesch,Michael D. Ernst*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了包含7938个真实合并冲突的数据集Merge-Bench，并训练了基于GRPO强化学习的LLMergeJ模型（14B参数）来解析Java程序中的合并冲突。实验表明LLMergeJ在Java上优于3个商业LLM，仅次于Gemini 2.5 Pro；跨11种编程语言的最佳模型正确率不足60%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 版本控制合并是软件开发中困难且重要的任务，现有方法未能充分利用机器学习技术来解决合并冲突问题。

Method: (1) 构建Merge-Bench数据集：从1439个GitHub仓库收集7938个真实合并冲突块，以开发者提交的合并解决方案作为真实标签，无需人工标注。(2) 训练LLMergeJ模型：采用Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)在线强化学习方法对大型语言模型进行微调。(3) 在Java程序上评估LLMergeJ（14B参数），并在11种编程语言上评估商业LLM。

Result: LLMergeJ（14B参数）在Java合并冲突解析上超越3个商业LLM，仅落后于Gemini 2.5 Pro。商业LLM在11种编程语言上的性能表现相对稳定，但最佳模型正确解决率不足60%。

Conclusion: 强化学习（GRPO）可有效提升LLM在合并冲突解析任务上的表现，但当前最优模型仍存在较大提升空间（<60%正确率），表明该任务仍具挑战性。

Abstract: This paper applies machine learning to the difficult and important task of version control merging. (1) We constructed a dataset, Merge-Bench, of 7938 real-world merge conflict hunks from 1439 GitHub repositories. The ground truth is the merge resolution that developers committed to the repository. Our dataset construction methodology is scalable to arbitrary amounts of data since no manual labeling is required. (2) We trained a model, LLMergeJ, to resolve merge conflicts in Java programs. Our approach uses Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), an online reinforcement learning method, to train a Large Language Model (LLM). (3) We performed two evaluations of the performance of LLMs on resolving merge conflicts. On Java programs, LLMergeJ with 14B parameters outperforms 3 commercial LLMs, trailing only Gemini 2.5 Pro. Across 11 programming languages, commercial LLM performance is largely stable from language to language. The best models correctly resolve less than 60% of merge conflicts.

</details>


### [140] [Reading the Finetuning Prior: Verbatim Content Recovery via Contrastive Decoding Diffing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25902)
*Michał Brzozowski,Zuzanna Dubanowska,Enrico Cassano,Neo Christopher Chung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出对比解码差异法(CDD)，一种仅需输出层logit分布、无需权重访问的模型差异分析方法，能在无白盒访问条件下精确恢复微调模型植入的事实信息，性能优于现有白盒方法且速度快约170倍，并首次实现了从数据生成器伪影到模型权重的完整指纹追踪链。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有白盒方法(如ADL)虽能恢复微调领域信息，但需要完整的模型内部访问权限(权重、层选择等)，而实际部署的模型往往仅提供黑盒访问。审计模型是否被植入特定知识对于AI系统的透明度和问责制至关重要。

Method: CDD包含三个关键思想：1)绕过聊天模板暴露原始微调先验；2)使用最大模糊化的前缀种子引导生成；3)在每一步解码中放大微调模型与基座模型之间的logit空间差异。仅需输出层logit分布，无需权重访问、层选择或逐模型调参。

Result: 在四种架构(1B-32B参数)上，CDD能逐字恢复植入事实（药物名称、投票计数、物理测量值、程序细节等），统一优于ADL（尽管访问更少）且速度快约170倍。在真实领域微调设置中，所有单数据集非CoT变体几乎完美恢复，混合数据集设置中正确识别所有四个数据集。此外，意外发现了由LLM数据生成器模式坍塌引入的虚构人物伪影，实现了首个端到端指纹追踪链。

Conclusion: CDD作为灰盒方法超越了白盒基线，证明了其在实际AI系统透明度和问责制中的实用价值，为模型审计提供了更易部署、更高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Narrowly finetuned language models memorize implanted content verbatim, but auditing what a deployed model has been taught, without access to its weights or training data, remains an open challenge. Recent work shows that activation differences between base and finetuned models carry readable traces of the finetuning domain; the state-of-the-art Activation Difference Lens (ADL) recovers a vague domain-level description but requires full "white-box" access to model internals. We introduce Contrastive Decoding Diffing (CDD), a model diffing method that operates on output-level logit distributions only, with no weight access, no layer selection, and no per-model tuning, yet recovers implanted facts. CDD consists of three ideas: bypassing the chat template to expose the raw finetuning prior, seeding generation with maximally vague pre-fills, and amplifying the logit-space difference between finetuned and base models at each decoding step. A single default configuration recovers implanted facts verbatim -- exact drug names, vote counts, physical measurements, and procedural details -- across four architectures (1B--32B parameters), uniformly outperforming ADL despite less access and running ~170x faster. Furthermore, CDD surfaces unintended data pipeline artifacts: a fictional persona introduced by the LLM data generator via mode collapse leaked into model weights and was extracted by CDD, constituting to our knowledge the first demonstrated end-to-end fingerprinting chain from data generator artifact to model weights to recovered output. We validate on real-domain finetuning settings, achieving near-perfect recovery across all single-dataset non-CoT variants and correctly identifying all four datasets in the mixed-dataset setting. CDD's success as a grey-box method outperforming white-box baselines underscores its practical utility for transparency and accountability in AI systems.

</details>


### [141] [Joint Optimization of Training and Inference in Federated Edge Learning via Constrained Multi-Objective Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25916)
*Zhen Li,Jun Cai,Chao Yang,Haoran Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种联合优化联邦训练与边缘推理的在线框架，通过双队列转换机制关联推理请求与训练数据，并引入数据和模型新鲜度来建模动态环境。将模式选择、通信与计算资源分配建模为多目标马尔可夫决策过程，并开发了C-MOPPO算法以学习不同偏好下的策略集，通过约束策略优化丰富Pareto前沿，在推理准确率、延迟和能耗之间取得了良好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源受限的边缘设备上，联邦边缘学习中的训练与推理相互竞争有限资源，且需要同时优化推理准确率、延迟和能耗三个相互冲突的目标，现有工作缺乏有效的联合在线优化框架。

Method: 设计了串联队列转换机制桥接推理请求与训练数据；将数据和模型新鲜度纳入准确率建模；将联合优化问题转化为多目标马尔可夫决策过程（MOMDP）；提出了约束多目标近端策略优化（C-MOPPO）算法，先学习不同偏好下的策略集，再利用约束策略优化丰富Pareto前沿。

Result: C-MOPPO算法在不同系统配置下均能实现目标间的良好平衡，显著优于各类基线方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的在线优化框架和C-MOPPO算法能有效联合管理FEEL中的训练与推理，在多个性能指标上取得均衡且优异的表现。

Abstract: Federated edge learning (FEEL) has recently emerged as a promising paradigm for achieving edge intelligence (EI) via enabling collaborative model training across edge devices while protecting data privacy. In this paper, we put forth an online optimization framework that jointly manages federated training and inference on resource-constrained edge devices. We introduce a tandem-queue-inspired conversion mechanism that bridges inference requests and training data, and further incorporate both data and model freshness into the accuracy formulation to capture temporal dynamics in real-world environments. To maximize inference accuracy while minimizing latency and energy consumption, the mode selections, communication, and computation resource allocations of edge devices are jointly optimized. We formulate this optimization as a multi-objective optimization problem, which is NP-hard and further complicated by the online setting. To address these challenges, we transform the problem into a multi-objective Markov decision process (MOMDP) and develop a \underline{c}onstrained \underline{m}ulti-\underline{o}bjective \underline{p}roximal \underline{p}olicy \underline{o}ptimization (C-MOPPO) algorithm. Specifically, C-MOPPO first learns a set of policies with different preferences across three objectives, then leverages constrained policy optimization to enrich the Pareto front and obtain high-quality, dense solutions. Extensive experiments demonstrate that C-MOPPO achieves well-balanced trade-offs among objectives and significantly outperforms baselines under various system configurations.

</details>


### [142] [Quantitative Evaluation of the Severity of Posttraumatic Stress Disorder through Transfer Learning from Specific Phobia Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25933)
*Nicolas Ricka,Gauthier Pellegrin,Denis A. Fompeyrine,Thomas Rohaly,Leah Enders,Heather Roy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出基于多元核密度估计(MKDE)的机器学习方法，利用心率和皮电反应信号客观评估PTSD严重程度，在21名军事人员数据上达到86%的分类准确率，平均绝对误差5.6。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前PTSD临床评估依赖主观评价，耗时、昂贵且易受人为偏差影响，需要一种客观、低成本的评估方法。

Method: 收集21名参与者在沉浸式仿真中的HR和GSR信号及PCL-M标签；在公共蜘蛛恐惧症数据集上训练恐惧反应模型；从PTSD数据集的恐惧反应曲线提取预测特征；采用MKDE技术构建机器学习分类器。

Result: PTSD状态分类准确率86%（PCL-M阈值36）；平均绝对误差(MAE)为5.6；临床PTSD严重度量表平均绝对百分比误差17%。

Conclusion: 该算法利用生理信号实现客观、低负担的PTSD严重度评估，在筛查和随访中具有临床实用潜力。

Abstract: Posttraumatic stress disorder (PTSD) is a prevalent and debilitating mental health condition with significant personal and societal impacts. Current clinical assessments of PTSD often rely on subjective evaluations, which can be time-consuming, costly, and prone to human bias. This study proposes a machine learning (ML) approach based on multivariate kernel density estimation (MKDE) technique for the objective evaluation of PTSD severity. We collected heart rate (HR) and galvanic skin response (GSR) signals as well as PTSD Checklist - Military Version (PCL-M) labels from 21 participants during an immersive simulation. A fear-response model was trained on a public arachnophobia dataset, and predictive features of PTSD were extracted from the fear-response curves estimated on the military dataset. The model achieved an accuracy of 86\% in classifying PTSD status, effectively distinguishing participants with and without PTSD (PCL-M threshold of 36). The average mean absolute error (MAE) of the models is 5.6, and it estimated a clinical PTSD severity scale with a mean absolute percentage error of 17\%. Our algorithm demonstrates promising potential for enhancing estimation of PTSD severity and followup by offering an objective and low-effort evaluation approach using physiology. These findings suggest clinical utility in both screening and follow-up settings.

</details>


### [143] [From Latent Space to Training Data: Explainable Specialization in Minimal MLPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25939)
*Enrique Alba,Ezequiel Lopez-Rubio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了单隐层MLP中隐藏神经元是否会在训练偏置下产生特化，以及这种特化是否有助于从学习权重中基于原型重建训练数据集。实验表明，覆盖正则化能有效降低重建误差并提高原型使用特化比，而分离正则化效果不一，重叠惩罚则系统性有害。作者揭示了有害机制：重叠活跃方法虽能拟合数据，但会将原型中心推至训练输入凸包之外，而覆盖损失能阻止这种驱逐。最终提出设计原则：任何排斥性结构损失必须由兼容的吸引子补偿，否则将破坏潜在几何结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究训练偏置能否使单隐层MLP的隐藏神经元产生特化，以及这种特化是否有助于从学习权重中基于原型重建训练数据集。

Method: 使用高斯激活的单隐层MLP，宽度等于数据集大小，比较三种结构损失（覆盖、分离、低重叠）与标准拟合基线；在均匀采样的一维数据集上进行N=3到N=100的480次受控实验。

Result: 覆盖正则化在所有规模下获得最低平均重建误差，并提高原型使用特化比；分离正则化效果不一；重叠惩罚系统性有害。重叠活跃方法会将原型中心推至训练输入凸包之外，而覆盖损失能阻止这种驱逐。

Conclusion: 任何排斥性结构损失必须由兼容的吸引子补偿，否则将破坏其旨在优化的潜在几何结构。

Abstract: We here study whether training biases can make hidden neurons specialize in minimal one-hidden-layer MLPs, and whether such specialization improves prototype-based reconstruction of the training dataset from the learned weights. We consider Gaussianactivation MLPs of width equal to dataset size and compare three structural losses that respectively encourage coverage of the training samples, separation between neuron-induced prototypes, and low overlap of hidden responses, against the standard fitting baseline. Experiments on uniformly sampled one-dimensional datasets show a stable pattern from N = 3 to N = 100 across 480 controlled runs. Coverage regularization gives the lowest mean reconstruction error at every tested size and raises the prototype-usage specialization ratio relative to the standard baseline, while separation has mixed effects and overlap penalties are systematically harmful. We show that the harm is not an optimization failure: overlap-active approaches fit the data as well as overlap-free ones but route the optimizer to a degenerate equilibrium in which prototype centers are pushed outside the convex hull of the training inputs. Coverage cannot reward this expulsion and acts as an attractor: separation admits it only at large temperature and overlap admits it at the nominal hyperparameter choice. A direct τ-sweep on the separation-only mask and a prototype-position visualization at N = 100 confirm the mechanism. The findings yield a simple design principle for prototype-recoverability-aware training: every repulsive structural loss must be compensated by a compatible attractor, or it will collapse the latent geometry it was meant to refine.

</details>


### [144] [STaT: Resolving Shape Distortion in Non-Stationary Time Series via Tri-Modal Synergy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25943)
*Hui Cheng,Jinsheng Guo,Zhenhao Weng,Yan Qiao,Meng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出STaT多模态架构，通过符号-时间-文本三模态对齐，在保持低平均误差的同时减少预测形状失真，在8个基准上提升幅度指标达8.9%、降低形状失真达8.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列多模态方法在最小化平均误差时会产生过于平滑的预测，丢失关键波动信息，即平均误差与形状保真度之间存在矛盾。

Method: 提出STaT架构：(1) 符号模态：将连续时间序列离散化为符号标记，识别结构模式与转折点；(2) 时间模态：提取序列固有依赖关系；(3) 文本模态：利用领域语义指导宏观趋势预测。三模态通过对齐机制融合。

Result: 在8个真实世界基准上，幅度指标提升最高8.9%，形状失真降低最高8.5%。

Conclusion: STaT通过符号-时间-文本三模态对齐，有效解决了多模态时间序列预测中平均误差与形状保真度之间的权衡问题。

Abstract: Recent research in time series forecasting frequently investigates the integration of textual and visual modalities with numerical models to better navigate non-stationary environments. Despite delivering solid numerical results, existing multi-modal approaches usually encounter a dilemma: prioritizing the minimization of average errors can result in excessively smooth forecasts that overlook essential fluctuations. To resolve this limitation, we introduce STaT, an innovative multimodal architecture for Symbolic-Temporal-Textual Alignment, which seamlessly unites three synergistic modalities. Specifically, the symbolic modality converts continuous time series into discrete tokens, facilitating the accurate identification of structural patterns and turning points; the temporal modality extracts inherent sequential dependencies; and the textual modality leverages domain semantics to steer the macroscopic forecasting trends. Comprehensive evaluations on eight real-world benchmarks indicate that STaT delivers exceptional performance, enhancing conventional magnitude indicators by up to 8.9% while simultaneously decreasing shape distortion by up to 8.5%.

</details>


### [145] [Small Models, Strong Priors: Architectural Inductive Bias for Parameter-Efficient Neural PDE Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25949)
*Shyam Sankaran,Hanwen Wang,Paris Perdikaris*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出WaveLiT架构，通过离散小波变换、线性注意力、多尺度特征金字塔和小波域辅助损失等结构化归纳偏置，使1-10M参数的小模型在多个PDE基准上媲美100-1000倍大小的基础模型，证明架构偏置比模型规模更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反驳神经PDE求解器领域"规模至上"的趋势，论证结构化归纳偏置比参数规模更能带来参数效率，并指出偏置的失败模式本身就能揭示其捕捉到的物理结构信息。

Method: 提出WaveLiT架构，包含：(1)离散小波变换实现无损多分辨率分词；(2)增强线性注意力模块；(3)共享权重的多尺度特征金字塔；(4)小波域辅助损失函数。

Result: 1-10M参数的WaveLiT在8个TheWell基准上与100-1000倍大小的基础模型竞争，在波动和声学主导的基准上增益最大；10M的基础变体在联合训练中表现出结构化、可物理解释的迁移模式。

Conclusion: 小模型PDE性能由架构归纳偏置而非规模塑造，偏置的失败结构是其内容的有用经验信号；整个Pipeline只需单GPU即可完成训练。

Abstract: Neural PDE solvers have followed the scaling trajectory of vision and language, with recent foundation models reaching billions of parameters. We argue that scale is a poor substitute for architectural inductive bias in this domain: structured priors deliver outsized parameter efficiency, and the pattern of where they succeed and fail is itself informative about what they capture. We instantiate this argument in WaveLiT, an architecture combining a discrete wavelet transform for lossless multi-resolution tokenization, an augmented linear attention block, a shared-weight multiscale feature pyramid, and a wavelet-domain auxiliary loss. Bespoke 1-10M-parameter WaveLiT models compete with foundation models of 100-1000$\times$ their size across eight TheWell benchmarks, with the largest gains on wave and acoustic-dominated benchmarks where the wavelet-multiscale prior fits the dominant dynamical structure and small per-step errors do not compound geometrically under rollout. Trained jointly across all eight benchmarks, a 10M-parameter foundation variant exhibits a structured, physically interpretable transfer pattern -- strongest where the wavelet-multiscale prior matches the dynamics, weakest on chaotic advection-dominated flows. The entire pipeline trains on a single GPU. The results suggest that small-model PDE performance is shaped by architectural inductive bias rather than scale, and that the structure of a prior's failures is a useful empirical signal about its content.

</details>


### [146] [Step-TP: A Grounded, Step-Level Dataset with Chain-of-Thought Reasoning for LLM-Guided Tensor Program Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25954)
*Mengfan Liu,Da Zheng,Junwei Su,Chuan Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Step-TP数据集，通过结构化的逐步思维链推理和可验证的中间表示，为张量程序优化提供原子级、可解释的步骤级监督，使LLM能够进行可靠的多步优化而非结果模仿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-guided的张量程序优化方法仅提供端到端的优化程序对，使用token效率低下的表示，缺乏可验证的步骤级监督和可解释性，导致LLM在大型组合优化空间中难以做出可靠的单步决策。

Method: Step-TP数据集设计遵循四个原则：(i) token高效、可验证的中间表示(IR)，可确定性降低到TVM TIR；(ii) 原子化、可组合的优化策略，将复杂轨迹分解为可解释的单步决策；(iii) 结构化思维链监督，结合显式的IR到IR状态转换；(iv) 策略过滤机制，平衡覆盖范围的同时防止捷径利用。

Result: 构建了一个在中间程序状态上形成封闭推理循环的数据集，使LLM能够实现可靠的多步优化，而非简单的结果模仿。

Conclusion: Step-TP通过提供可验证的、原子级的步骤级监督和结构化思维链推理，解决了LLM在张量程序优化中单步决策不可靠的问题，为可解释、可组合的LLM-guided优化奠定了数据基础。

Abstract: Despite the strong reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs), optimizing the execution efficiency of tensor programs remains challenging due to the need for precise, composable transformation decisions. Recent LLM-guided approaches frame tensor program optimization as an iterative decision process, but existing datasets provide only end-to-end optimized program pairs using token-inefficient representations, lacking verifiable step-level supervision and interpretability. As a result, LLMs struggle to make reliable single-step decisions in large combinatorial optimization spaces. We introduce Step-TP, a post-training dataset for tensor program optimization that provides grounded, atomic, step-level supervision with structured chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning. Step-TP forms a closed reasoning loop over intermediate program states, enabling reliable multi-step optimization rather than outcome imitation. Its design is guided by four principles: (i) a token-efficient, verifiable intermediate representation (IR) that deterministically lowers to TVM TIR; (ii) atomic and composable optimization strategies that decompose complex trajectories into interpretable single-step decisions; (iii) structured CoT supervision coupled with explicit IR-to-IR state transitions; and (iv) strategy filtering to balance coverage while preventing shortcut exploitation. The dataset and implementation are available at a GitHub link, https://github.com/LIUMENGFAN-gif/StepTP.

</details>


### [147] [Mapping the Schedule x Bit-Width Boundary in Sub-100M Quantisation-Aware Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25966)
*Christian Brandt Thomassen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模实验（720+625次运行）发现，在子1亿参数的解码器语言模型中，量化感知训练（QAT）的最优学习率调度（warmdown fraction=33%）与位宽（FP16/INT8/INT6）无关；INT4在50M以上同样最优为wd33，50M以下则为噪声主导区间。基于实验提出了实用建议：FP16调优的调度可直接用于INT8/INT6 QAT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证量化感知训练（QAT）中，最优学习率调度是否依赖于位宽（bit-width），特别是低精度（INT6/INT4）是否需要不同于高精度训练的调度策略。

Method: 设计两阶段实验：Phase 2进行720次全因子网格实验（位宽×warmdown fraction×LR大小×模型大小×种子），Phase 5进行625次跟进实验（沿优化器、调度形状、训练长度、规模扩展、INT4五个维度探针）。使用对数线性缩放定律拟合INT6惩罚，并进行权重到网格距离的机制分析。

Result: 1）INT6 QAT不需要不同的调度策略（原假设被证伪），最优warmdown在所有位宽和规模下均为33%；2）INT6惩罚遵循对数线性缩放定律，Phase 2拟合可预测Phase 5的5个未见过规模（均在95%预测区间内）；3）INT4在50M及以上wd33明确最优，50M以下属于噪声主导区间；4）权重到网格距离分析排除了快速网格捕捉机制。

Conclusion: 子1亿参数规模下，FP16调优的LR调度可直接用于INT8/INT6 QAT；INT4在50M以上使用wd33，50M以下调度选择在噪声中无显著差异。

Abstract: We test whether the optimal learning-rate schedule depends on bit-width during from-initialisation quantisation-aware training (QAT) for sub-100M decoder language models.
